    function buildImmediateAttackBuffer(kind='rifle') {
      if (!audioCtx) return null;
      const profiles = {
        pistol:  {duration:.014, level:.34, body:1700, low:155},
        deagle:  {duration:.022, level:.52, body:1200, low:92},
        smg:     {duration:.011, level:.29, body:2100, low:175},
        rifle:   {duration:.018, level:.45, body:1450, low:118},
        sniper:  {duration:.027, level:.62, body:980,  low:72},
        shotgun: {duration:.030, level:.68, body:820,  low:64},
        heavy:   {duration:.022, level:.50, body:1080, low:88}
      };
      const p = profiles[kind] || profiles.rifle;
      const length = Math.max(32, Math.ceil(audioCtx.sampleRate * p.duration));
      const buffer = audioCtx.createBuffer(1, length, audioCtx.sampleRate);
      const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      // Sample zero is deliberately non-zero: there is no fade-in and no hidden
      // lead-in. The first render quantum already contains the audible crack.
      let seed = (kind.length * 2654435761) >>> 0;
      const rand = () => {
        seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >>> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
        return ((seed >>> 0) / 4294967295) * 2 - 1;
      };
      for (let i=0;i<length;i++) {
        const t = i / audioCtx.sampleRate;
        const fast = Math.exp(-t * 540);
        const body = Math.exp(-t * 115);
        const click = i === 0 ? 1 : i === 1 ? -.78 : i === 2 ? .52 : 0;
        const highNoise = rand() * fast;
        const ring = Math.sin(Math.PI * 2 * p.body * t) * fast;
        const thump = Math.sin(Math.PI * 2 * p.low * t) * body;
        data[i] = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1,
          p.level * (click * .72 + highNoise * .52 + ring * .28 + thump * .24)
        ));
      }
      return buffer;
    }

    function buildImmediateAttackBuffers() {
      if (!audioCtx || immediateAttackBuffers.size) return;
      for (const key of ['pistol','deagle','smg','rifle','sniper','shotgun','heavy']) {
        immediateAttackBuffers.set(key, buildImmediateAttackBuffer(key));
      }
    }

    function startAudioKeepAlive() {
      if (!audioCtx || audioCtx.state === 'closed' || audioKeepAliveSource) return;
      try {
        // A microscopic continuously-running signal prevents the audio renderer
        // from cooling down between shots. It is around -140 dB and inaudible.
        const buffer = audioCtx.createBuffer(1, 128, audioCtx.sampleRate);
        const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i=0;i<data.length;i++) data[i] = (i & 1 ? -1 : 1) * 1e-7;
        audioKeepAliveGain = audioCtx.createGain();
        audioKeepAliveGain.gain.value = 1;
        audioKeepAliveSource = audioCtx.createBufferSource();
        audioKeepAliveSource.buffer = buffer;
        audioKeepAliveSource.loop = true;
        audioKeepAliveSource.connect(audioKeepAliveGain).connect(audioCtx.destination);
        audioKeepAliveSource.start();
      } catch (error) {
        audioKeepAliveSource = null;
        safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike audio] keep-alive unavailable', error);
      }
    }

    function warmImmediateShotPath() {
      if (!audioCtx || !shotBus || !immediateAttackBuffers.size) return;
      try {
        const source = audioCtx.createBufferSource();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        source.buffer = immediateAttackBuffers.get('rifle');
        gain.gain.value = 0;
        source.connect(gain).connect(shotBus);
        source.start();
        source.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + .002);
        source.onended = () => { try { source.disconnect(); gain.disconnect(); } catch (_) {} };
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    function buildLocalGameplaySfxBuffers() {
      if(!audioCtx||localSfxBuffers.size)return;
      const sr=audioCtx.sampleRate;
      const clamp=v=>Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,v));
      const make=(key,duration,render,seedBase=1)=>{
        const length=Math.max(64,Math.floor(sr*duration));
        const buffer=audioCtx.createBuffer(1,length,sr),data=buffer.getChannelData(0);
        let seed=(0x9e3779b9^(key.length*2654435761)^seedBase)>>>0,low=0,slow=0;
        const rand=()=>{seed^=seed<<13;seed^=seed>>>17;seed^=seed<<5;return((seed>>>0)/4294967295)*2-1;};
        const state={rand:0,low:0,slow:0};
        for(let i=0;i<length;i++){
          const t=i/sr,n=rand();low=low*.82+n*.18;slow=slow*.965+n*.035;
          state.rand=n;state.low=low;state.slow=slow;
          data[i]=clamp(render(t,i,state));
        }
        localSfxBuffers.set(key,buffer);
      };
      const pulse=(t,start,width)=>t>=start&&t<start+width?Math.sin(Math.PI*(t-start)/width):0;
      make('uiClick',.085,(t,i,s)=>(i<2?(i?-.52:.75):0)+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*920*t)*Math.exp(-t*34)*.30+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*55)*.08);
      make('uiHover',.055,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*1450*t)*Math.exp(-t*52)*.18+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*70)*.035);
      make('scopeOn',.12,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,0,.024)*.44+pulse(t,.052,.022)*-.30+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*28)*.035);
      make('scopeOff',.12,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,0,.022)*-.34+pulse(t,.058,.024)*.43+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*28)*.035);
      make('weaponSwitch',.16,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.008,.026)*.42+pulse(t,.075,.032)*-.34+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*620*t)*Math.exp(-t*23)*.12+s.low*Math.exp(-t*18)*.10);
      make('emptyClick',.095,(t,i,s)=>(i<3?[.72,-.58,.35][i]:0)+pulse(t,.044,.018)*-.30+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*62)*.05);
      make('jump',.15,(t,i,s)=>s.low*Math.exp(-t*24)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*115*t)*Math.exp(-t*20)*.15);
      make('land',.20,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*18)*.38+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*72*t)*Math.exp(-t*14)*.30+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*42)*.06);
      make('impactHit',.13,(t,i,s)=>s.low*Math.exp(-t*36)*.36+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*310*t)*Math.exp(-t*31)*.15);
      make('playerHurt',.22,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*18)*.36+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*125*t)*Math.exp(-t*15)*.18);
      make('killConfirm',.24,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(720+900*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*9)*.28+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*1080*t)*Math.exp(-Math.max(0,t-.07)*15)*(t>.07?.20:0));
      make('bodyDrop',.28,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*13)*.32+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*58*t)*Math.exp(-t*12)*.26);
      make('playerDeath',.62,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*7)*.38+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(115-60*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*6)*.30);
      make('meleeSwing',.19,(t,i,s)=>s.rand*Math.sin(Math.PI*Math.min(1,t/.13))*Math.exp(-t*15)*.20+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(220+1300*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*18)*.10);
      make('postPump',.38,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.01,.07)*.30+pulse(t,.17,.09)*-.34+s.low*Math.exp(-t*8)*.18);
      make('postBolt',.34,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.01,.045)*.34+pulse(t,.10,.05)*-.25+pulse(t,.23,.055)*.32+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*12)*.055);

      make('reloadPistolActual',.72,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.03,.08)*-.22+pulse(t,.25,.10)*.27+pulse(t,.52,.07)*-.30+pulse(t,.63,.045)*.34+s.low*Math.exp(-t*4.2)*.10);
      make('reloadRifleActual',1.08,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.04,.10)*-.28+pulse(t,.31,.12)*.25+pulse(t,.64,.11)*.34+pulse(t,.91,.075)*-.27+pulse(t,1.00,.05)*.31+s.low*Math.exp(-t*3.3)*.09);
      make('reloadMagOutActual',.20,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.012,.055)*-.34+pulse(t,.115,.048)*.22+s.low*Math.exp(-t*12)*.11);

      make('buySuccess',.20,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(540+700*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*9)*.22);
      make('buyFail',.22,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(220-65*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*8)*.24);
      make('roundStart',.42,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(320+480*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*4.5)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*640*t)*Math.exp(-t*7)*.10);

      make('bombPlantStart',.20,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.01,.03)*.28+pulse(t,.08,.028)*.24+pulse(t,.15,.025)*.20);
      make('bombPlant',.42,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.01,.04)*.34+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*880*t)*Math.exp(-t*8)*.18+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*440*t)*Math.exp(-t*5)*.14);
      make('bombDefuseStart',.18,(t,i,s)=>s.rand*Math.exp(-t*22)*.08+pulse(t,.02,.035)*.24+pulse(t,.11,.03)*-.18);
      make('bombDefuse',.46,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(620+780*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*5)*.23+pulse(t,.30,.06)*.22);
      make('bombBeep',.105,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*860*t)*Math.exp(-t*28)*.34);
      make('bombBeepFast',.095,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*1120*t)*Math.exp(-t*31)*.38);
      make('bombExplosion',1.25,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*4.2)*.62+s.low*Math.exp(-t*7)*.28+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(74-28*Math.min(1,t))*t)*Math.exp(-t*3.6)*.48);
      make('bulletImpact',.16,(t,i,s)=>(i<3?[.58,-.42,.25][i]:0)+s.low*Math.exp(-t*34)*.29+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*680*t)*Math.exp(-t*32)*.11);
      make('respawn',.34,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(330+760*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*7)*.20+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*24)*.035);
      make('matchStart',.52,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(280+520*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*4.8)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*560*t)*Math.exp(-t*8)*.10);
      make('bombPickup',.20,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.01,.035)*.28+pulse(t,.09,.04)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*520*t)*Math.exp(-t*18)*.08);
      make('bombDrop',.25,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*Math.exp(-t*15)*.30+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*92*t)*Math.exp(-t*13)*.22+pulse(t,.02,.05)*.15);
      make('menuOpen',.15,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(420+620*t)*t)*Math.exp(-t*13)*.17+pulse(t,.02,.025)*.15);

      make('helmetHit',.23,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.002,.026)*.36+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*940*t)*Math.exp(-t*22)*.23+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*38)*.08);

      make('shellEject',.34,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.02,.025)*.30+pulse(t,.16,.022)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*760*t)*Math.exp(-t*12)*.10+s.low*Math.exp(-t*15)*.07);
      make('bombPlantTick',.17,(t,i,s)=>pulse(t,.008,.025)*.29+pulse(t,.082,.023)*.22+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*610*t)*Math.exp(-t*22)*.07);
      make('bombDefuseLoop',.24,(t,i,s)=>s.rand*Math.exp(-t*17)*.06+pulse(t,.015,.030)*.20+pulse(t,.105,.026)*-.17+pulse(t,.182,.022)*.15);
      make('countdownTick',.16,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*820*t)*Math.exp(-t*23)*.28+pulse(t,.006,.020)*.20);
      make('lowHealthBeat',.38,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*64*t)*Math.exp(-Math.max(0,t-.015)*15)*(t<.14?.38:0)+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*58*(t-.18))*Math.exp(-Math.max(0,t-.18)*18)*(t>.18?.25:0));
      make('clothMove',.16,(t,i,s)=>s.low*Math.sin(Math.PI*Math.min(1,t/.13))*Math.exp(-t*12)*.14+s.rand*Math.exp(-t*26)*.035);
      make('roundDraw',.68,(t,i,s)=>Math.sin(2*Math.PI*390*t)*Math.exp(-t*3.5)*.20+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*520*t)*Math.exp(-t*4.4)*.12);
      make('ambientWater',2.0,(t,i,s)=>s.slow*.12+s.low*.06+Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(105+8*Math.sin(t*2.3))*t)*.015,73);
      for(let variant=0;variant<6;variant++)make(`footstep${variant+1}`,.18,(t,i,s)=>{
        const heel=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(78+variant*4)*t)*Math.exp(-t*22)*.20;
        const grit=s.low*Math.exp(-t*25)*(.18+variant*.008);
        const toe=pulse(t,.055+variant*.002,.038)*(.11+variant*.006);
        return heel+grit+toe;
      },variant+17);
    }

    function buildAwpBoomBuffer() {
      if(!audioCtx||awpBoomBuffer)return awpBoomBuffer;
      const sr=audioCtx.sampleRate,duration=1.42,length=Math.max(1,Math.floor(sr*duration));
      const buffer=audioCtx.createBuffer(2,length,sr);
      let seed=0xA7F00D31>>>0,lowL=0,lowR=0;
      const rand=()=>{seed^=seed<<13;seed^=seed>>>17;seed^=seed<<5;return((seed>>>0)/4294967295)*2-1;};
      for(let channel=0;channel<2;channel++){
        const data=buffer.getChannelData(channel);
        for(let i=0;i<length;i++){
          const t=i/sr;
          const n=rand();
          if(channel===0)lowL=lowL*.965+n*.035;else lowR=lowR*.965+n*.035;
          const low=channel===0?lowL:lowR;
          const crack=n*Math.exp(-t*112)*1.10;
          const pressure=low*Math.exp(-t*7.2)*.92;
          const chest=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(118-68*Math.min(1,t/.42))*t)*Math.exp(-t*10.5)*.92;
          const sub=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(53-24*Math.min(1,t/1.1))*t+(channel?.09:0))*Math.exp(-t*3.1)*1.06;
          const body=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(238-118*Math.min(1,t/.34))*t)*Math.exp(-t*15)*.48;
          const reflection=(t>.055?Math.sin(2*Math.PI*86*(t-.055))*Math.exp(-(t-.055)*5.8)*.34:0)
                         +(t>.13?low*Math.exp(-(t-.13)*5.2)*.28:0);
          const raw=crack+pressure+chest+sub+body+reflection;
          data[i]=Math.max(-.985,Math.min(.985,Math.tanh(raw*1.34)*.94));
        }
      }
      awpBoomBuffer=buffer;
      return buffer;
    }

    function initAudioContext() {
      if (audioCtx) return audioCtx;
      const AudioContextCtor = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioContextCtor) return null;
      try {
        audioCtx = new AudioContextCtor({latencyHint:'interactive'});
      } catch (_) {
        try { audioCtx = new AudioContextCtor({latencyHint:.001}); }
        catch (_) { audioCtx = new AudioContextCtor(); }
      }
      // Keep gun attacks on a dedicated, shortest-possible route. Longer tails,
      // reloads, UI tones, and impacts use the normal effects bus.
      shotBus = audioCtx.createGain();
      shotBus.gain.value = .96;
      shotLimiter = audioCtx.createDynamicsCompressor();
      shotLimiter.threshold.value = -2;
      shotLimiter.knee.value = 2;
      shotLimiter.ratio.value = 8;
      shotLimiter.attack.value = .0005;
      shotLimiter.release.value = .11;
      shotBus.connect(shotLimiter).connect(audioCtx.destination);
      sfxBus = audioCtx.createGain();
      sfxBus.gain.value = .67;
      sfxBus.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      buildImmediateAttackBuffers();
      buildAwpBoomBuffer();
      safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike audio] context latency',{
        state:audioCtx.state,
        sampleRate:audioCtx.sampleRate,
        baseLatency:Number.isFinite(audioCtx.baseLatency)?audioCtx.baseLatency:null,
        outputLatency:Number.isFinite(audioCtx.outputLatency)?audioCtx.outputLatency:null
      });

      const length = Math.max(1, Math.floor(audioCtx.sampleRate * .35));
      noiseBuffer = audioCtx.createBuffer(1, length, audioCtx.sampleRate);
      const data = noiseBuffer.getChannelData(0);
      let previous = 0;
      for (let i=0;i<length;i++) {
        const white = Math.random()*2-1;
        previous = previous*.18 + white*.82;
        data[i] = previous;
      }
      buildLocalGameplaySfxBuffers();
      return audioCtx;
    }

    function base64ToArrayBuffer(base64) {
      const binary = atob(base64);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
      for (let i=0;i<binary.length;i++) bytes[i]=binary.charCodeAt(i);
      return bytes.buffer;
    }

    function trimSfxOnset(buffer,key='rifle') {
      if (!buffer || !audioCtx || buffer.length < 128) return buffer;
      const isReload = key.startsWith('reload');
      const channels = buffer.numberOfChannels;
      const sr = buffer.sampleRate;
      // R115: getChannelData(c) exposes the channel PCM through a live Float32Array.
      // Cache one view per channel for this synchronous scan instead of crossing the Web Audio
      // API boundary again inside tens of thousands of sample-scan iterations. The
      // sample values, channel order, thresholds, onset/end math, fades, and output
      // copies remain identical.
      const channelData = new Array(channels);
      for(let c=0;c<channels;c++)channelData[c]=buffer.getChannelData(c);
      const scanFrames = Math.min(buffer.length, Math.floor(sr * (isReload ? 2.5 : .9)));
      let onset = 0;
      let maxPeak = 0, maxIndex = 0;

      if (!isReload) {
        for (let i=0;i<scanFrames;i+=2) {
          let peak = 0;
          for (let c=0;c<channels;c++) peak=Math.max(peak,Math.abs(channelData[c][i]));
          if (peak > maxPeak) { maxPeak = peak; maxIndex = i; }
        }
        if (maxPeak < .001) return buffer;
        const quietThreshold = Math.max(.0012, maxPeak * .018);
        const quietRun = Math.max(12, Math.floor(sr * .0012));
        const maxBack = Math.max(0, maxIndex - Math.floor(sr * .28));
        let run = 0;
        onset = Math.max(0, maxIndex - Math.floor(sr * .001));
        for (let i=maxIndex;i>=maxBack;i--) {
          let samplePeak = 0;
          for (let c=0;c<channels;c++) samplePeak=Math.max(samplePeak,Math.abs(channelData[c][i]));
          if (samplePeak <= quietThreshold) {
            run++;
            if (run >= quietRun) { onset = i + quietRun; break; }
          } else run = 0;
        }
        onset = Math.max(onset, maxIndex - Math.floor(sr * .001));
      } else {
        for (let c=0;c<channels;c++) {
          const data=channelData[c];
          for (let i=0;i<scanFrames;i+=4) {
            const value=Math.abs(data[i]);
            if(value>maxPeak){maxPeak=value;maxIndex=i;}
          }
        }
        if (maxPeak < .001) return buffer;
        const threshold = Math.max(.0025, maxPeak * .025);
        const windowSize = 64;
        outer: for (let start=0;start<scanFrames-windowSize;start+=windowSize) {
          let peak=0;
          for (let c=0;c<channels;c++) {
            const data=channelData[c];
            for (let i=start;i<start+windowSize;i++) peak=Math.max(peak,Math.abs(data[i]));
          }
          if (peak >= threshold) { onset=Math.max(0,start-Math.floor(sr*.004)); break outer; }
        }
      }

      const shotCaps={
        suppressed:.46,pistol:.52,deagle:.64,smg:.44,rifle:.68,rifle556:.62,
        sniper:.92,shotgun:.80,pumpShot:.76,shotgunSemi:.44,heavy:.66
      };
      let end = buffer.length;
      if (!isReload) {
        end = Math.min(end,onset+Math.floor(sr*(shotCaps[key]||.68)));
        // Some source files contain multiple takes. Once the first tail becomes
        // quiet, stop before a second large transient can enter the same shot.
        const searchStart=Math.min(end,onset+Math.floor(sr*.09));
        const window=Math.max(32,Math.floor(sr*.0025));
        const quietLimit=Math.max(.0015,maxPeak*.035);
        const restartLimit=Math.max(.012,maxPeak*.20);
        let quietWindows=0;
        for(let start=searchStart;start+window<end;start+=window){
          let peak=0;
          for(let c=0;c<channels;c++){
            const data=channelData[c];
            for(let i=start;i<start+window;i+=2)peak=Math.max(peak,Math.abs(data[i]));
          }
          if(peak<quietLimit)quietWindows++;else{
            if(quietWindows>=4&&peak>restartLimit){end=Math.max(onset+64,start-Math.floor(sr*.006));break;}
            quietWindows=0;
          }
        }
      }
      const remaining=end-onset;
      if (remaining < 64) return buffer;
      const trimmed=audioCtx.createBuffer(channels,remaining,sr);
      const fadeFrames=isReload?0:Math.min(remaining-1,Math.floor(sr*.012));
      for (let c=0;c<channels;c++) {
        const input=channelData[c].subarray(onset,end);
        const output=trimmed.getChannelData(c);
        output.set(input);
        if(fadeFrames>0){
          for(let i=0;i<fadeFrames;i++)output[remaining-fadeFrames+i]*=(1-i/fadeFrames);
        }
      }
      if(onset>Math.floor(sr*.0015))safeConsoleInfo(`[CatStrike audio] aligned ${(onset/sr*1000).toFixed(1)}ms from ${key}`);
      return trimmed;
    }

    function primeSfxContext() {
      const ctx=initAudioContext();
      if(!ctx)return false;
      const now=performance.now();
      if(ctx.state==='suspended' && now-lastAudioWakeAttempt>25){
        lastAudioWakeAttempt=now;
        ctx.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();warmImmediateShotPath();}).catch(()=>{});
      } else if (ctx.state==='running') startAudioKeepAlive();
      if(!sfxPrimed && shotBus){
        warmImmediateShotPath();
        sfxPrimed=true;
      }
      return ctx.state==='running';
    }

    function preloadEmbeddedSfx() {
      if (embeddedSfxPromise) return embeddedSfxPromise;
      const ctx = initAudioContext();
      if (!ctx) return Promise.resolve(false);
      const embeddedEntries=Object.entries(EMBEDDED_SFX_BASE64);
      embeddedSfxPromise = Promise.all(embeddedEntries.map(async ([key,data]) => {
        try {
          const buffer = await ctx.decodeAudioData(base64ToArrayBuffer(data));
          embeddedSfxBuffers.set(key,trimSfxOnset(buffer,key));
        } catch (error) {
          safeConsoleWarn('Embedded SFX decode failed:',key,error);
        } finally {
          // Decoded AudioBuffers are now authoritative; release each large source
          // string immediately instead of retaining duplicate audio in JS memory.
          delete EMBEDDED_SFX_BASE64[key];
        }
      })).then(()=>{embeddedEntries.length=0;return embeddedSfxBuffers.size>0;});
      return embeddedSfxPromise;
    }

    async function fetchDecodedBuffer(url,key='rifle') {
      const ctx = initAudioContext();
      if (!ctx) throw new Error('Web Audio unavailable');
      const fetchAudio=async()=>{
        const controller=typeof AbortController==='function'?new AbortController():null;
        const timeout=controller?setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),9000):0;
        try{return await fetch(url,{mode:'cors',cache:'force-cache',signal:controller?.signal});}
        finally{if(timeout)clearTimeout(timeout);}
      };
      let response = null;
      // Use CacheStorage when allowed so later launches avoid another network trip.
      if ('caches' in window && isSecureContext) {
        try {
          const cache = await caches.open('catstrike-recorded-sfx-v4');
          response = await cache.match(url);
          if (!response) {
            const fresh = await fetchAudio();
            if (!fresh.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${fresh.status}`);
            response = fresh.clone();
            cache.put(url,fresh.clone()).catch(()=>{});
          }
        } catch (_) {
          response = null;
        }
      }
      if (!response) {
        response = await fetchAudio();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      }
      const decoded=await ctx.decodeAudioData(await response.arrayBuffer());
      return trimSfxOnset(decoded,key);
    }

    function trimActionOnset(buffer,key='uiClick') {
      if (!buffer || !audioCtx || buffer.length < 64) return buffer;
      const channels=buffer.numberOfChannels;
      const maxScan=Math.min(buffer.length,Math.floor(buffer.sampleRate*(key.startsWith('footstep')?.22:1.5)));
      let globalPeak=0;
      for(let c=0;c<channels;c++){
        const data=buffer.getChannelData(c);
        for(let i=0;i<maxScan;i+=4)globalPeak=Math.max(globalPeak,Math.abs(data[i]));
      }
      if(globalPeak<.0005)return buffer;
      const threshold=Math.max(.0012,globalPeak*(key.startsWith('footstep')?.035:.018));
      const runNeeded=Math.max(8,Math.floor(buffer.sampleRate*.001));
      let onset=0,run=0;
      outer:for(let i=0;i<maxScan;i++){
        let peak=0;
        for(let c=0;c<channels;c++)peak=Math.max(peak,Math.abs(buffer.getChannelData(c)[i]));
        if(peak>=threshold){
          onset=Math.max(0,i-Math.floor(buffer.sampleRate*(key.startsWith('footstep')?.006:.003)));
          break outer;
        }
        if(peak<threshold*.35)run++;else run=0;
        if(run>runNeeded)onset=i;
      }
      if(onset<Math.floor(buffer.sampleRate*.0015))return buffer;
      const remaining=buffer.length-onset;
      if(remaining<64)return buffer;
      const trimmed=audioCtx.createBuffer(channels,remaining,buffer.sampleRate);
      for(let c=0;c<channels;c++)trimmed.copyToChannel(buffer.getChannelData(c).subarray(onset),c);
      return trimmed;
    }

    async function fetchDecodedActionBuffer(url,key) {
      const ctx=initAudioContext();
      if(!ctx)throw new Error('Web Audio unavailable');
      let response=null;
      if('caches' in window&&isSecureContext){
        try{
          const cache=await caches.open('catstrike-action-sfx-v2');
          response=await cache.match(url);
          if(!response){
            const controller=new AbortController();const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),6000);
            const fresh=await fetch(url,{mode:'cors',cache:'force-cache',signal:controller.signal}).finally(()=>clearTimeout(timer));
            if(!fresh.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${fresh.status}`);
            response=fresh.clone();cache.put(url,fresh.clone()).catch(()=>{});
          }
        }catch(_){response=null;}
      }
      if(!response){
        const controller=new AbortController();const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),6000);
        response=await fetch(url,{mode:'cors',cache:'force-cache',signal:controller.signal}).finally(()=>clearTimeout(timer));
        if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      }
      const decoded=await ctx.decodeAudioData(await response.arrayBuffer());
      return key.startsWith('reload')?trimSfxOnset(decoded,'reloadRifle'):trimActionOnset(decoded,key);
    }

    function preloadActionSfx() {
      if(actionSfxPromise)return actionSfxPromise;
      initAudioContext();
      actionSfxPromise=Promise.allSettled(Object.entries(ACTION_SFX).map(async([key,config])=>{
        for(const url of config.urls){
          try{
            const buffer=await fetchDecodedActionBuffer(url,key);
            actionSfxBuffers.set(key,buffer);
            return true;
          }catch(error){safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] action SFX fetch failed:',key,error);}
        }
        return false;
      })).then(results=>{
        safeConsoleInfo('[CatStrike audio] recorded action buffers ready',actionSfxBuffers.size,'/',Object.keys(ACTION_SFX).length);
        return results;
      });
      return actionSfxPromise;
    }

    function fallbackActionSlice(key) {
      const rifle=recordedSfxBuffers.get('reloadRifle')||embeddedSfxBuffers.get('reloadRifle');
      const shotgun=recordedSfxBuffers.get('reloadShotgun')||embeddedSfxBuffers.get('reloadShotgun');
      switch(key){
        case 'reloadPistolActual': return rifle?{buffer:rifle,offset:.02,duration:Math.min(1.08,rifle.duration-.02),config:{volume:.82,rateMin:1,rateMax:1}}:null;
        case 'reloadRifleActual': return rifle?{buffer:rifle,offset:0,duration:rifle.duration,config:{volume:.82,rateMin:1,rateMax:1}}:null;
        case 'reloadMagOutActual': return rifle?{buffer:rifle,offset:.03,duration:Math.min(.16,rifle.duration-.03),config:{volume:.70,rateMin:.98,rateMax:1.02}}:null;
        case 'reloadMagInActual': return rifle?{buffer:rifle,offset:.47,duration:Math.min(.16,rifle.duration-.47),config:{volume:.74,rateMin:.98,rateMax:1.02}}:null;
        case 'reloadBoltActual': return rifle?{buffer:rifle,offset:.77,duration:Math.min(.30,rifle.duration-.77),config:{volume:.74,rateMin:.98,rateMax:1.02}}:null;
        case 'reloadShellActual': return shotgun?{buffer:shotgun,offset:.34,duration:Math.min(.15,shotgun.duration-.34),config:{volume:.78,rateMin:.98,rateMax:1.02}}:null;
        case 'reloadShotgunActual': return shotgun?{buffer:shotgun,offset:.47,duration:Math.min(.39,shotgun.duration-.47),config:{volume:.84,rateMin:1,rateMax:1}}:null;
        default:return null;
      }
    }

    function playActionSfx(key,{volumeScale=1,pan=0,rate=null,duration=null,offset=null,delay=0,targetDuration=null,trackReload=false}={}) {
      const ctx=initAudioContext();
      if(!ctx||!sfxBus||ctx.state==='closed')return false;
      const play=()=>{
        let buffer=customSfxBuffers.get(key)||actionSfxBuffers.get(key),config=ACTION_SFX[key]||LOCAL_SFX_CONFIG[key];
        let fallback=null;
        if(!buffer&&localSfxBuffers.has(key)){
          buffer=localSfxBuffers.get(key);config=LOCAL_SFX_CONFIG[key]||config||{volume:.5,rateMin:1,rateMax:1};
        }
        if(!buffer){
          fallback=fallbackActionSlice(key);
          if(fallback){buffer=fallback.buffer;config=fallback.config;if(offset==null)offset=fallback.offset;if(duration==null)duration=fallback.duration;}
        }
        if(!buffer&&(key==='scopeOn'||key==='scopeOff'||key==='uiClick'||key==='uiHover')){
          buffer=embeddedSfxBuffers.get('reloadRifle')||embeddedSfxBuffers.get('reloadShotgun');
          config={volume:key==='uiHover'?.13:.23,rateMin:key==='uiHover'?1.85:1.35,rateMax:key==='uiHover'?2.05:1.55};
          if(offset==null)offset=key==='scopeOff'?.07:0;
          if(duration==null)duration=key==='uiHover'?.055:key.startsWith('scope')?.12:.08;
        }
        if(!buffer||!config){soundEventFailures.set(key,(soundEventFailures.get(key)||0)+1);return false;}
        const source=ctx.createBufferSource(),gain=ctx.createGain();
        source.buffer=buffer;
        const safeOffset=Math.min(Math.max(0,offset??0),Math.max(0,buffer.duration-.002));
        const segmentDuration=Math.min(duration??(buffer.duration-safeOffset),Math.max(.002,buffer.duration-safeOffset));
        let actualRate=rate??(config.rateMin+Math.random()*(config.rateMax-config.rateMin));
        if(targetDuration&&targetDuration>.015)actualRate=segmentDuration/targetDuration;
        source.playbackRate.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(actualRate,.45,2.4);
        gain.gain.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(config.volume*volumeScale,0,1.35);
        let tail=gain;
        if(ctx.createStereoPanner){
          const panner=ctx.createStereoPanner();panner.pan.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pan,-1,1);
          gain.connect(panner);panner.connect(sfxBus);tail=panner;
        }else gain.connect(sfxBus);
        const startAt=ctx.currentTime+Math.max(0,delay);
        source.start(startAt,safeOffset,segmentDuration);
        soundEventCounts.set(key,(soundEventCounts.get(key)||0)+1);
        activeGameplaySfxSources.add(source);
        const outputDuration=segmentDuration/source.playbackRate.value;
        source.stop(startAt+outputDuration+.025);
        if(trackReload)activeReloadAudioSources.add(source);
        source.onended=()=>{
          activeReloadAudioSources.delete(source);activeGameplaySfxSources.delete(source);
          try{source.disconnect();gain.disconnect();if(tail!==gain)tail.disconnect();}catch(_){}
        };
        return source;
      };
      if(ctx.state==='suspended'){
        ctx.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();play();}).catch(()=>{});
        return true;
      }
      return play();
    }

    let playerDeathAudioPathWarmed=false;
    function warmPlayerDeathAudioPath(){
      if(playerDeathAudioPathWarmed)return true;
      if(!audioCtx||audioCtx.state!=='running'||!sfxBus)return false;
      if(!localSfxBuffers.get('playerDeath')&&!actionSfxBuffers.get('playerDeath'))return false;
      // Prime the *exact* runtime playerDeath route, including Map lookups, source/gain/
      // panner creation, active-source bookkeeping and onended cleanup. R178/R179
      // manually recreated only the Web Audio graph, leaving playActionSfx's first-use
      // JS path cold until the player actually died. Keep the prime completely silent
      // and restore audit counters so diagnostics still describe real gameplay only.
      const previousCount=soundEventCounts.get('playerDeath');
      const previousFailure=soundEventFailures.get('playerDeath');
      try{
        const ok=playActionSfx('playerDeath',{volumeScale:0,duration:.012,rate:1});
        if(ok)playerDeathAudioPathWarmed=true;
        return ok;
      }catch(error){safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] death-audio warmup fallback:',error);return false;}
      finally{
        if(previousCount===undefined)soundEventCounts.delete('playerDeath');else soundEventCounts.set('playerDeath',previousCount);
        if(previousFailure===undefined)soundEventFailures.delete('playerDeath');else soundEventFailures.set('playerDeath',previousFailure);
      }
    }

    function startAmbientAudio(){
      const ctx=initAudioContext();if(!ctx||ambientWaterSource||ctx.state==='closed')return;
      const buffer=localSfxBuffers.get('ambientWater');if(!buffer)return;
      const begin=()=>{
        if(ambientWaterSource||ctx.state!=='running')return;
        const source=ctx.createBufferSource(),gain=ctx.createGain();source.buffer=buffer;source.loop=true;gain.gain.value=0;
        source.connect(gain).connect(sfxBus);source.start();ambientWaterSource=source;ambientWaterGain=gain;
        source.onended=()=>{try{source.disconnect();gain.disconnect();}catch(_){}if(ambientWaterSource===source){ambientWaterSource=null;ambientWaterGain=null;}};
      };
      if(ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();begin();}).catch(()=>{});else begin();
    }
    let nextAmbientAudioUpdate=0,lastAmbientAudioTarget=NaN;
    function stopAmbientAudio(){
      if(ambientWaterSource){try{ambientWaterSource.stop();}catch(_){}ambientWaterSource=null;}
      if(ambientWaterGain){try{ambientWaterGain.disconnect();}catch(_){}ambientWaterGain=null;}
      lastAmbientAudioTarget=NaN;nextAmbientAudioUpdate=0;
    }
    function updateAmbientAudio(nowSeconds=performance.now()*.001,force=false){
      if(!ambientWaterGain||!audioCtx)return;
      if(!force&&nowSeconds<nextAmbientAudioUpdate)return;
      nextAmbientAudioUpdate=nowSeconds+.05;
      const inactive=paused||gameOver||!started,px=player.pos.x,pz=player.pos.z;
      const target=inactive||(px===px&&pz===pz&&(Math.abs(px)>=22||Math.abs(pz)>=22))?0:.16*THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1-Math.hypot(px,pz)/22,0,1);
      if(force||!Number.isFinite(lastAmbientAudioTarget)||Math.abs(target-lastAmbientAudioTarget)>.002){
        ambientWaterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(target,audioCtx.currentTime,.16);
        lastAmbientAudioTarget=target;
      }
    }

    let lastUiHoverAt=0,lastUiClickAt=0,lastUiHoverTarget=null,pressedUiTarget=null;
    function playDirectMenuSound(kind){
      const ctx=initAudioContext();if(!ctx)return false;
      const perform=()=>{
        if(!audioCtx||audioCtx.state!=='running'||!sfxBus)return false;
        const now=audioCtx.currentTime;
        const gain=audioCtx.createGain(),osc=audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const isClick=kind==='click';
        osc.type=isClick?'square':'sine';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(isClick?540:1180,now);
        osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(isClick?185:1640,now+(isClick?.095:.055));
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(isClick?.34:.13,now+.001);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+(isClick?.11:.065));
        osc.connect(gain).connect(sfxBus);osc.start(now);osc.stop(now+(isClick?.12:.075));
        if(isClick&&noiseBuffer){
          const source=audioCtx.createBufferSource(),filter=audioCtx.createBiquadFilter(),ng=audioCtx.createGain();
          source.buffer=noiseBuffer;filter.type='bandpass';filter.frequency.value=920;filter.Q.value=.7;
          ng.gain.setValueAtTime(.24,now);ng.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+.075);
          source.connect(filter).connect(ng).connect(sfxBus);source.start(now);source.stop(now+.08);
        }
        return true;
      };
      if(ctx.state==='suspended'){ctx.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();perform();}).catch(()=>{});return true;}
      return perform();
    }
    function installInterfaceSfx(){
      if(interfaceSfxInstalled)return;interfaceSfxInstalled=true;
      const selector='button,[role="button"],summary';
      const fineHoverQuery=matchMedia('(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)');
      const getTarget=event=>{const target=event.target?.closest?.(selector);return target&&!target.disabled&&target.closest?.('#menu,#pause,#result,#buyMenu')?target:null;};
      const clearPress=target=>{if(!target)return;target.classList.remove('menu-press');if(pressedUiTarget===target)pressedUiTarget=null;};
      document.addEventListener('pointerover',event=>{
        if(!fineHoverQuery.matches)return;
        const target=getTarget(event);if(!target||target===lastUiHoverTarget)return;
        const from=event.relatedTarget?.closest?.(selector);if(from===target)return;
        lastUiHoverTarget=target;target.classList.add('menu-hover');
        const now=performance.now();if(now-lastUiHoverAt>42){lastUiHoverAt=now;if(audioCtx?.state==='running')playDirectMenuSound('hover');}
      },{passive:true});
      document.addEventListener('pointerout',event=>{
        const target=event.target?.closest?.(selector),related=event.relatedTarget;
        if(target&&(!related||!(related instanceof Node)||!target.contains(related))){target.classList.remove('menu-hover');if(target===lastUiHoverTarget)lastUiHoverTarget=null;}
      },{passive:true});
      document.addEventListener('focusin',event=>{const target=getTarget(event);if(!target)return;target.classList.add('menu-hover');if(audioCtx?.state==='running')playDirectMenuSound('hover');});
      document.addEventListener('focusout',event=>event.target?.closest?.(selector)?.classList.remove('menu-hover'));
      document.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{
        if(event.pointerType==='mouse'&&event.button!==0)return;
        const target=getTarget(event);if(!target)return;
        clearPress(pressedUiTarget);pressedUiTarget=target;target.classList.add('menu-press');
        const now=performance.now();if(now-lastUiClickAt>28){lastUiClickAt=now;playDirectMenuSound('click');}
      },true);
      document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>clearPress(pressedUiTarget),true);
      document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>clearPress(pressedUiTarget),true);
      document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
        if(event.repeat||(event.key!=='Enter'&&event.key!==' '))return;
        const target=getTarget(event);if(!target)return;target.classList.add('menu-press');playDirectMenuSound('click');
      },true);
      document.addEventListener('keyup',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' ')clearPress(event.target?.closest?.(selector));},true);
      addEventListener('blur',()=>clearPress(pressedUiTarget));
    }

    function preloadRecordedSfx() {
      if (recordedSfxPromise) return recordedSfxPromise;
      initAudioContext();
      recordedSfxPromise = Promise.allSettled(Object.entries(RECORDED_SFX).map(async ([key,config]) => {
        for (const url of config.urls) {
          try {
            const buffer = await fetchDecodedBuffer(url,key);
            recordedSfxBuffers.set(key,buffer);
            matchRecordedSfxBuffers.set(key,buffer);            return;
          } catch (_) {}
        }
      }));
      return recordedSfxPromise;
    }

    function ensureAudio() {
      const ctx = initAudioContext();
      preloadEmbeddedSfx();
      primeSfxContext();
      return !!ctx;
    }

    function tone(freq, duration, type='square', gain=.035, endFreq=null, delay=0) {
      if (!audioCtx || !sfxBus) return;
      const now=audioCtx.currentTime+delay, o=audioCtx.createOscillator(), g=audioCtx.createGain();
      o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(20,freq),now);
      if(endFreq) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20,endFreq),now+duration);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(.0002,gain),now+.001);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+duration);
      o.connect(g).connect(sfxBus); o.start(now); o.stop(now+duration+.015);
    }
    function noiseBurst(duration, gain, frequency, filterType='bandpass', q=.8, delay=0) {
      if (!audioCtx || !sfxBus || !noiseBuffer) return;
      const now=audioCtx.currentTime+delay;
      const source=audioCtx.createBufferSource(), filter=audioCtx.createBiquadFilter(), g=audioCtx.createGain();
      source.buffer=noiseBuffer;
      filter.type=filterType; filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency,now); filter.Q.value=q;
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(.0002,gain),now+.001);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+duration);
      source.connect(filter).connect(g).connect(sfxBus);
      source.start(now); source.stop(now+duration+.02);
    }

    function playAwpBoomSound(volumeScale=1,pan=0,isBot=false){
      if(!audioCtx||!shotBus||audioCtx.state!=='running')return false;
      const buffer=buildAwpBoomBuffer();if(!buffer)return false;
      try{
        const source=audioCtx.createBufferSource(),gain=audioCtx.createGain(),nodes=[source,gain];
        source.buffer=buffer;
        source.playbackRate.value=isBot?.985:1;
        gain.gain.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(volumeScale*(isBot?.48:1.24),0,1.48);
        source.connect(gain);
        if(audioCtx.createStereoPanner){const panner=audioCtx.createStereoPanner();nodes.push(panner);panner.pan.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pan,-1,1);gain.connect(panner);panner.connect(shotBus);}else gain.connect(shotBus);
        const record={source,isBot,startedAt:audioCtx.currentTime,nodes};activeShotSources.add(record);
        debugWeaponSourceStarts++;debugShotStartsByWeapon.set('awp',(debugShotStartsByWeapon.get('awp')||0)+1);debugLastShotDurations.set('awp',buffer.duration/source.playbackRate.value);
        source.start(audioCtx.currentTime);source.stop(audioCtx.currentTime+buffer.duration/source.playbackRate.value+.02);
        source.onended=()=>{activeShotSources.delete(record);for(const node of nodes){try{node.disconnect();}catch(_){}}};
        return true;
      }catch(_){return false;}
    }

    function playImmediateAttack(key,volumeScale=1,pan=0) {
      if (!audioCtx || !shotBus || audioCtx.state !== 'running') return false;
      const family = immediateAttackBuffers.has(key) ? key : 'rifle';
      const buffer = immediateAttackBuffers.get(family);
      if (!buffer) return false;
      try {
        const source = audioCtx.createBufferSource();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        source.buffer = buffer;
        gain.gain.value = Math.max(0,Math.min(1.4,volumeScale));
        source.connect(gain);
        let tail=gain,panner=null;
        if(audioCtx.createStereoPanner){panner=audioCtx.createStereoPanner();panner.pan.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pan,-1,1);gain.connect(panner);panner.connect(shotBus);tail=panner;}
        else gain.connect(shotBus);
        source.onended=()=>{try{source.disconnect();gain.disconnect();if(tail!==gain)tail.disconnect();}catch(_){}};
        source.start();
        return true;
      } catch (_) { return false; }
    }

    const FAMILY_SHOT_PROFILES = {
      suppressed:{source:'suppressed',fallback:'pistol',gain:.78,rate:1,variance:.004,highpass:95,lowpass:4800,maxDuration:.48},

    function weaponSfxKey(weapon) {
      if (!weapon) return 'rifle';
      if (customSfxBuffers.has(weapon.id)) return weapon.id;
      if (weapon.id==='deagle') return 'deagle';
      if (weapon.id==='usp'||weapon.id==='glock'||weapon.id==='p250') return 'pistol';
      if (weapon.sniper) return 'sniper';
      if (weapon.id==='negev') return 'heavy';
      if (weapon.type==='shotgun') return 'shotgun';
      if (weapon.type==='smg') return 'smg';
      return 'rifle';
    }

    function resolveShotProfile(weaponOrKey) {
      if(weaponOrKey&&typeof weaponOrKey==='object')return WEAPON_SHOT_PROFILES[weaponOrKey.id]||FAMILY_SHOT_PROFILES[weaponSfxKey(weaponOrKey)]||FAMILY_SHOT_PROFILES.rifle;
      return FAMILY_SHOT_PROFILES[weaponOrKey]||FAMILY_SHOT_PROFILES.rifle;
    }

    function playWeaponRecording(weaponOrKey,volumeScale=1,pan=0,isBot=false) {
      if(!audioCtx||!shotBus||audioCtx.state==='closed'||audioCtx.state!=='running')return false;
      const weapon=weaponOrKey&&typeof weaponOrKey==='object'?weaponOrKey:null;
      const profile=resolveShotProfile(weaponOrKey);
      const customKey=weapon?.id||((typeof weaponOrKey==='string'&&customSfxBuffers.has(weaponOrKey))?weaponOrKey:null);
      const custom=customKey?customSfxBuffers.get(customKey):null;
      const recorded=recordedSfxBuffers.get(profile.source)||recordedSfxBuffers.get(profile.fallback);
      const embedded=embeddedSfxBuffers.get(profile.fallback)||embeddedSfxBuffers.get('rifle');
      const buffer=custom||recorded||embedded;
      if(!buffer)return false;

      if(isBot&&activeShotSources.size>=MAX_ACTIVE_SHOT_SOURCES){
        // R93: preserve Set insertion order and the exact eviction policy without
        // materializing every live source into a temporary array during audio pressure.
        // Pick the first bot source; if none exists, fall back to the first source.
        let oldest,firstSource;
        for(const record of activeShotSources){
          if(firstSource===undefined)firstSource=record;
          if(record.isBot){oldest=record;break;}
        }
        if(oldest===undefined)oldest=firstSource;
        if(oldest){
          activeShotSources.delete(oldest);
          try{oldest.source.stop();}catch(_){}
          for(const node of oldest.nodes||[]){try{node.disconnect();}catch(_){}}
        }
      }

      const source=audioCtx.createBufferSource(),gain=audioCtx.createGain();
      const nodes=[source,gain];
      source.buffer=buffer;
      source.playbackRate.value=custom?1:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(profile.rate*(1+(Math.random()*2-1)*profile.variance),.72,1.28);
      gain.gain.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((custom ? .88 : profile.gain)*volumeScale,0,1.35);
      let head=source,tail=gain;
      if(!custom&&profile.highpass){const hp=audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();nodes.push(hp);hp.type='highpass';hp.frequency.value=profile.highpass;hp.Q.value=.55;head.connect(hp);head=hp;}
      if(!custom&&profile.lowpass){const lp=audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();nodes.push(lp);lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.value=profile.lowpass;lp.Q.value=.35;head.connect(lp);head=lp;}
      head.connect(gain);
      if(audioCtx.createStereoPanner){const panner=audioCtx.createStereoPanner();nodes.push(panner);panner.pan.value=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pan,-1,1);gain.connect(panner);panner.connect(shotBus);tail=panner;}
      else gain.connect(shotBus);

      let maxSourceDuration=custom?Math.min(buffer.duration,1.25):Math.min(buffer.duration,profile.maxDuration||.68);
      if(weapon?.type==='shotgun'){
        const interval=60/Math.max(1,weapon.rpm||70);
        const overlapRatio=weapon.id==='xm1014'?.82:.94;
        maxSourceDuration=Math.min(maxSourceDuration,Math.max(.22,interval*overlapRatio));
      }else if(weapon?.rpm>=150){
        // A single-shot recording must not leave eight full-length tails stacked
        // during automatic fire. Keep a short natural tail, scaled to fire rate.
        const interval=60/Math.max(1,weapon.rpm);
        maxSourceDuration=Math.min(maxSourceDuration,THREE.MathUtils.clamp(interval*2.25,.14,.50));
      }
      const outputDuration=maxSourceDuration/source.playbackRate.value;
      const weaponId=weapon?.id||String(weaponOrKey||profile.source||'rifle');
      const record={source,isBot,startedAt:audioCtx.currentTime,nodes};
      activeShotSources.add(record);
      debugWeaponSourceStarts++;
      debugShotStartsByWeapon.set(weaponId,(debugShotStartsByWeapon.get(weaponId)||0)+1);
      debugLastShotDurations.set(weaponId,outputDuration);
      source.start(audioCtx.currentTime,0,maxSourceDuration);
      source.stop(audioCtx.currentTime+outputDuration+.025);
      source.onended=()=>{activeShotSources.delete(record);for(const node of nodes){try{node.disconnect();}catch(_){}}};
      return true;
    }

    function syntheticShotFallback(type) {
      if (type==='shotgun') {
        noiseBurst(.19,.42,1050,'lowpass',.7);noiseBurst(.055,.22,3500,'highpass',.45);
        tone(105,.17,'sine',.25,42);tone(160,.07,'sawtooth',.10,72,.008);
      } else if (type==='smg'||type==='pistol') {
        noiseBurst(.062,.20,1800,'bandpass',.75);noiseBurst(.025,.12,4800,'highpass',.4);tone(150,.065,'triangle',.12,68);
      } else {
        noiseBurst(.095,.29,1450,'bandpass',.7);noiseBurst(.032,.15,4300,'highpass',.45);
        tone(125,.10,'triangle',.17,52);tone(410,.035,'square',.045,210,.004);
      }
    }

    function shotSound(weaponOrKey,volumeScale=1,pan=0,isBot=false) {
      const ctx=initAudioContext();
      if(!ctx)return false;
      const play=()=>{
        if(weaponOrKey&&typeof weaponOrKey==='object'&&weaponOrKey.id==='awp'){
          if(playAwpBoomSound(volumeScale,pan,isBot))return true;
        }
        if(playWeaponRecording(weaponOrKey,volumeScale,pan,isBot))return true;
        const family=weaponOrKey&&typeof weaponOrKey==='object'?weaponSfxKey(weaponOrKey):(weaponOrKey||'rifle');
        if(playImmediateAttack(family,volumeScale,pan))return true;
        syntheticShotFallback(family);
        return true;
      };
      if(ctx.state==='suspended'){
        const now=performance.now();lastAudioWakeAttempt=now;
        ctx.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();warmImmediateShotPath();play();}).catch(()=>{});
        return true;
      }
      return play();
    }
    function meowHit() { playActionSfx('impactHit'); }
    let lastPlayerCasingAt=-999,lastHeadshotSfxAt=-999,lastWhizzSfxAt=-999;
    function playCasingMechanic(weapon,volumeScale=1,pan=0,actor=null){
      if(!weapon||weapon.type==='melee')return;
      const now=performance.now()/1000;
      const isBot=!!actor;
      const last=isBot?(actor.lastCasingSfxAt??-999):lastPlayerCasingAt;
      const minimum=isBot?.045:.045;
      if(now-last<minimum)return;
      if(isBot)actor.lastCasingSfxAt=now;else lastPlayerCasingAt=now;
      const isShell=weapon.type==='shotgun'||weapon.id==='awp'||weapon.id==='ssg08';
      let delay=isShell?.16:.065;
      if(weapon.id==='nova'||weapon.id==='mag7')delay=Math.min(.62,Math.max(.25,60/weapon.rpm*.38));
      else if(weapon.id==='xm1014')delay=.085;
      else if(weapon.id==='awp'||weapon.id==='ssg08')delay=Math.min(.66,Math.max(.31,60/weapon.rpm*.30));
      playActionSfx(isShell?'shellEject':'casingDrop',{delay,volumeScale:volumeScale*(isShell?.68:.46),pan,rate:.90+Math.random()*.20});
    }
    function playPostShotMechanic(weapon,volumeScale=1,pan=0){
      if(!weapon||weapon.type==='melee'){if(weapon?.type==='melee')playActionSfx('meleeSwing',{volumeScale,pan});return;}
      if(weapon.id==='nova'||weapon.id==='mag7'){
        const delay=Math.min(.52,Math.max(.22,60/weapon.rpm*.34));
        playActionSfx('reloadShotgunActual',{delay,volumeScale:volumeScale*.84,pan,duration:.34});
      }else if(weapon.id==='xm1014'){
        playActionSfx('semiAutoCycle',{delay:.065,volumeScale:volumeScale*.66,pan,duration:.13});
      }else if(weapon.id==='awp'||weapon.id==='ssg08'){
        const delay=Math.min(.52,Math.max(.24,60/weapon.rpm*.24));
        playActionSfx('reloadBoltActual',{delay,volumeScale:volumeScale*.78,pan,duration:.30});
      }
    }

    function stopReloadAudio(){
      for(const source of activeReloadAudioSources){try{source.stop();}catch(_){}}
      activeReloadAudioSources.clear();
    }
    function scheduleReloadAction(key,delay,targetDuration,volumeScale=1,options={}){
      return playActionSfx(key,{...options,delay:Math.max(0,delay),targetDuration:Math.max(.035,targetDuration),volumeScale,trackReload:true});
    }
    function beginReloadAudio(weapon,totalDuration){
      stopReloadAudio();ensureAudio();
      const duration=Math.max(.55,totalDuration||weapon?.reload||2);
      const id=weapon?.id||'';
      const isPistol=weapon?.category==='Pistols';
      const isTubeShotgun=id==='nova'||id==='xm1014';
      const isMagShotgun=id==='mag7';
      const isBolt=id==='awp'||id==='ssg08';
      const isHeavy=id==='negev';
      const ammo=Math.max(0,Math.min(weapon?.magSize||0,weapon?.ammo||0));
      const wasEmpty=ammo<=0;

      if(isTubeShotgun){
        const shellCount=Math.max(1,(weapon.magSize||6)-ammo);
        const tail=id==='nova'?(wasEmpty?.42:.14):(wasEmpty?.24:.12);
        const first=Math.min(.34,Math.max(.10,duration*.10));
        const last=Math.max(first, duration-tail-.06);
        const spacing=shellCount>1?(last-first)/(shellCount-1):0;
        const shellLength=Math.min(.22,Math.max(.09,spacing*.58||.16));
        for(let i=0;i<shellCount;i++)scheduleReloadAction('reloadShellActual',first+spacing*i,shellLength,.96,{pan:(i%2?-.04:.04),rate:.98+Math.random()*.04});
        if(wasEmpty){
          if(id==='nova')scheduleReloadAction('reloadShotgunActual',Math.max(first+.10,duration-.39),Math.min(.35,duration*.18),1.02);
          else scheduleReloadAction('semiAutoCycle',Math.max(first+.10,duration-.18),Math.min(.14,duration*.08),.84);
        }
        return;
      }

      if(isMagShotgun){
        scheduleReloadAction('reloadMagOutActual',duration*.12,Math.min(.20,duration*.10),.92);
        scheduleReloadAction('reloadMagInActual',duration*.53,Math.min(.24,duration*.11),1.00);
        if(wasEmpty)scheduleReloadAction('reloadShotgunActual',Math.max(.12,duration-.39),Math.min(.34,duration*.16),.98);
        return;
      }

      const fullKey=isPistol?'reloadPistolActual':'reloadRifleActual';
      const fullBuffer=customSfxBuffers.get(fullKey)||actionSfxBuffers.get(fullKey)||localSfxBuffers.get(fullKey)||fallbackActionSlice(fullKey)?.buffer;
      const natural=Math.max(.7,fullBuffer?.duration||1.18);
      const maxStretch=natural/.45;
      const target=Math.min(duration*.92,maxStretch);
      const start=Math.max(.015,duration-target-.055);
      scheduleReloadAction(fullKey,start,target,isHeavy?1.03:1);
      if(isBolt)scheduleReloadAction('reloadBoltActual',Math.max(.08,duration-.36),Math.min(.28,duration*.13),.96);
      else if(isHeavy){
        scheduleReloadAction('reloadMagInActual',duration*.61,Math.min(.24,duration*.08),.90);
        scheduleReloadAction('reloadBoltActual',Math.max(.10,duration-.42),Math.min(.31,duration*.08),1.02);
      }
    }
    function reloadSound(weapon=currentWeapon) { beginReloadAudio(weapon,weapon?.reload||2); }
    function reloadFinishSound(weapon=currentWeapon) {
      if(weapon?.id==='nova'||weapon?.id==='xm1014'||weapon?.id==='mag7'||weapon?.id==='awp'||weapon?.id==='ssg08'||weapon?.id==='negev')return;
      playActionSfx('reloadComplete',{volumeScale:.72});
    }


    const IMPORTABLE_WEAPON_IDS = [
      'usp','glock','p250','deagle','mp9','mac10','ump45','p90','nova','xm1014','mag7',
      'famas','galil','m4a4','m4a1s','ak47','aug','sg553','ssg08','awp','scar20','negev'
    ];
    function importedSfxKey(filename) {
      const name=filename.toLowerCase().replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'');
      const isReload=name.includes('reload')||name.includes('load')||name.includes('mag')||name.includes('clip')||name.includes('shell')||name.includes('pump')||name.includes('cock')||name.includes('bolt')||name.includes('slide')||name.includes('charge');
      if(isReload){
        if(name.includes('shell')||name.includes('singlebullet')||name.includes('roundinsert'))return 'reloadShellActual';
        if(name.includes('magout')||name.includes('clipout')||name.includes('remove'))return 'reloadMagOutActual';
        if(name.includes('magin')||name.includes('clipin')||name.includes('insert'))return 'reloadMagInActual';
        if(name.includes('pump')||name.includes('cock')||name.includes('shotgun'))return 'reloadShotgunActual';
        if(name.includes('bolt')||name.includes('charge'))return 'reloadBoltActual';
        if(name.includes('pistol')||name.includes('handgun')||name.includes('glock')||name.includes('usp')||name.includes('p250')||name.includes('deagle')||name.includes('slide'))return 'reloadPistolActual';
        return 'reloadRifleActual';
      }
      const aliases = {
        m4a1s:['m4a1s','m4a1silenced'],m4a4:['m4a4'],ak47:['ak47'],sg553:['sg553','krieg'],
        ssg08:['ssg08','scout'],scar20:['scar20'],xm1014:['xm1014'],ump45:['ump45'],
        mac10:['mac10'],deagle:['deagle','deserteagle'],glock:['glock18','glock'],
        p250:['p250'],usp:['usps','usp'],mp9:['mp9'],p90:['p90'],nova:['nova'],
        mag7:['mag7'],famas:['famas'],galil:['galilar','galil'],aug:['aug'],awp:['awp'],
        negev:['negev']
      };
      for (const id of IMPORTABLE_WEAPON_IDS) {
        const names=aliases[id]||[id];
        if (names.some(alias=>name.includes(alias))) return id;
      }
      if (name.includes('shotgun')) return 'shotgun';
      if (name.includes('sniper')) return 'sniper';
      if (name.includes('pistol')) return 'pistol';
      if (name.includes('smg')) return 'smg';
      if (name.includes('rifle')) return 'rifle';
      return null;
    }
    async function importCustomSfx(files) {
      ensureAudio();
      const button=$('sfxImportBtn');
      let loaded=0, skipped=0;
      for (const file of files) {
        const key=importedSfxKey(file.name);
        if (!key) { skipped++; continue; }
        try {
          const buffer=await audioCtx.decodeAudioData(await file.arrayBuffer());
          customSfxBuffers.set(key,trimSfxOnset(buffer,key));
          loaded++;
        } catch (_) { skipped++; }
      }
      if (button) button.textContent=loaded ? `CS2 SFX WOADED: ${loaded}` : 'NO MATCHING SFX';
      playActionSfx(loaded?'buySuccess':'buyFail',{volumeScale:.75});
      if (loaded) {
        setTimeout(()=>{if(button)button.textContent='LOAD OWN CS2 SFX';},2600);
      }
      return {loaded,skipped};
    }
    $('sfxImportBtn')?.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      $('sfxImportInput')?.click();
    });
    $('sfxImportInput')?.addEventListener('change',event=>{
      importCustomSfx([...event.target.files]);
      event.target.value='';
    });

    // Create/wake audio only from trusted input. Starting AudioContext during
    // page parsing leaves it suspended under autoplay policy and makes the first
    // shot wait for an asynchronous resume.
    const wakeAudioFromGesture=()=>{
      ensureAudio();
      // Network recordings may load while the menu is open, but they are never
      // decoded or swapped from inside a shot callback.
      if(!started)preloadRecordedSfx();
    };
    addEventListener('pointerdown',wakeAudioFromGesture,{capture:true});
    addEventListener('keydown',wakeAudioFromGesture,{capture:true});
    addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden&&audioCtx?.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume().then(startAudioKeepAlive).catch(()=>{});});

    // ---------- Instant hitscan ballistics ----------
    // Damage resolves in the firing frame. A very short tracer remains purely as feedback.
    const MAX_TRACERS=lowSpec?12:28;
    const tracerPositions=new Float32Array(MAX_TRACERS*6);
    const tracerColors=new Float32Array(MAX_TRACERS*6);
    const tracerGeometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    tracerGeometry.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(tracerPositions,3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    tracerGeometry.setAttribute('color',new THREE.BufferAttribute(tracerColors,3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    tracerGeometry.setDrawRange(0,0);
    const tracerMaterial=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({vertexColors:true,transparent:true,opacity:.9,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,toneMapped:false});
    const tracerLines=new THREE.LineSegments(tracerGeometry,tracerMaterial);tracerLines.frustumCulled=false;tracerLines.updateMatrix();tracerLines.matrixAutoUpdate=false;tracerLines.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;scene.add(tracerLines);
    const hitscanTracers=[];
    const hitscanTracerPool=[];
    // Tracer endpoints never move after creation; only their color fades. Track
    // the first slot whose endpoint data actually changed so static line positions
    // are not rewritten and re-uploaded every frame during the tracer lifetime.
    let tracerPositionDirtyFrom=Infinity;
    const tracerTeamColors={
      cats:new THREE.Color(0xffe4a2),
      meowfia:new THREE.Color(0xff8b78)
    };

    function rayAABBHitDistance(origin,dir,c,maxDist){
      let tMin=0,tMax=maxDist,d,o,a,b,temp;

      d=dir.x;o=origin.x;
      if(Math.abs(d)<1e-8){if(o<c.minX||o>c.maxX)return Infinity;}
      else{a=(c.minX-o)/d;b=(c.maxX-o)/d;if(a>b){temp=a;a=b;b=temp;}if(a>tMin)tMin=a;if(b<tMax)tMax=b;if(tMin>tMax)return Infinity;}

      d=dir.y;o=origin.y;
      if(Math.abs(d)<1e-8){if(o<0||o>c.h)return Infinity;}
      else{a=-o/d;b=(c.h-o)/d;if(a>b){temp=a;a=b;b=temp;}if(a>tMin)tMin=a;if(b<tMax)tMax=b;if(tMin>tMax)return Infinity;}

      d=dir.z;o=origin.z;
      if(Math.abs(d)<1e-8){if(o<c.minZ||o>c.maxZ)return Infinity;}
      else{a=(c.minZ-o)/d;b=(c.maxZ-o)/d;if(a>b){temp=a;a=b;b=temp;}if(a>tMin)tMin=a;if(b<tMax)tMax=b;if(tMin>tMax)return Infinity;}

      if(tMax<=.025)return Infinity;
      return tMin>.025?tMin:tMax;
    }
    function raySphereHitDistance(origin,dir,cx,cy,cz,radius,maxDist){
      const ox=origin.x-cx,oy=origin.y-cy,oz=origin.z-cz;
      const b=ox*dir.x+oy*dir.y+oz*dir.z;
      const c=ox*ox+oy*oy+oz*oz-radius*radius;
      const disc=b*b-c;if(disc<0)return Infinity;
      const root=Math.sqrt(disc);let t=-b-root;if(t<=.025)t=-b+root;
      return t>.025&&t<=maxDist?t:Infinity;
    }
    function addHitscanTracer(origin,end,team){
      const full=hitscanTracers.length>=MAX_TRACERS;
      const dirtySlot=full?0:hitscanTracers.length;
      const tracer=full?hitscanTracers.shift():(hitscanTracerPool.pop()||{});
      tracer.sx=origin.x;tracer.sy=origin.y;tracer.sz=origin.z;
      tracer.ex=end.x;tracer.ey=end.y;tracer.ez=end.z;
      tracer.life=.065;tracer.maxLife=.065;
      const color=team==='cats'?tracerTeamColors.cats:tracerTeamColors.meowfia;
      tracer.r=color.r;tracer.g=color.g;tracer.b=color.b;
      hitscanTracers.push(tracer);
      tracerPositionDirtyFrom=Math.min(tracerPositionDirtyFrom,dirtySlot);
    }
    const hitscanEndScratch=new THREE.Vector3();
    let lastWorldImpactSfxAt=-999;
    function spawnProjectile(origin,dir,team,damage,_speed,owner=null,tracerOrigin=origin,rangeOverride=null){
      const maxRange=rangeOverride||Math.max(150,MAP_SIZE*1.55);
      let nearest=maxRange,hitEntity=null,hitWall=false;
      const wallDistance=nearestColliderHitDistance(origin,dir,nearest);
      if(wallDistance<nearest){nearest=wallDistance;hitEntity=null;hitWall=true;}
      // Team membership is immutable after bot creation. In team modes, only the
      // opposing fixed-team list can ever pass isHostile(), and that filtered list
      // preserves the same relative bot order as the original full bots traversal.
      // R95: FFA can use the same canonical active cache already proven by sensing.
      // configuredActiveBots preserves bots[] encounter order exactly, while reserve
      // slots are excluded only when the old loop would reject them via !bot.active.
      // In FFA every active non-owner bot is hostile by definition, so retain the
      // owner/dead checks while removing reserve traversal and redundant hostility work.
      if(activeMode.freeForAll){
        for(let i=0;i<configuredActiveBots.length;i++){
          const bot=configuredActiveBots[i];
          if(bot===owner||bot.dead)continue;
          const t=raySphereHitDistance(origin,dir,bot.pos.x,1.28,bot.pos.z,.76,nearest);
          if(t<nearest){nearest=t;hitEntity=bot;hitWall=false;}
        }
      }else{
        // Opposite-team membership already proves bot !== owner and isHostile(owner,bot).
        // Bot teams never mutate after creation, so do not re-evaluate those guaranteed
        // predicates for every bullet; active/dead state and exact encounter order remain live.
        const projectileBotTargets=configuredActiveTeamBots[owner.team==='cats'?'meowfia':'cats'];
        for(let i=0;i<projectileBotTargets.length;i++){
          const bot=projectileBotTargets[i];
          if(bot.dead)continue;
          const t=raySphereHitDistance(origin,dir,bot.pos.x,1.28,bot.pos.z,.76,nearest);
          if(t<nearest){nearest=t;hitEntity=bot;hitWall=false;}
        }
      }
      if(owner!==player&&!player.dead&&isHostile(owner,player)){
        const t=raySphereHitDistance(origin,dir,player.pos.x,player.pos.y,player.pos.z,.7,nearest);
        if(t<nearest){nearest=t;hitEntity=player;hitWall=false;}
      }
      hitscanEndScratch.copy(dir).multiplyScalar(nearest).add(origin);
      addHitscanTracer(tracerOrigin,hitscanEndScratch,team);
      const ownerWeapon=owner===player?currentWeapon:(owner?.weaponDef||null);
      const headThreshold=hitEntity===player?player.pos.y+.12:hitEntity?1.63:Infinity;
      const headImpact=Boolean(hitEntity&&hitscanEndScratch.y>=headThreshold&&ownerWeapon?.type!=='melee');
      const impactNow=performance.now()/1000;
      if(headImpact&&impactNow-lastHeadshotSfxAt>.055){
        lastHeadshotSfxAt=impactNow;
        playActionSfx(owner===player?'headshotConfirm':'helmetHit',{volumeScale:owner===player?.92:.78});
      }
      if(hitEntity&&ownerWeapon?.type==='melee')playActionSfx('meleeHit',{volumeScale:owner===player?.86:.55});
      if(owner!==player&&!player.dead&&hitEntity!==player){
        const px=player.pos.x-origin.x,py=player.pos.y-origin.y,pz=player.pos.z-origin.z;
        const along=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(px*dir.x+py*dir.y+pz*dir.z,0,nearest);
        const cx=origin.x+dir.x*along-player.pos.x,cy=origin.y+dir.y*along-player.pos.y,cz=origin.z+dir.z*along-player.pos.z;
        const missDistance=Math.hypot(cx,cy,cz);
        if(missDistance<1.45&&impactNow-lastWhizzSfxAt>.11){
          lastWhizzSfxAt=impactNow;const side=dir.x*Math.cos(player.yaw)-dir.z*Math.sin(player.yaw);
          playActionSfx('bulletWhizz',{volumeScale:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1.25-missDistance*.55,.30,.88),pan:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(side,-.85,.85),rate:.92+Math.random()*.16});
        }
      }
      if(hitEntity===player)damagePlayer(damage,owner,ownerWeapon);
      else if(hitEntity)damageBot(hitEntity,damage,owner,ownerWeapon);
      else if(hitWall){
        impactBurst(hitscanEndScratch.x,hitscanEndScratch.y,hitscanEndScratch.z,team==='cats'?0xffd27b:0xff7768);
        if(owner===player&&impactNow-lastWorldImpactSfxAt>.035){lastWorldImpactSfxAt=impactNow;playActionSfx(ownerWeapon?.type==='melee'?'meleeHit':'bulletImpact',{volumeScale:ownerWeapon?.type==='melee'?.62:.58});}
      }
    }
    let previousTracerDrawCount=0;
    function updateProjectiles(dt){
      let alive=0,positionDirtyFrom=tracerPositionDirtyFrom;
      for(let i=0;i<hitscanTracers.length;i++){
        const tracer=hitscanTracers[i];tracer.life-=dt;
        if(tracer.life<=0){
          hitscanTracerPool.push(tracer);
          positionDirtyFrom=Math.min(positionDirtyFrom,alive);
          continue;
        }
        hitscanTracers[alive]=tracer;
        const idx=alive*6,fade=Math.max(0,tracer.life/tracer.maxLife);
        if(alive>=positionDirtyFrom){
          tracerPositions[idx]=tracer.sx;tracerPositions[idx+1]=tracer.sy;tracerPositions[idx+2]=tracer.sz;
          tracerPositions[idx+3]=tracer.ex;tracerPositions[idx+4]=tracer.ey;tracerPositions[idx+5]=tracer.ez;
        }
        const brightness=.35+.65*fade,r=tracer.r*brightness,g=tracer.g*brightness,b=tracer.b*brightness;
        tracerColors[idx]=r;tracerColors[idx+1]=g;tracerColors[idx+2]=b;
        tracerColors[idx+3]=r;tracerColors[idx+4]=g;tracerColors[idx+5]=b;
        alive++;
      }
      hitscanTracers.length=alive;
      if(alive!==previousTracerDrawCount)tracerGeometry.setDrawRange(0,alive*2);
      if(alive>positionDirtyFrom)tracerGeometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
      if(alive)tracerGeometry.attributes.color.needsUpdate=true;
      tracerPositionDirtyFrom=Infinity;
      previousTracerDrawCount=alive;
    }

    // ---------- Proper 5v5 teams and team spawn formations ----------
    const bots = [];
    const TEAM_COLORS = { cats: 0x69b8ff, meowfia: 0xff7070 };
    const TEAM_SPAWNS = {
      cats: [
        {x:-47,z:-28,yaw:-Math.PI/2}, {x:-47,z:-14,yaw:-Math.PI/2},
        {x:-47,z:0,yaw:-Math.PI/2},   {x:-47,z:14,yaw:-Math.PI/2},
        {x:-47,z:28,yaw:-Math.PI/2}
      ],
      meowfia: [
        {x:47,z:28,yaw:Math.PI/2},  {x:47,z:14,yaw:Math.PI/2},
        {x:47,z:0,yaw:Math.PI/2},   {x:47,z:-14,yaw:Math.PI/2},
        {x:47,z:-28,yaw:Math.PI/2}
      ]
    };
    function addSpawnPads(team) {
      const geometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(.72, .98, 28);
      geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI/2);
      const material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: TEAM_COLORS[team], transparent: true, opacity: .18,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false
      });
      const pads = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geometry, material, TEAM_SPAWNS[team].length);
      const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
      TEAM_SPAWNS[team].forEach((p, i) => {
        dummy.position.set(p.x, .018, p.z); dummy.updateMatrix(); pads.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
      });
      pads.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
      pads.frustumCulled = false;
      pads.updateMatrix();pads.matrixAutoUpdate=false;pads.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
      scene.add(pads);
    }
    addSpawnPads('cats');
    addSpawnPads('meowfia');

    const allyNames = ['Captain Purr','Agent Mew','Tuna Tactical','Mittens','Major Whiskers'];
    const enemyNames = ['Sir Hissalot','Chairman Meow','Pawblo','Cat Damon','Clawdia'];

    // Each bot gets a signature look so you know exactly who just clapped you.
    function addSignature(cat, i) {
      const signature = new THREE.Group();
      const A = (geo, matl, x, y, z, rx=0, ry=0, rz=0) => {
        const m = mesh(geo, matl, false, false);
        m.position.set(x, y, z); m.rotation.set(rx, ry, rz);
        signature.add(m); return m;
      };
      const gold = mat(0xf3c552, .4), blackM = mat(0x17181c), redM = mat(0xc23b3b),
            greenM = mat(0x3f7d47), pinkM = mat(0xf27bb0), whiteM = mat(0xf4f1e8), blueM = mat(0x6fb3d9),
            glassM = mat(0x9fd4e8,.15);
      switch (i % 8) {
        case 0:
          A(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.34,.34,.06,10), blackM, 0,2.62,0);
          A(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.24,.26,.42,10), blackM, 0,2.85,0);
          A(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.12,.02,6,14), gold, .2,2.04,.55);
          break;
        case 1:
          A(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.36,.4,.22,10), redM, 0,2.58,0);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.5,.05,.3), blackM, 0,2.5,.3, .25);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.07,6,4), gold, 0,2.6,.36);
          break;
        case 2:
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.62,.3,.5), redM, 0,1.74,.32, .12);
          A(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.14,.34,4), redM, -.36,1.6,-.1, 0,0,1.2);
          break;
        case 3:
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.62,.16,.1), blackM, 0,2.05,.52);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2,.14,.11), glassM, -.18,2.05,.53);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2,.14,.11), glassM, .18,2.05,.53);
          break;
        case 4:
          A(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.34,.37,.2,10), greenM, 0,2.56,0);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.46,.04,.26), greenM, 0,2.49,.32, .2);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.055,6,4), redM, 0,2.58,.35);
          break;
        case 5:
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.13,6,4), pinkM, -.12,2.62,0);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.13,6,4), pinkM, .12,2.62,0);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.07,6,4), pinkM, 0,2.6,0);
          A(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.07,.018,6,12), gold, .42,2.28,.05);
          break;
        case 6:
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.13,8,6), blackM, -.2,2.05,.5);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.62,.045,.05), blackM, 0,2.16,.42, 0,0,.18);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.16,.1,.1), redM, .34,2.6,0, 0,0,.4);
          break;
        case 7:
          A(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.3,.1,6,12), blueM, 0,1.62,0, 1.35);
          A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2,.44,.1), blueM, .16,1.3,.28, .1);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.18,6,4), whiteM, -.32,.06,.1);
          A(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.18,6,4), whiteM, .32,.06,.1);
          break;
      }
      // Merge pieces that already share the exact same material. Their local
      // transforms are baked into geometry, so the signature looks identical but
      // repeated same-material pieces cost one draw instead of several.
      const signatureMaterialGroups = new Map();
      for (const child of [...signature.children]) {
        if (!child.isMesh || Array.isArray(child.material) || !child.geometry) continue;
        let group = signatureMaterialGroups.get(child.material.uuid);
        if (!group) signatureMaterialGroups.set(child.material.uuid, group = []);
        group.push(child);
      }
      for (const group of signatureMaterialGroups.values()) {
        if (group.length < 2) continue;
        const transformed = group.map(child => {
          child.updateMatrix();
          const geometry = child.geometry.clone();
          geometry.applyMatrix4(child.matrix);
          return geometry;
        });
        let merged = null;
        try { merged = mergeGeometries(transformed, false); } catch (_) { merged = null; }
        for (const geometry of transformed) geometry.dispose();
        if (!merged) continue;
        const combined = mesh(merged, group[0].material, false, false);
        combined.name = 'CatStrike.SignatureBatch';
        for (const child of group) {
          signature.remove(child);
          child.geometry?.dispose();
        }
        signature.add(combined);
      }
      cat.add(signature);
      return signature;
    }

    function makeNameTagCanvas(name, accent) {
      // R121: individual name-tag Sprites were never rendered. Bot creation used to
      // allocate a CanvasTexture + SpriteMaterial + Sprite for every bot, add each
      // Sprite to its root, then immediately copy only the source canvas into the
      // shared atlas below and dispose/remove all ten transient Three.js objects
      // before the first game render. Build exactly the same 512x112 canvas pixels
      // directly and atlas them without creating throwaway GPU-facing resources.
      const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 512; c.height = 112;
      const g = c.getContext('2d');
      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(12,14,18,.78)';
      g.beginPath(); g.roundRect(6, 6, 500, 100, 26); g.fill();
      g.strokeStyle = '#' + accent.toString(16).padStart(6,'0');
      g.lineWidth = 7; g.stroke();
      g.fillStyle = '#f4efe2';
      g.font = '700 56px system-ui, sans-serif';
      g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
      g.fillText(name, 256, 58);
      return c;
    }

    function createBot(index, team, spawnSlot, name) {
      const root = new THREE.Group();
      // R67: bot roots are explicitly composed in updateBatchedBotWeapons() after
      // their position/facing changes. Disable Three.js' automatic second compose
      // during renderer traversal so each moving bot root matrix is built once,
      // while matrixWorld propagation and every child transform remain unchanged.
      root.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
      // User preference: keep only the simpler no-legs character model and drop
      // the legged rigged variant entirely. This also avoids the close-range
      // visual mismatch between the two character forms.
      const rigged = null;
      const cat = createCat({scale:.72,geometry:boykisserLodGeometry,castShadow:false});
      if (ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES) cat.traverse(node => { if (node.isMesh) node.layers.set(MODEL_OUTLINE_LAYER); });
      root.add(cat);
      const catLod = null;
      const signature=!lowSpec?addSignature(root,index):null;
      signature?.traverse(node => node.layers.set(DECORATION_LAYER));
      const nameTagCanvas=makeNameTagCanvas(name, TEAM_COLORS[team]);
      // Team rings are rendered later in two exact instanced batches (one per
      // team), rather than as one separate draw call per bot.
      const teamRing = { visible: true };
      // The visual mesh is emitted by one shared dynamic InstancedMesh after
      // all bots are created. This proxy retains the exact per-bot transform and
      // recoil state used by combat/animation code.
      const weaponType=index%3===0?'shotgun':index%3===1?'smg':'rifle';
      const weapon = new THREE.Object3D();
      weapon.userData.renderType=weaponType;
      const weaponModelTransform=new THREE.Object3D();
      const weaponWidth=weaponType==='shotgun'?2.58:weaponType==='smg'?2.08:2.3;
      const weaponLength=weaponType==='smg'?2.12:2.3;
      weaponModelTransform.position.set(0,-.08,.66);
      weaponModelTransform.rotation.set(-Math.PI/2,0,0);
      weaponModelTransform.scale.set(weaponWidth,weaponLength,weaponWidth);
      weaponModelTransform.updateMatrix();
      weapon.userData.modelMatrix=weaponModelTransform.matrix.clone();
      weapon.scale.setScalar(.34); weapon.position.set(.3,1.35,.42); weapon.rotation.set(-.4,.1,-.55);
      scene.add(root);
      const bot = {
        root,cat,catLod,weapon,nameTag:null,nameTagCanvas,signature,teamRing,name,team,spawnSlot,health:100,dead:true,active:true,respawn:0,invulnerableUntil:0,
        mixer:rigged?.mixer||null,animActions:rigged?.actions||null,currentAnimation:rigged?.currentAnimation||null,animAccumulator:0,_usingRiggedVisual:true,
        kills:0,deaths:0,gunGameLevel:0,gunGameKillsOnLevel:0,weaponDef:null,
        pos:new THREE.Vector3(),speed:4.05+Math.random()*.75,strafe:Math.random()<.5?-1:1,
        nextThink:0,nextSense:0,nextShot:0,preferred:8+Math.random()*8,radius:.48,
        los:false,dist:99,dirX:team==='cats'?1:-1,dirZ:0,moveX:0,moveZ:0,_moveMagnitude:0,fur:furPalette[index%furPalette.length],target:null,
        reactionUntil:0,lastSeenAt:-999,lastSeenX:0,lastSeenZ:0,burstShots:0,burstPauseUntil:0,
        aimBiasX:0,aimBiasY:0,aimBiasRefresh:0,visibleSince:0,
        _objectiveGuardPoint:{x:0,z:0},_batchIndex:index,
        ragdollActive:false,ragdollAge:0,ragdollVelX:0,ragdollVelY:0,ragdollVelZ:0,ragdollTargetX:0,ragdollTargetZ:0,_ragdollWorkIndex:-1,
        _objectiveGuardCos:Math.cos((spawnSlot/5)*Math.PI*2),_objectiveGuardSin:Math.sin((spawnSlot/5)*Math.PI*2)
      };
      bots.push(bot);
      respawnBot(bot, true);
      return bot;
    }

    function isAliveEntity(entity) {
      return entity && !entity.dead;
    }

    function chooseTeamSpawn(team, self, preferredSlot = 0) {
      const points = TEAM_SPAWNS[team];
      let best = points[((preferredSlot % points.length) + points.length) % points.length];
      let bestScore = -Infinity;
      for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
        const p = points[i];
        if (collideCircleXZ(p.x, p.z, .58)) continue;
        let nearestEnemySq = 9999;
        let nearestFriendSq = 9999;
        let occupied = false;

        if (self !== player && isAliveEntity(player)) {
          const dx = player.pos.x-p.x, dz = player.pos.z-p.z, d2 = dx*dx+dz*dz;
          if (player.team === team) nearestFriendSq = Math.min(nearestFriendSq, d2);
          else nearestEnemySq = Math.min(nearestEnemySq, d2);
          if (d2 < 2.25) occupied = true;
        }
        for (const other of bots) {
          if (other === self || !isAliveEntity(other)) continue;
          const dx = other.pos.x-p.x, dz = other.pos.z-p.z, d2 = dx*dx+dz*dz;
          if (other.team === team) nearestFriendSq = Math.min(nearestFriendSq, d2);
          else nearestEnemySq = Math.min(nearestEnemySq, d2);
          if (d2 < 2.25) occupied = true;
        }
        const slotBonus = i === preferredSlot ? 16 : 0;
        const score = nearestEnemySq * 2.2 + Math.min(nearestFriendSq, 100) * .08 + slotBonus - (occupied ? 100000 : 0);
        if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = p; }
      }
      return best;
    }

    // R173: Only corpses that are actually moving need per-frame ragdoll work.
    // The old R172 loop scanned every configured bot every gameplay frame, even
    // when all ten were alive. Keep a compact live-work list instead; settled
    // corpses remain visible in world space but stop consuming simulation time.
    const activeBotRagdolls=[];
    function registerBotRagdoll(bot){
      if(bot._ragdollWorkIndex>=0)return;
      bot._ragdollWorkIndex=activeBotRagdolls.length;activeBotRagdolls.push(bot);
    }
    function unregisterBotRagdoll(bot){
      const index=bot._ragdollWorkIndex;if(!(index>=0))return;
      const last=activeBotRagdolls.pop();
      if(last!==bot){activeBotRagdolls[index]=last;last._ragdollWorkIndex=index;}
      bot._ragdollWorkIndex=-1;
    }

    function ragdollImpulse(entity,attacker,fallbackYaw=0){
      let dx=0,dz=0;
      if(attacker?.pos){dx=entity.pos.x-attacker.pos.x;dz=entity.pos.z-attacker.pos.z;}
      let len=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
      if(len<.001){dx=-Math.sin(fallbackYaw);dz=-Math.cos(fallbackYaw);len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;}
      return {x:dx/len,z:dz/len};
    }
    function startBotRagdoll(bot,attacker){
      const impulse=ragdollImpulse(bot,attacker,bot.root.rotation.y);
