      bot.ragdollActive=true;bot.ragdollAge=0;registerBotRagdoll(bot);
      bot.ragdollVelX=impulse.x*(1.25+Math.random()*.7);
      bot.ragdollVelZ=impulse.z*(1.25+Math.random()*.7);
      bot.ragdollVelY=1.8+Math.random()*.75;
      bot.ragdollTargetX=(Math.random()<.5?-1:1)*(Math.PI*.48+Math.random()*.18);
      bot.ragdollTargetZ=(Math.random()-.5)*.68;
      bot.root.visible=true;bot.cat.visible=true;
      bot.root.position.set(bot.pos.x,.03,bot.pos.z);
      bot.root.rotation.x=0;bot.root.rotation.z=0;bot.root.updateMatrix();
    }
    function stopBotRagdoll(bot,hide=false){
      unregisterBotRagdoll(bot);
      bot.ragdollActive=false;bot.ragdollAge=0;bot.ragdollVelX=bot.ragdollVelY=bot.ragdollVelZ=0;
      bot.root.rotation.x=0;bot.root.rotation.z=0;bot.root.position.y=0;
      bot.cat.position.y=0;
      if(hide)bot.root.visible=false;
      bot.root.updateMatrix();
    }
    function startPlayerRagdoll(attacker){
      const impulse=ragdollImpulse(player,attacker,player.yaw);
      playerRagdollState.active=true;playerRagdollState.age=0;
      playerRagdollState.velX=impulse.x*(1.35+Math.random()*.65);
      playerRagdollState.velZ=impulse.z*(1.35+Math.random()*.65);
      playerRagdollState.velY=1.9+Math.random()*.7;
      playerRagdollState.targetX=(Math.random()<.5?-1:1)*(Math.PI*.48+Math.random()*.16);
      playerRagdollState.targetZ=(Math.random()-.5)*.62;
      playerRagdollState.deathYaw=player.yaw;
      playerRagdoll.position.set(player.pos.x,.03,player.pos.z);
      playerRagdoll.rotation.set(0,player.yaw,0);playerRagdoll.visible=true;playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
      gunPivot.visible=false;lastCameraPitch=NaN;lastCameraYaw=NaN;
    }
    function stopPlayerRagdoll(){
      playerRagdollState.active=false;playerRagdollState.age=0;playerRagdoll.visible=false;
      playerRagdoll.position.set(player.pos.x,0,player.pos.z);playerRagdoll.rotation.set(0,player.yaw,0);playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
      gunPivot.visible=true;lastCameraPitch=NaN;lastCameraYaw=NaN;
    }
    function updateSingleRagdoll(root,state,pos,dt,radius){
      if(!state.active)return;
      state.age+=dt;
      state.velY-=9.8*dt;
      moveWithCollision(pos,state.velX*dt,state.velZ*dt,radius);
      let y=root.position.y+state.velY*dt;
      if(y<=.24){
        y=.24;if(state.velY<0)state.velY=0;
        const drag=Math.exp(-4.8*dt);state.velX*=drag;state.velZ*=drag;
        if(Math.abs(state.velX)<.025)state.velX=0;if(Math.abs(state.velZ)<.025)state.velZ=0;
      }
      root.position.set(pos.x,y,pos.z);
      const settle=1-Math.exp(-(state.age<.18?5.5:8.5)*dt);
      root.rotation.x=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(root.rotation.x,state.targetX,settle);
      root.rotation.z=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(root.rotation.z,state.targetZ,settle);
      root.updateMatrix();
    }
    const deathCamTarget=new THREE.Vector3();
    const deathCamDesired=new THREE.Vector3();
    function updateRagdolls(dt){
      for(let i=activeBotRagdolls.length-1;i>=0;i--){
        const bot=activeBotRagdolls[i];
        // A respawn/reset can unregister a corpse outside this loop. The compact
        // list normally guarantees both predicates, but keep this defensive guard
        // so a future synchronous state change cannot simulate a live character.
        if(!bot.dead||!bot.ragdollActive){unregisterBotRagdoll(bot);continue;}
        bot.ragdollAge+=dt;bot.ragdollVelY-=9.8*dt;
        moveWithCollision(bot.pos,bot.ragdollVelX*dt,bot.ragdollVelZ*dt,.42);
        let y=bot.root.position.y+bot.ragdollVelY*dt;
        if(y<=.24){
          y=.24;if(bot.ragdollVelY<0)bot.ragdollVelY=0;
          const drag=Math.exp(-4.8*dt);bot.ragdollVelX*=drag;bot.ragdollVelZ*=drag;
          if(Math.abs(bot.ragdollVelX)<.025)bot.ragdollVelX=0;if(Math.abs(bot.ragdollVelZ)<.025)bot.ragdollVelZ=0;
        }
        bot.root.position.set(bot.pos.x,y,bot.pos.z);
        const settle=1-Math.exp(-(bot.ragdollAge<.18?5.5:8.5)*dt);
        bot.root.rotation.x=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(bot.root.rotation.x,bot.ragdollTargetX,settle);
        bot.root.rotation.z=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(bot.root.rotation.z,bot.ragdollTargetZ,settle);
        bot.root.updateMatrix();
        // Once the body is grounded, translationally still, and within 0.00015 rad
        // (~0.009 degrees) of its final pose, further exponential convergence is
        // sub-pixel at normal camera distances. Keep the corpse visible/ragdollActive
        // for gameplay/debug semantics, but remove it from the per-frame work list.
        if(y===.24&&bot.ragdollVelY===0&&bot.ragdollVelX===0&&bot.ragdollVelZ===0&&
           Math.abs(bot.root.rotation.x-bot.ragdollTargetX)<.00015&&Math.abs(bot.root.rotation.z-bot.ragdollTargetZ)<.00015){
          unregisterBotRagdoll(bot);
        }
      }
      if(player.dead&&playerRagdollState.active){
        updateSingleRagdoll(playerRagdoll,playerRagdollState,player.pos,dt,.42);
        const yaw=playerRagdollState.deathYaw;
        deathCamTarget.set(playerRagdoll.position.x,.78,playerRagdoll.position.z);
        deathCamDesired.set(playerRagdoll.position.x+Math.sin(yaw)*3.4,2.65,playerRagdoll.position.z+Math.cos(yaw)*3.4);
        camera.position.lerp(deathCamDesired,1-Math.exp(-6.5*dt));camera.lookAt(deathCamTarget);
        lastCameraPitch=NaN;lastCameraYaw=NaN;
      }
    }

    function respawnBot(bot,initial=false){
      if(!bot.active){bot.dead=true;stopBotRagdoll(bot,true);bot.target=null;return;}
      stopBotRagdoll(bot,false);
      const p=chooseTeamSpawn(bot.team,bot,bot.spawnSlot);
      bot.pos.set(p.x,0,p.z);bot.root.position.set(p.x,0,p.z);bot.root.rotation.set(0,p.yaw,0);bot.root.updateMatrix();bot._facingDirty=true;
      bot.health=100;bot.dead=false;bot.target=null;bot.los=false;bot.lastSeenAt=-999;
      bot.reactionUntil=0;bot.burstShots=0;bot.burstPauseUntil=0;bot.aimBiasRefresh=0;bot.visibleSince=0;
      bot.weaponDef=getBotWeapon(bot);
      bot.invulnerableUntil=performance.now()/1000+(activeMode.roundBased?.35:1.1);
      bot.root.visible=true;bot.nextShot=performance.now()/1000+.65+Math.random()*.45;
      bot.cat.position.y=0;bot.cat.visible=true;bot._usingRiggedVisual=true;
      if(!initial&&activeMode.respawn)addKillFeed(`<span style="color:${bot.team==='cats'?'#76bdff':'#ff8a8a'}">${bot.name}</span> respawned.`);
    }

    // Keep five reserve bots per fixed team. Exactly one bot on the player's
    // chosen side is disabled, preserving the original participant counts.
    for (let i=0;i<5;i++) createBot(i, 'cats', i, allyNames[i]);
    for (let i=0;i<5;i++) createBot(i+5, 'meowfia', i, enemyNames[i]);

    // R86: cache configured bot membership immediately after creation. All bots
    // begin active, matching the original pre-mode-configuration state exactly;
    // configureBotsForMode() later rebuilds these lists whenever membership changes.
    const configuredActiveBots=[...bots];
    const configuredActiveTeamBots={
      cats:bots.filter(bot=>bot.team==='cats'),
      meowfia:bots.filter(bot=>bot.team==='meowfia')
    };

    // Bot-held weapon cats all use the same LOD geometry/material. One dynamic
    // instance batch preserves every individual rifle/SMG/shotgun transform and
    // recoil while replacing nine scene meshes and nine outline-depth meshes.
    const batchedBotWeapons=new THREE.InstancedMesh(boykisserLodGeometry,catSurfaceMaterial,bots.length);
    batchedBotWeapons.name='CatStrike.BotWeaponCats';
    batchedBotWeapons.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
    batchedBotWeapons.layers.set(MODEL_OUTLINE_DYNAMIC_OCCLUDER_LAYER);
    batchedBotWeapons.frustumCulled=false;
    batchedBotWeapons.updateMatrix();batchedBotWeapons.matrixAutoUpdate=false;batchedBotWeapons.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
    scene.add(batchedBotWeapons);
    const botWeaponCombinedMatrix=new THREE.Matrix4();
    const botWeaponRootMatrix=new THREE.Matrix4();
    // 255 = unknown, 0 = already hidden with the exact zero-scale matrix,
    // 1 = visible. Dead/inactive bots can remain hidden for hundreds of frames;
    // do not rebuild and rewrite the identical zero matrix until visibility changes.
    const botWeaponBatchVisibility=new Uint8Array(bots.length);botWeaponBatchVisibility.fill(255);
    // R63: the rendered bot-weapon matrix depends only on the bot root X/Z/yaw
    // plus weapon recoil X rotation; all other weapon/root transform components and
    // the model matrix are immutable after creation. Cache those exact inputs so
    // stationary settled bots do not compose/multiply/upload an identical matrix.
    const botWeaponBatchX=new Float64Array(bots.length),botWeaponBatchZ=new Float64Array(bots.length),
          botWeaponBatchYaw=new Float64Array(bots.length),botWeaponBatchRecoilX=new Float64Array(bots.length),
          botWeaponLocalMatrixRecoilX=new Float64Array(bots.length);
    botWeaponBatchX.fill(NaN);botWeaponBatchZ.fill(NaN);botWeaponBatchYaw.fill(NaN);botWeaponBatchRecoilX.fill(NaN);botWeaponLocalMatrixRecoilX.fill(NaN);
    function updateBatchedBotWeapons(){
      let changed=false;
      // R83: active membership is canonicalized by configureBotsForMode(). Inactive
      // weapon instances are zeroed at that exact membership-change point, so the
      // per-frame batch updater only needs to visit configured bots. Active dead
      // bots remain in this list and are still hidden live on the exact death frame.
      for(let activeIndex=0;activeIndex<configuredActiveBots.length;activeIndex++){
        const bot=configuredActiveBots[activeIndex],i=bot._batchIndex,visible=!bot.dead;
        if(!visible){
          if(botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]===0)continue;
          botWeaponCombinedMatrix.makeScale(0,0,0);
          batchedBotWeapons.setMatrixAt(i,botWeaponCombinedMatrix);
          botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]=0;changed=true;
          continue;
        }
        const x=bot.root.position.x,z=bot.root.position.z,yaw=bot.root.rotation.y,recoilX=bot.weapon.rotation.x;
        const rootTranslationChanged=!Object.is(botWeaponBatchX[i],x)||!Object.is(botWeaponBatchZ[i],z);
        const rootYawChanged=!Object.is(botWeaponBatchYaw[i],yaw);
        const rootMatrixChanged=rootTranslationChanged||rootYawChanged;
        const recoilMatrixChanged=!Object.is(botWeaponBatchRecoilX[i],recoilX);
        if(botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]===1&&!rootMatrixChanged&&!recoilMatrixChanged)continue;
        botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]=1;botWeaponBatchX[i]=x;botWeaponBatchZ[i]=z;botWeaponBatchYaw[i]=yaw;botWeaponBatchRecoilX[i]=recoilX;
        // R118: bot roots have matrixAutoUpdate=false and a permanently unit scale.
        // Between 30 Hz steering ticks, movement changes only X/Z translation; the
        // quaternion/rotation basis is bit-identical. Matrix4.compose() would rewrite
        // the same 12 basis/affine values on every render frame, so update only the
        // two translation elements in that common case. updateMatrix() is retained
        // whenever yaw changes, and the direct path explicitly mirrors its
        // matrixWorldNeedsUpdate side effect so every child/world transform remains
        // on the exact same renderer update path.
        if(rootYawChanged)bot.root.updateMatrix();
        else if(rootTranslationChanged){const rootElements=bot.root.matrix.elements;rootElements[12]=x;rootElements[14]=z;bot.root.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate=true;}
        // R68: bot motion changes the combined instance matrix every live frame, but
        // the weapon's local matrix changes only when its recoil X changes. Avoid
        // recomposing that identical local Object3D matrix merely because the bot
        // translated; changed recoil still executes Three.js' original updateMatrix().
        if(!Object.is(botWeaponLocalMatrixRecoilX[i],recoilX)){
          bot.weapon.updateMatrix();botWeaponLocalMatrixRecoilX[i]=recoilX;
        }
        botWeaponRootMatrix.multiplyMatrices(bot.root.matrix,bot.weapon.matrix);
        botWeaponCombinedMatrix.multiplyMatrices(botWeaponRootMatrix,bot.weapon.userData.modelMatrix);
        batchedBotWeapons.setMatrixAt(i,botWeaponCombinedMatrix);changed=true;
      }
      if(changed)batchedBotWeapons.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    // Dynamic team rings: the same RingGeometry and materials as before, but two
    // instanced draws replace nine separate meshes. Hidden/dead rings receive a
    // zero-scale matrix so gameplay visibility remains exact.
    const batchedTeamRingGeometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(.47,.58,20);
    batchedTeamRingGeometry.rotateX(-Math.PI/2);
    const batchedTeamRings = {};
    for (const team of ['cats','meowfia']) {
      const count = bots.reduce((n,bot)=>n+(bot.team===team?1:0),0);
      const material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:TEAM_COLORS[team],transparent:true,opacity:.78,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false});
      const rings = new THREE.InstancedMesh(batchedTeamRingGeometry,material,count);
      rings.name = `CatStrike.${team}.TeamRings`;
      rings.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      rings.layers.set(DECORATION_LAYER);
      rings.frustumCulled=false;
      rings.updateMatrix();rings.matrixAutoUpdate=false;rings.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
      scene.add(rings);
      batchedTeamRings[team]=rings;
    }
    const teamRingBots = {
      cats: bots.filter(bot=>bot.team==='cats'),
      meowfia: bots.filter(bot=>bot.team==='meowfia')
    };
    // R85: preserve each bot's immutable per-team instance slot so the live ring
    // updater can traverse only configured-active bots without changing which
    // InstancedMesh matrix belongs to which character.
    for(const team of ['cats','meowfia'])for(let i=0;i<teamRingBots[team].length;i++)teamRingBots[team][i]._teamRingBatchIndex=i;
    // Rings only translate and toggle between exact scales 0/1. Cache those three
    // inputs so stationary/dead bots do not rewrite an identical instance matrix.
    const teamRingMatrixState = {
      cats: {x:new Float64Array(teamRingBots.cats.length),z:new Float64Array(teamRingBots.cats.length),scale:new Int8Array(teamRingBots.cats.length)},
      meowfia: {x:new Float64Array(teamRingBots.meowfia.length),z:new Float64Array(teamRingBots.meowfia.length),scale:new Int8Array(teamRingBots.meowfia.length)}
    };
    teamRingMatrixState.cats.scale.fill(-1);teamRingMatrixState.meowfia.scale.fill(-1);
    function updateTeamRingBatch(rings,activeTeamBots,state){
      let changed=false;
      // R85: inactive reserve-ring matrices are zeroed exactly when membership is
      // configured. Per-frame work therefore only visits configured-active bots;
      // dead/root/tag visibility remains live and identical on every frame.
      for(let activeIndex=0;activeIndex<activeTeamBots.length;activeIndex++){
        const bot=activeTeamBots[activeIndex],index=bot._teamRingBatchIndex;
        const scale=!bot.dead&&bot.teamRing.visible?1:0,x=bot.pos.x,z=bot.pos.z;
        // R108: once an instance is already zero-scale, movement cannot produce any
        // fragments because every ring triangle remains degenerate. Keep the CPU-side
        // cached X/Z current, but do not rewrite/upload an equivalent invisible matrix
        // until scale becomes 1 again; that transition always writes the live X/Z.
        if(state.scale[index]===scale){
          if(scale===0){state.x[index]=x;state.z[index]=z;continue;}
          if(state.x[index]===x&&state.z[index]===z)continue;
        }
        state.scale[index]=scale;state.x[index]=x;state.z[index]=z;
        // R101: this matrix is always pure uniform scale + translation. Writing the
        // exact column-major Matrix4 values directly into the InstancedMesh buffer
        // avoids makeScale(), setPosition(), Matrix4.toArray(), and their redundant
        // writes while producing the same 16 numeric values for setMatrixAt().
        const matrixOffset=index*16,matrixArray=rings.instanceMatrix.array;
        matrixArray[matrixOffset]=scale;matrixArray[matrixOffset+1]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+2]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+3]=0;
        matrixArray[matrixOffset+4]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+5]=scale;matrixArray[matrixOffset+6]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+7]=0;
        matrixArray[matrixOffset+8]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+9]=0;matrixArray[matrixOffset+10]=scale;matrixArray[matrixOffset+11]=0;
        matrixArray[matrixOffset+12]=x;matrixArray[matrixOffset+13]=.025;matrixArray[matrixOffset+14]=z;matrixArray[matrixOffset+15]=1;changed=true;
      }
      if(changed)rings.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    function updateBatchedTeamRings(){
      updateTeamRingBatch(batchedTeamRings.cats,configuredActiveTeamBots.cats,teamRingMatrixState.cats);
      updateTeamRingBatch(batchedTeamRings.meowfia,configuredActiveTeamBots.meowfia,teamRingMatrixState.meowfia);
    }

    // Nameplates keep their exact original canvases, typography, borders,
    // teammate-through-wall rule, enemy depth testing, and world-space size.
    // Two dynamic plane batches replace nine independent Sprite draw calls.
    const nameTagAtlasCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    nameTagAtlasCanvas.width=512;nameTagAtlasCanvas.height=112*bots.length;
    const nameTagAtlasContext=nameTagAtlasCanvas.getContext('2d');
    for(let i=0;i<bots.length;i++){
      nameTagAtlasContext.drawImage(bots[i].nameTagCanvas,0,i*112);
      bots[i]._nameTagAtlasIndex=i;
      bots[i]._nameTagVisible=true;
      bots[i]._nameTagScale=1;
      bots[i]._nameTagTeammate=false;
      bots[i].nameTagCanvas=null;
    }
    const nameTagAtlasTexture=configureMapTexture(new THREE.CanvasTexture(nameTagAtlasCanvas));
    const NAME_TAG_VERTEX_CAPACITY=bots.length*6;
    function createNameTagBatch(depthTest,opacity,renderOrder,name){
      const geometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      geometry.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(NAME_TAG_VERTEX_CAPACITY*3),3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
      const uvs=new Float32Array(NAME_TAG_VERTEX_CAPACITY*2);
      for(let slot=0;slot<bots.length;slot++){
        const v0=slot/bots.length,v1=(slot+1)/bots.length,offset=slot*12;
        uvs.set([0,v0,1,v0,1,v1,0,v0,1,v1,0,v1],offset);
      }
      geometry.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.BufferAttribute(uvs,2));
      geometry.setDrawRange(0,NAME_TAG_VERTEX_CAPACITY);
      const material=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:nameTagAtlasTexture,transparent:true,opacity,depthTest,depthWrite:false,toneMapped:false,fog:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
      const batch=new THREE.Mesh(geometry,material);
      batch.name=name;batch.renderOrder=renderOrder;batch.frustumCulled=false;batch.layers.set(DECORATION_LAYER);
      batch.updateMatrix();batch.matrixAutoUpdate=false;batch.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;scene.add(batch);
      return batch;
    }
    const teammateNameTagBatch=createNameTagBatch(false,1,1000,'CatStrike.TeammateNameTags');
    const enemyNameTagBatch=createNameTagBatch(true,.92,6,'CatStrike.EnemyNameTags');
    // R62: billboard geometry only depends on camera right/up plus each tag's
    // exact center/scale/batch. Track those inputs so stationary tags under an
    // unchanged view do not rewrite 18 floats or upload an identical buffer.
    let nameTagCameraRevision=0;
    let nameTagLastRightX=NaN,nameTagLastRightY=NaN,nameTagLastRightZ=NaN;
    let nameTagLastUpX=NaN,nameTagLastUpY=NaN,nameTagLastUpZ=NaN;
    function updateBatchedNameTags(nowSeconds=performance.now()*.001){
      // The outline visibility, offscreen animation gate, and billboards all use
      // the same camera matrices. Refresh them once per animation timestamp.
      refreshExactOutlineCameraFrame(nowSeconds);
      // R92: Vector3.setFromMatrixColumn() is only a three-property copy from
      // matrixWorld.elements. Read those six scalar values directly because this
      // hot path immediately consumed them as scalars again. This preserves the
      // exact IEEE-754 inputs while removing two method calls and two scratch objects.
      const cameraElements=camera.matrixWorld.elements;
      const rightX=cameraElements[0],rightY=cameraElements[1],rightZ=cameraElements[2];
      const upX=cameraElements[4],upY=cameraElements[5],upZ=cameraElements[6];
      if(!Object.is(rightX,nameTagLastRightX)||!Object.is(rightY,nameTagLastRightY)||!Object.is(rightZ,nameTagLastRightZ)||
         !Object.is(upX,nameTagLastUpX)||!Object.is(upY,nameTagLastUpY)||!Object.is(upZ,nameTagLastUpZ)){
        nameTagLastRightX=rightX;nameTagLastRightY=rightY;nameTagLastRightZ=rightZ;
        nameTagLastUpX=upX;nameTagLastUpY=upY;nameTagLastUpZ=upZ;
        nameTagCameraRevision++;
      }
      const teammatePositions=teammateNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.array;
      const enemyPositions=enemyNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.array;
      let teammateVisibleCount=0,enemyVisibleCount=0,teammateDirty=false,enemyDirty=false;
      // R86: inactive name-tag vertices are cleared exactly when configured
      // membership changes. The live per-frame billboard pass therefore visits only
      // configured-active bots while preserving each bot's immutable atlas/buffer slot.
      for(let activeIndex=0;activeIndex<configuredActiveBots.length;activeIndex++){
        const bot=configuredActiveBots[activeIndex],slot=bot._nameTagAtlasIndex,base=slot*18;
        const visible=bot._nameTagVisible&&!bot.dead; // Corpse roots may stay visible; dead bodies never retain nametags.
        const teammateVisible=visible&&bot._nameTagTeammate;
        const enemyVisible=visible&&!bot._nameTagTeammate;
        if(!teammateVisible){
          if(bot._teammateNameTagDrawn){teammatePositions.fill(0,base,base+18);bot._teammateNameTagDrawn=false;teammateDirty=true;bot._nameTagBillboardBatch=0;}
        }else{
          teammateVisibleCount++;bot._teammateNameTagDrawn=true;
        }
        if(!enemyVisible){
          if(bot._enemyNameTagDrawn){enemyPositions.fill(0,base,base+18);bot._enemyNameTagDrawn=false;enemyDirty=true;bot._nameTagBillboardBatch=0;}
        }else{
          enemyVisibleCount++;bot._enemyNameTagDrawn=true;
        }
        if(!teammateVisible&&!enemyVisible)continue;
        const positions=teammateVisible?teammatePositions:enemyPositions;
        const scale=bot._nameTagScale||1,batch=teammateVisible?1:2,cx=bot.pos.x,cz=bot.pos.z;
        if(bot._nameTagBillboardBatch===batch&&bot._nameTagBillboardRevision===nameTagCameraRevision&&Object.is(bot._nameTagBillboardX,cx)&&Object.is(bot._nameTagBillboardZ,cz)&&Object.is(bot._nameTagBillboardScale,scale))continue;
        if(teammateVisible)teammateDirty=true;else enemyDirty=true;
        bot._nameTagBillboardBatch=batch;bot._nameTagBillboardRevision=nameTagCameraRevision;bot._nameTagBillboardX=cx;bot._nameTagBillboardZ=cz;bot._nameTagBillboardScale=scale;
        const halfW=.95*scale,halfH=.21*scale;
        // Same operation order as the former Vector3 copy/addScaledVector chain,
        // written directly into the dynamic vertex buffer to avoid 16 reusable
        // Vector3 method calls per visible tag on every rendered gameplay frame.
        const cy=2.35;
        const x0=(cx+rightX*-halfW)+upX*-halfH;
        const y0=(cy+rightY*-halfW)+upY*-halfH;
        const z0=(cz+rightZ*-halfW)+upZ*-halfH;
        const x1=(cx+rightX* halfW)+upX*-halfH;
        const y1=(cy+rightY* halfW)+upY*-halfH;
        const z1=(cz+rightZ* halfW)+upZ*-halfH;
        const x2=(cx+rightX* halfW)+upX* halfH;
        const y2=(cy+rightY* halfW)+upY* halfH;
        const z2=(cz+rightZ* halfW)+upZ* halfH;
        const x3=(cx+rightX*-halfW)+upX* halfH;
        const y3=(cy+rightY*-halfW)+upY* halfH;
        const z3=(cz+rightZ*-halfW)+upZ* halfH;
        positions[base]=x0;positions[base+1]=y0;positions[base+2]=z0;
        positions[base+3]=x1;positions[base+4]=y1;positions[base+5]=z1;
        positions[base+6]=x2;positions[base+7]=y2;positions[base+8]=z2;
        positions[base+9]=x0;positions[base+10]=y0;positions[base+11]=z0;
        positions[base+12]=x2;positions[base+13]=y2;positions[base+14]=z2;
        positions[base+15]=x3;positions[base+16]=y3;positions[base+17]=z3;
      }
      if(teammateDirty)teammateNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
      if(enemyDirty)enemyNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
      teammateNameTagBatch.visible=teammateVisibleCount>0;
      enemyNameTagBatch.visible=enemyVisibleCount>0;
    }

    updateBatchedBotWeapons();
    updateBatchedTeamRings();
    updateBatchedNameTags();
    loadingUI?.set(82, 'SPAWNING PLAYERS...', 'Rigged bot clones, hitboxes, names, and weapons are prepared.');
    await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));

    // ---------- Strict 3D bot vision ----------
    // The old AI used a 2D X/Z segment and then kept tracking the target's exact
    // live position after visibility was lost. This ray test uses the same solid
    // AABBs as hitscan bullets and checks actual eye/chest/head heights.
    const botVisionDir=new THREE.Vector3();
    const botVisionRayStart=new THREE.Vector3();
    function entityVisionHeight(entity,upper=false){
      if(entity===player)return player.pos.y+(upper?.22:-.18);
      return upper?1.72:1.28;
    }
    function clearVisionRayXYZ(ox,oy,oz,tx,ty,tz){
      // R117: the AI and outline callers already own scalar coordinates. Enter the
      // LOS path directly with those values instead of first writing a temporary
      // Vector3 and immediately reading it back here. Ray math, operation order,
      // scratch vectors, origin offset, collider query and thresholds are unchanged.
      const dx=tx-ox,dy=ty-oy,dz=tz-oz;
      const fullDistance=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz);
      if(fullDistance<.08)return true;
      const invDistance=1/fullDistance;
      botVisionDir.x=dx*invDistance;botVisionDir.y=dy*invDistance;botVisionDir.z=dz*invDistance;
      botVisionRayStart.x=ox+botVisionDir.x*.07;
      botVisionRayStart.y=oy+botVisionDir.y*.07;
      botVisionRayStart.z=oz+botVisionDir.z*.07;
      const maxDistance=Math.max(0,fullDistance-.14);
      return nearestColliderHitDistance(botVisionRayStart,botVisionDir,maxDistance,.24)===Infinity;
    }
    function botCanSeeTarget(bot,target,knownAliveHostile=false){
      // R96: sensing validates target life/hostility immediately before this helper.
      // Those synchronous callers can skip repeating the same two pure predicates;
      // firing-time validation still uses the full live guard because state may have
      // changed since the earlier sensing tick. Ray origins, samples and occlusion
      // tests are otherwise unchanged.
      if(!knownAliveHostile&&(!isAliveEntity(target)||!isHostile(bot,target)))return false;
      const ox=bot.pos.x,oz=bot.pos.z,tx=target.pos.x,tz=target.pos.z;
      // Chest first, then a small head/shoulder sample. A target is visible only
      // when real geometry is exposed; neither sample bypasses map collision.
      if(clearVisionRayXYZ(ox,1.62,oz,tx,entityVisionHeight(target,false),tz))return true;
      return clearVisionRayXYZ(ox,1.62,oz,tx,entityVisionHeight(target,true),tz);
    }
    function hasLineOfSightXZ(ax,az,bx,bz){
      return clearVisionRayXYZ(ax,1.55,az,bx,1.35,bz);
    }
    function collideCircleXZ(x,z,radius) {
      if(!colliderGridReady){
        for(const c of colliders){const nx=Math.max(c.minX,Math.min(x,c.maxX)),nz=Math.max(c.minZ,Math.min(z,c.maxZ)),dx=x-nx,dz=z-nz;if(dx*dx+dz*dz<radius*radius)return true;}
        return false;
      }
      if(radius===MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS){
        const centerCellX=Math.floor(x/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),centerCellZ=Math.floor(z/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE);
        const gridX=centerCellX-colliderGridMinX,gridZ=centerCellZ-colliderGridMinZ;
        if(gridX>=0&&gridX<colliderGridWidth&&gridZ>=0&&gridZ<colliderGridHeight&&movementDefinitelyClearCells[gridZ*colliderGridWidth+gridX])return false;
      }
      const stamp=beginColliderQuery(),radiusSq=radius===MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS?MOVEMENT_COLLISION_RADIUS_SQ:radius*radius;
      // R110: the dense grid has fixed inclusive bounds. Clamp the query rectangle
      // once, then traverse only valid cells and index them directly. R109 tested
      // four bounds predicates for every visited cell, including empty outside-grid
      // cells. Removing those impossible cells preserves the exact in-grid encounter
      // order (cellX outer, cellZ inner), query-stamp behavior, and collision result.
      const minCellX=Math.max(Math.floor((x-radius)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),colliderGridMinX);
      const maxCellX=Math.min(Math.floor((x+radius)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),colliderGridMinX+colliderGridWidth-1);
      const minCellZ=Math.max(Math.floor((z-radius)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),colliderGridMinZ);
      const maxCellZ=Math.min(Math.floor((z+radius)/COLLIDER_CELL_SIZE),colliderGridMinZ+colliderGridHeight-1);
      for(let cellX=minCellX;cellX<=maxCellX;cellX++)for(let cellZ=minCellZ;cellZ<=maxCellZ;cellZ++){
        const bucket=colliderGrid[(cellZ-colliderGridMinZ)*colliderGridWidth+(cellX-colliderGridMinX)];if(!bucket)continue;
        for(let i=0;i<bucket.length;i++){
          const c=bucket[i];if(c._queryStamp===stamp)continue;c._queryStamp=stamp;
          const nx=Math.max(c.minX,Math.min(x,c.maxX)),nz=Math.max(c.minZ,Math.min(z,c.maxZ)),dx=x-nx,dz=z-nz;
          if(dx*dx+dz*dz<radiusSq)return true;
        }
      }
      return false;
    }
    function moveWithCollision(pos, dx, dz, radius) {
      // Adding a zero/underflowed delta can produce the exact same IEEE-754
      // coordinate. In that case the collision query cannot affect the resulting
      // position, so avoid walking the static-collider grid for an axis that does
      // not move at all. Object.is preserves signed-zero behavior: if the addition
      // changes -0 to +0, the original collision/update path still runs.
      const nx=pos.x+dx;
      if(!Object.is(nx,pos.x)&&!collideCircleXZ(nx,pos.z,radius)) pos.x=nx;
      const nz=pos.z+dz;
      if(!Object.is(nz,pos.z)&&!collideCircleXZ(pos.x,nz,radius)) pos.z=nz;
    }

    // ---------- Combat ----------
    function switchWeapon(index,force=false){
      if(index<0||index>=weapons.length||(!force&&index===weaponIndex)||(!force&&reloading))return;
      if(force&&reloading){reloading=false;stopReloadAudio();$('reloadText').textContent='';}
      setAiming(false);setCurrentGunOpacity(1);
      gunCats[weaponIndex].visible=false;
      weaponIndex=index;currentWeapon=weapons[index];resetWeaponAccuracy();
      gunCats[weaponIndex].visible=true;setCurrentGunOpacity(1);
      gunPivot.rotation.z=.16;lastShot=-999;updateHUD();
      playActionSfx('weaponSwitch');
    }
    function switchWeaponById(id,force=false){const item=weaponById.get(id);if(item)switchWeapon(item.index,force);}
    function weaponSlotFor(weapon){
      if(!weapon)return-1;
      if(weapon.id==='knife'||weapon.type==='melee')return SLOT_KNIFE;
      return weapon.category==='Pistols'?SLOT_SECONDARY:SLOT_PRIMARY;
    }
    function syncOwnedWeapons(){
      ownedWeapons=new Set(loadoutSlots.filter(Boolean));
    }
    function updateLoadoutPreview(useSelectedTeam=!started){
      const preview=useSelectedTeam?defaultLoadoutForTeam(selectedTeam):loadoutSlots;
      const ids=[preview[SLOT_PRIMARY],preview[SLOT_SECONDARY],preview[SLOT_KNIFE]||'knife'];
      const labels=['previewPrimary','previewSecondary','previewKnife'];
      for(let slot=0;slot<3;slot++){
        const element=$(labels[slot]);if(!element)continue;
        const name=ids[slot]?weaponById.get(ids[slot])?.weapon.name||'EMPTY':'EMPTY';
        element.innerHTML=`<b>${slot+1} · ${SLOT_LABELS[slot]}</b> ${name}`;
      }
    }
    function setLoadoutSlot(slot,id,equip=true){
      if(slot<0||slot>2)return false;
      if(slot===SLOT_KNIFE)id='knife';
      const item=id?weaponById.get(id):null;
      if(id&&!item)return false;
      if(item&&slot!==SLOT_KNIFE&&weaponSlotFor(item.weapon)!==slot)return false;
      loadoutSlots[slot]=id||null;syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);
      if(equip&&id)switchWeaponById(id,true);
      return true;
    }
    function switchOwnedSlot(slot,force=false){
      const id=loadoutSlots[slot];
      if(!id){$('reloadText').textContent=`SLOT ${slot+1} · ${SLOT_LABELS[slot]} IS EMPTY`;setTimeout(()=>{if($('reloadText').textContent.includes('IS EMPTY'))$('reloadText').textContent='';},900);return false;}
      switchWeaponById(id,force);return true;
    }
    function cycleOwnedSlot(){
      if(started&&!paused&&!gameOver&&!player.dead&&!buyMenuOpen)cancelRespawnProtection();
      const currentSlot=Math.max(0,loadoutSlots.indexOf(currentWeapon.id));
      for(let offset=1;offset<=3;offset++){
        const slot=(currentSlot+offset)%3;
        if(loadoutSlots[slot]){switchOwnedSlot(slot);return;}
      }
    }

    function startReload(){
      if(player.dead)return;
      if(started&&!paused&&!gameOver&&!buyMenuOpen)cancelRespawnProtection();
      if(reloading||currentWeapon.type==='melee'||currentWeapon.ammo>=currentWeapon.magSize)return;
      setAiming(false);resetWeaponAccuracy();reloading=true;reloadEnd=performance.now()/1000+currentWeapon.reload;
      $('reloadText').textContent='CAT IS GWOOMING...';reloadSound(currentWeapon);
    }
    function finishReload(){
      currentWeapon.ammo=currentWeapon.magSize;currentWeapon.reserve=Infinity;reloading=false;
      reloadFinishSound(currentWeapon);$('reloadText').textContent='';updateHUD();
    }

    function jump() {
      if(player.onGround && !player.dead && started && !paused){
        cancelRespawnProtection();
        player.velY=6.2; player.onGround=false; playActionSfx('jump');
      }
    }

    const aimDirection = new THREE.Vector3();
    const aimOrigin = new THREE.Vector3();
    const muzzleOrigin = new THREE.Vector3();
    const centerRaycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    const centerNDC = new THREE.Vector2(0,0);
    const aimRight = new THREE.Vector3();
    const aimUp = new THREE.Vector3();
    const preparedAimOrigin = new THREE.Vector3();
    const preparedAimDirection = new THREE.Vector3();
    function getAimRay(originOut, directionOut, spread=0, pelletIndex=0) {
      // fireWeapon() has already updated the camera hierarchy immediately before
      // resolving the muzzle position. No transform can change between that update
      // and this center ray, so avoid a second identical full hierarchy traversal.
      centerRaycaster.setFromCamera(centerNDC,camera);
      originOut.copy(centerRaycaster.ray.origin);
      directionOut.copy(centerRaycaster.ray.direction);
      if (spread>0) {
        aimRight.set(1,0,0).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);
        aimUp.set(0,1,0).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);
        const angle=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
        const radius=Math.sqrt(Math.random())*spread;
        directionOut.addScaledVector(aimRight,Math.cos(angle)*radius);
        directionOut.addScaledVector(aimUp,Math.sin(angle)*radius).normalize();
      }
      return directionOut;
    }
    function prepareMultiPelletAimRay(spread) {
      // Camera/crosshair state cannot change during one synchronous shotgun shot,
      // and fireWeapon() has already updated the camera hierarchy for the muzzle.
      // Reuse that exact current matrix; per-pellet random spread below retains the
      // original random-call order and floating-point operations.
      centerRaycaster.setFromCamera(centerNDC,camera);
      preparedAimOrigin.copy(centerRaycaster.ray.origin);
      preparedAimDirection.copy(centerRaycaster.ray.direction);
      if(spread>0){
        aimRight.set(1,0,0).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);
        aimUp.set(0,1,0).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);
      }
    }
    function getPreparedMultiPelletAimRay(originOut,directionOut,spread) {
      originOut.copy(preparedAimOrigin);
      directionOut.copy(preparedAimDirection);
      if(spread>0){
        const angle=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
        const radius=Math.sqrt(Math.random())*spread;
        directionOut.addScaledVector(aimRight,Math.cos(angle)*radius);
        directionOut.addScaledVector(aimUp,Math.sin(angle)*radius).normalize();
      }
      return directionOut;
    }

    function fireWeapon(now){
      if(reloading||player.dead||buyMenuOpen)return;
      if(started&&!paused&&!gameOver)cancelRespawnProtection();
      if(currentWeapon.type!=='melee'&&currentWeapon.ammo<=0){playActionSfx('emptyClick');startReload();return;}
      const interval=60/currentWeapon.rpm;if(now-lastShot<interval)return;
      lastShot=now;if(lastPurchase&&lastPurchase.id===currentWeapon.id)lastPurchase.used=true;
      // Trigger the acoustic transient before HUD, raycasting, particles, or any
      // other gameplay work. This keeps input-to-sound independent of scene cost.
      shotSound(currentWeapon);playPostShotMechanic(currentWeapon,1,0);playCasingMechanic(currentWeapon,1,0,false);
      if(currentWeapon.type!=='melee')currentWeapon.ammo--;
      updateHUD();
      currentInaccuracy=calculateInaccuracy(currentWeapon);
      camera.updateMatrixWorld(true);muzzleFlash.getWorldPosition(muzzleOrigin);
      const pelletBase=currentWeapon.type==='shotgun'?currentWeapon.spread:0;
      const shotSpread=pelletBase+currentInaccuracy;
      if(currentWeapon.pellets===1){
        getAimRay(aimOrigin,aimDirection,shotSpread,0);
        spawnProjectile(aimOrigin,aimDirection,player.team,currentWeapon.damage,0,player,muzzleOrigin,currentWeapon.range||null);
      }else{
        prepareMultiPelletAimRay(shotSpread);
        for(let i=0;i<currentWeapon.pellets;i++){
          getPreparedMultiPelletAimRay(aimOrigin,aimDirection,shotSpread);
          spawnProjectile(aimOrigin,aimDirection,player.team,currentWeapon.damage,0,player,muzzleOrigin,currentWeapon.range||null);
        }
      }
      muzzleFlash.material.opacity=currentWeapon.type==='melee'?.2:1;muzzleLight.intensity=currentWeapon.type==='melee'?.7:3.8;
      gunPivot.position.z=aiming?-1.16:-1.08;gunPivot.rotation.x=currentWeapon.type==='shotgun'?-.17:-.12;
      if(currentWeapon.type!=='melee'){
        applyShotRecoil(currentWeapon);shotsFired++;
        if(currentWeapon.id!=='negev')accuracyPenalty=Math.min(.035,accuracyPenalty+currentWeapon.spray*(1+Math.min(shotsFired,12)*.045));
        else accuracyPenalty=Math.min(.025,accuracyPenalty+.0022);
      }
    }

    function isHostile(a,b){if(!a||!b||a===b)return false;return activeMode.freeForAll?true:a.team!==b.team;}
    // R75: bot active membership changes only in configureBotsForMode(). Keep the
    // exact bots[] encounter order in a reusable list so per-frame systems do not
    // rescan inactive reserve slots or allocate a fresh filtered array.
    function rebuildConfiguredActiveBots(){
      configuredActiveBots.length=0;
      configuredActiveTeamBots.cats.length=0;
      configuredActiveTeamBots.meowfia.length=0;
      let weaponBatchChanged=false,teamRingBatchChanged=false,teammateNameTagChanged=false,enemyNameTagChanged=false;
      const teammatePositions=teammateNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.array;
      const enemyPositions=enemyNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.array;
      for(const bot of bots){
        if(bot.active){
          configuredActiveBots.push(bot);
          configuredActiveTeamBots[bot.team].push(bot);
        }else{
          // R83: membership changes only here. Immediately preserve the old batch
          // updater's exact inactive zero-scale result once, instead of revisiting
          // the same inactive slot every rendered gameplay frame.
          const i=bot._batchIndex;
          if(botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]!==0){
            botWeaponCombinedMatrix.makeScale(0,0,0);
            batchedBotWeapons.setMatrixAt(i,botWeaponCombinedMatrix);
            botWeaponBatchVisibility[i]=0;weaponBatchChanged=true;
          }
          // R85: inactive team-ring instances are likewise guaranteed hidden until
          // the next configuration. Zero their stable per-team slot once here so
          // the render-frame updater never needs to revisit reserve bots.
          const ringIndex=bot._teamRingBatchIndex,ringState=teamRingMatrixState[bot.team];
          if(ringState.scale[ringIndex]!==0){
            // R101: keep reserve-slot clearing byte-identical to the live ring writer
            // without constructing/copying an intermediate Matrix4.
            const ringMatrixArray=batchedTeamRings[bot.team].instanceMatrix.array,ringMatrixOffset=ringIndex*16;
            ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+1]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+2]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+3]=0;
            ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+4]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+5]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+6]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+7]=0;
            ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+8]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+9]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+10]=0;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+11]=0;
            ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+12]=bot.pos.x;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+13]=.025;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+14]=bot.pos.z;ringMatrixArray[ringMatrixOffset+15]=1;
            ringState.scale[ringIndex]=0;ringState.x[ringIndex]=bot.pos.x;ringState.z[ringIndex]=bot.pos.z;teamRingBatchChanged=true;
          }
          // R86: the old per-frame name-tag traversal zeroed any previously drawn
          // inactive slot on the first frame after configuration. Do the identical
          // buffer clear here at the membership transition, then omit reserve bots
          // from every subsequent frame until they become configured again.
          const tagBase=bot._nameTagAtlasIndex*18;
          if(bot._teammateNameTagDrawn){teammatePositions.fill(0,tagBase,tagBase+18);bot._teammateNameTagDrawn=false;teammateNameTagChanged=true;bot._nameTagBillboardBatch=0;}
          if(bot._enemyNameTagDrawn){enemyPositions.fill(0,tagBase,tagBase+18);bot._enemyNameTagDrawn=false;enemyNameTagChanged=true;bot._nameTagBillboardBatch=0;}
        }
      }
      if(weaponBatchChanged)batchedBotWeapons.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
      if(teamRingBatchChanged){batchedTeamRings.cats.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;batchedTeamRings.meowfia.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;}
      if(teammateNameTagChanged)teammateNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
      if(enemyNameTagChanged)enemyNameTagBatch.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    function activeBots(){return configuredActiveBots;}
    function getBotWeapon(bot){
      if(activeMode.gunGame){const id=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[Math.min(bot.gunGameLevel,GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1)];return weaponById.get(id).weapon;}
      const pool=bot.team==='cats'?['mp9','famas','m4a4','aug','ssg08']:['mac10','galil','ak47','sg553','nova'];
      return weaponById.get(pool[(bot.spawnSlot+roundNumber)%pool.length]).weapon;
    }
    function isBombMode(){return activeMode.objective==='bomb'||activeMode.objective==='retake';}
    function getSite(label){return BOMB_SITES[label]||BOMB_SITES.A;}
    function isWithinSiteDistance(entity,site,limit){const dx=entity.pos.x-site.x,dz=entity.pos.z-site.z;if(Math.abs(dx)>limit||Math.abs(dz)>limit)return false;return Math.hypot(dx,dz)<=limit;}
    function resetBombState(){
      bombState.enabled=isBombMode();bombState.planted=false;bombState.exploded=false;bombState.site=null;bombState.carrier=null;
      bombState.plantProgress=0;bombState.defuseProgress=0;bombState.nextBeep=0;bombState.plantAnnounced=false;playerDefusing=false;
      bombState.playerPlanting=false;bombState.playerDefusingSound=false;bombState.botDefusingSound=false;
      bombVisual.visible=false;objectiveVisuals.visible=bombState.enabled&&started&&!gameOver;
      for(const [label,visual] of Object.entries(siteVisuals))visual.visible=Boolean(activeMode.bombSites?.includes(label));
      $('objectiveInfo').className='';$('objectiveInfo').textContent='';
    }
    function chooseBombCarrier(){
      const previous=bombState.carrier;
      if(player.team==='meowfia'&&!player.dead){bombState.carrier=player;if(previous!==player)playActionSfx('bombPickup');return player;}
      let candidate=null,bestX=-Infinity;
      // R102: configureBotsForMode() maintains this cache in the exact original bots[]
      // encounter order, containing only currently active Meowfia bots. Avoid rescanning
      // inactive reserve slots and re-testing immutable team/activity predicates whenever
      // the objective needs a carrier; death and X ranking remain live and unchanged.
      const carrierCandidates=configuredActiveTeamBots.meowfia;
      for(let i=0;i<carrierCandidates.length;i++){const bot=carrierCandidates[i];if(!bot.dead&&bot.pos.x>bestX){candidate=bot;bestX=bot.pos.x;}}
      if(!candidate){bombState.carrier=null;return null;}
      bombState.carrier=candidate;if(previous!==bombState.carrier)playActionSfx('bombPickup',{volumeScale:.72});return bombState.carrier;
    }
    function setBombVisualAt(x,y,z){bombVisual.position.set(x,y,z);bombVisual.visible=true;}
    function beginBombObjective(){
      resetBombState();if(!bombState.enabled)return;
      const choices=activeMode.bombSites||['A'];const label=choices[(roundNumber-1)%choices.length];bombState.site=getSite(label);
      if(activeMode.objective==='retake'){
        bombState.planted=true;timeLeft=40;bombState.nextBeep=performance.now()/1000+.4;
        setBombVisualAt(bombState.site.x,.24,bombState.site.z);
        $('reloadText').textContent=player.team==='cats'
          ?`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} — RETAKE IT!`
          :`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} — DEFEND IT!`;
      }else{
        chooseBombCarrier();
        $('reloadText').textContent=player.team==='meowfia'
          ?`ATTACK ${choices.length>1?'A / B':bombState.site.label} · ${bombState.carrier===player?'YOU HAVE THE C4':'ESCORT THE C4'}`
          :`DEFEND ${choices.length>1?'A / B':bombState.site.label} · Terrorists have the C4`;
      }
      objectiveVisuals.visible=true;updateObjectiveHUD();
    }
    function bombCarrierAlive(){return bombState.carrier===player?!player.dead:Boolean(bombState.carrier&&bombState.carrier.active&&!bombState.carrier.dead);}
    function updateObjectiveHUD(playerNearSite){
      const el=$('objectiveInfo');
      if(!bombState.enabled||!roundActive){if(el.className)el.className='';if(el.textContent)el.textContent='';return;}
      if(!el.classList.contains('active'))el.classList.add('active');
      if(el.classList.contains('danger')!==bombState.planted)el.classList.toggle('danger',bombState.planted);
      let nextText;
      if(bombState.planted){
        const pct=Math.round(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(bombState.defuseProgress/5,0,1)*100);
        const near=playerNearSite===undefined?isWithinSiteDistance(player,bombState.site,2.65)&&!player.dead:playerNearSite;
        if(player.team==='cats')nextText=playerDefusing?`DEFUSING C4... ${pct}%`:near?`C4 AT ${bombState.site.label} · HOLD E TO DEFUSE · ${formatTime(timeLeft)}`:`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} · ${formatTime(timeLeft)}`;
        else nextText=near?`DEFEND THE C4 AT ${bombState.site.label} · ${formatTime(timeLeft)}`:`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} · DEFEND IT · ${formatTime(timeLeft)}`;
      }else if(bombState.plantProgress>0){
        nextText=bombState.carrier===player?`PLANTING C4... ${Math.round(bombState.plantProgress/3*100)}%`:`TERRORISTS PLANTING AT ${bombState.site.label}... ${Math.round(bombState.plantProgress/3*100)}%`;
      }else{
        const carrier=bombCarrierAlive()?(bombState.carrier===player?'YOU':bombState.carrier.name):'another Terrorist';
        nextText=player.team==='meowfia'
          ?(bombState.carrier===player?`YOU HAVE C4 · REACH ${activeMode.bombSites.length>1?'A OR B':bombState.site.label} AND HOLD E TO PLANT`:`C4 CARRIED BY ${carrier} · ESCORT THEM`)
          :`C4 CARRIED BY ${carrier} · DEFEND ${activeMode.bombSites.length>1?'A AND B':bombState.site.label}`;
      }
      if(el.textContent!==nextText)el.textContent=nextText;
    }
    function plantBomb(){
      if(bombState.planted||!bombState.site)return;
      bombState.planted=true;bombState.plantProgress=0;bombState.carrier=null;timeLeft=40;lastTimerText='';bombState.nextBeep=performance.now()/1000+.15;
      setBombVisualAt(bombState.site.x,.24,bombState.site.z);playActionSfx('bombPlant');
      addKillFeed(`<span class="${player.team==='meowfia'?'you':'enemy'}">TERRORISTS</span> planted the C4 at <span style="color:var(--gold)">${bombState.site.label}</span>.`);
      $('reloadText').textContent=player.team==='cats'
        ?`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} — HOLD E NEAR IT TO DEFUSE`
        :`C4 PLANTED AT ${bombState.site.label} — DEFEND IT`;
      updateObjectiveHUD();
    }
    function defuseBomb(){
      if(!bombState.planted||!roundActive)return;
      bombState.planted=false;bombVisual.visible=false;playActionSfx('bombDefuse');
      addKillFeed(`<span class="${player.team==='cats'?'you':'enemy'}">COUNTER-TERRORISTS</span> defused the C4!`);
      finishRound('cats','defused');
    }
    function explodeBomb(){
      if(!bombState.planted||!roundActive)return;
      bombState.exploded=true;bombState.planted=false;bombVisual.visible=false;
      impactBurst(bombState.site.x,.5,bombState.site.z,0xff9d42);impactBurst(bombState.site.x,1.4,bombState.site.z,0xff4c32);
      playActionSfx('bombExplosion');addKillFeed(`<span class="enemy">C4</span> exploded at <span style="color:var(--gold)">${bombState.site.label}</span>!`);
      finishRound('meowfia','exploded');
    }
    function nearestAliveCatBotToBomb(){
      // The caller only consumes this result as a boolean (defuser present / absent),
      // never the identity or distance of the nearest bot. Preserve the exact original
      // eligibility test, including Math.hypot at the radius boundary, but stop as soon
      // as any qualifying CT bot is found instead of scanning and ranking every bot.
      const site=bombState.site,limit=2.35;
      // Bot teams are immutable after construction, and teamRingBots.cats preserves
      // the original bots[] encounter order. Scan only CT bots instead of re-checking
      // every T bot on each planted-C4 frame; the exact activity/death/radius tests
      // and first-qualifying-bot result are unchanged.
      for(const b of configuredActiveTeamBots.cats)if(!b.dead){
        const dx=b.pos.x-site.x,dz=b.pos.z-site.z;
        if(Math.abs(dx)>limit||Math.abs(dz)>limit)continue;
        if(Math.hypot(dx,dz)<=limit)return b;
      }
      return null;
    }
    let nextPlantTickAt=0,nextDefuseLoopAt=0;
    function updateBombObjective(dt,now){
      if(!bombState.enabled||!roundActive||!bombState.site)return;
      let playerNearSite;
      if(!bombState.planted){
        if(!bombCarrierAlive())chooseBombCarrier();
        if(bombCarrierAlive()){
          setBombVisualAt(bombState.carrier.pos.x,1.05,bombState.carrier.pos.z);
          const atSite=isWithinSiteDistance(bombState.carrier,bombState.site,bombState.site.radius-1.1);
          if(bombState.carrier===player){
            const planting=atSite&&inputUseHeld&&!player.dead;
            if(planting&&!bombState.playerPlanting){playActionSfx('bombPlantStart');nextPlantTickAt=now;}
            bombState.playerPlanting=planting;
            if(planting){if(now>=nextPlantTickAt){playActionSfx('bombPlantTick',{volumeScale:.82});nextPlantTickAt=now+.42;}bombState.plantProgress+=dt;if(bombState.plantProgress>=3)plantBomb();}
            else bombState.plantProgress=Math.max(0,bombState.plantProgress-dt*1.8);
          }else{
            bombState.playerPlanting=false;
            if(atSite){if(bombState.plantProgress<=0){playActionSfx('bombPlantStart',{volumeScale:.56});nextPlantTickAt=now;}if(now>=nextPlantTickAt){playActionSfx('bombPlantTick',{volumeScale:.54});nextPlantTickAt=now+.46;}bombState.plantProgress+=dt;if(bombState.plantProgress>=3)plantBomb();}
            else bombState.plantProgress=Math.max(0,bombState.plantProgress-dt*1.6);
          }
        }else bombVisual.visible=false;
      }else{
        setBombVisualAt(bombState.site.x,.24,bombState.site.z);
        const beepGap=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(.12,.72,THREE.MathUtils.clamp(timeLeft/40,0,1));
        if(now>=bombState.nextBeep){playActionSfx(timeLeft<10?'bombBeepFast':'bombBeep');bombState.nextBeep=now+beepGap;}
        // Reuse the exact player/site distance result for both defuse eligibility
        // and the objective HUD. R53 computed the same pure distance test twice
        // per planted-C4 gameplay frame.
        playerNearSite=isWithinSiteDistance(player,bombState.site,2.65)&&!player.dead;
        const playerNear=player.team==='cats'&&playerNearSite;
        const nextPlayerDefusing=playerNear&&inputUseHeld;
        if(nextPlayerDefusing&&!playerDefusing){playActionSfx('bombDefuseStart');nextDefuseLoopAt=now;}
        playerDefusing=nextPlayerDefusing;
        const botDefuser=nearestAliveCatBotToBomb();
        if(botDefuser&&!bombState.botDefusingSound&&!playerDefusing){playActionSfx('bombDefuseStart',{volumeScale:.52});nextDefuseLoopAt=now;}
        bombState.botDefusingSound=Boolean(botDefuser);
        if(playerDefusing||botDefuser){if(now>=nextDefuseLoopAt){playActionSfx('bombDefuseLoop',{volumeScale:playerDefusing?.78:.48});nextDefuseLoopAt=now+.36;}bombState.defuseProgress+=dt;if(bombState.defuseProgress>=5){defuseBomb();return;}}
        else bombState.defuseProgress=Math.max(0,bombState.defuseProgress-dt*1.8);
      }
      updateObjectiveHUD(bombState.planted?playerNearSite:undefined);
    }
    function getBotObjectivePoint(bot){
      if(!bombState.enabled||!roundActive||!bombState.site)return null;
      if(bombState.planted){
        if(bot.team==='cats')return bombState.site;
        const point=bot._objectiveGuardPoint;
        point.x=bombState.site.x+bot._objectiveGuardCos*5.3;point.z=bombState.site.z+bot._objectiveGuardSin*5.3;return point;
      }
      if(bot.team==='meowfia')return bombState.site;
      return null;
    }
    function updateModeMeta(){
      $('modeLabel').textContent=activeMode.name;
      const ownSide=!started||activeMode.freeForAll?'':` · YOU: ${player.team==='cats'?'CT':'T'}`;
      if(activeMode.roundBased)$('roundInfo').textContent=`ROUND ${roundNumber} · ${roundScore.cats}-${roundScore.meowfia}${ownSide}`;
      else if(activeMode.gunGame){const levelId=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[Math.min(player.gunGameLevel,GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1)],levelWeapon=weaponById.get(levelId).weapon;$('roundInfo').textContent=`LEVEL ${player.gunGameLevel+1}/${GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length} · ${gunGameCategoryForId(levelId)} · ${levelWeapon.name} · ${player.gunGameKillsOnLevel}/${player.gunGameLevel===GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1?1:2} KILLS`;}
      else $('roundInfo').textContent=`TIMED MATCH${ownSide}`;
      $('money').textContent=activeMode.buyMode==='economy'?`$${player.money}`:activeMode.buyMode==='free'?'FREE BUY · ∞ RESERVE':activeMode.gunGame?'ARMS RACE':'AUTO LOADOUT';
    }
    function updateScoreHUD(){
      if(activeMode.freeForAll||activeMode.gunGame){
        let leaderKills=player.kills||0,leaderLevel=player.gunGameLevel||0;
        for(const bot of bots)if(bot.active){if((bot.kills||0)>leaderKills)leaderKills=bot.kills||0;if((bot.gunGameLevel||0)>leaderLevel)leaderLevel=bot.gunGameLevel||0;}
        $('leftScoreLabel').textContent=activeMode.gunGame?'LEVEL':'YOU';
        $('rightScoreLabel').textContent=activeMode.gunGame?'WEADEW':'WEADEW';
        $('kills').textContent=activeMode.gunGame?`${player.gunGameLevel+1}`:player.kills;
        $('deaths').textContent=activeMode.gunGame?`${leaderLevel+1}`:leaderKills;
      }else{
        $('leftScoreLabel').textContent='Counter-Terrorists';$('rightScoreLabel').textContent='Terrorists';
        $('kills').textContent=activeMode.roundBased?roundScore.cats:teamKills.cats;
        $('deaths').textContent=activeMode.roundBased?roundScore.meowfia:teamKills.meowfia;
      }
      updateModeMeta();
    }
    function configureBotsForMode(){
      if(activeMode.freeForAll){
        const limit=Math.min(bots.length,activeMode.freeForAllBots||9);
        bots.forEach((b,i)=>{b.active=i<limit;});
      }else{
        const targets=activeMode.teamCounts||{cats:5,meowfia:5};
        for(const team of ['cats','meowfia']){
          const reserve=bots.filter(b=>b.team===team);
          const botTarget=Math.max(0,(targets[team]||0)-(player.team===team?1:0));
          reserve.forEach((b,i)=>{b.active=i<botTarget;});
        }
      }
      for(const b of bots){b.kills=0;b.deaths=0;b.gunGameLevel=0;b.gunGameKillsOnLevel=0;b.dead=true;stopBotRagdoll(b,true);b.target=null;}
      stopPlayerRagdoll();rebuildConfiguredActiveBots();
    }
    function setGunGameLevel(entity,level,equip=true,resetProgress=true){
      const clamped=Math.max(0,Math.min(GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1,level|0));
      entity.gunGameLevel=clamped;if(resetProgress)entity.gunGameKillsOnLevel=0;
      const id=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[clamped];
      if(entity===player){
        loadoutSlots[SLOT_PRIMARY]=id;
        if(!loadoutSlots[SLOT_SECONDARY])loadoutSlots[SLOT_SECONDARY]=defaultSecondaryForTeam(player.team);
        loadoutSlots[SLOT_KNIFE]='knife';
        syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);
        if(equip)switchOwnedSlot(SLOT_PRIMARY,true);
      }else entity.weaponDef=weaponById.get(id).weapon;
      return id;
    }
    function announceGunGameAdvance(entity,nextId,prefix='ADVANCED'){
      if(entity!==player)return;
      $('reloadText').textContent=`${prefix} · ${weaponById.get(nextId).weapon.name}`;playActionSfx('buySuccess');
      setTimeout(()=>{if($('reloadText').textContent.startsWith(prefix))$('reloadText').textContent='';},1200);
    }
    function advanceGunGame(attacker,eliminationWeapon){
      if(!activeMode.gunGame||!attacker)return false;
      const level=attacker.gunGameLevel||0;
      const expectedId=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[Math.min(level,GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1)];
      if(!eliminationWeapon||eliminationWeapon.id!==expectedId){
        if(attacker===player){$('reloadText').textContent=`USE LEVEL WEAPON: ${weaponById.get(expectedId).weapon.name}`;setTimeout(()=>{if($('reloadText').textContent.startsWith('USE LEVEL WEAPON'))$('reloadText').textContent='';},1100);}
        updateScoreHUD();return false;
      }
      const needed=level===GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1?1:2;
      attacker.gunGameKillsOnLevel=(attacker.gunGameKillsOnLevel||0)+1;
      if(attacker.gunGameKillsOnLevel<needed){updateScoreHUD();return true;}
      if(level>=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1){attacker.gunGameKillsOnLevel=0;matchWinner=attacker;endGame();return true;}
      const nextId=setGunGameLevel(attacker,level+1,true);announceGunGameAdvance(attacker,nextId);
      updateScoreHUD();return true;
    }
    function applyGunGameKnifeSteal(attacker,victim){
      if(!activeMode.gunGame||!attacker||!victim)return false;
      const victimLevel=victim.gunGameLevel||0;
      if(victimLevel>0)setGunGameLevel(victim,victimLevel-1,true,true);
      else victim.gunGameKillsOnLevel=0;
      const attackerLevel=attacker.gunGameLevel||0;
      if(attackerLevel<GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1){
        const nextId=setGunGameLevel(attacker,attackerLevel+1,true,true);announceGunGameAdvance(attacker,nextId,'KNIFE STEAL');
      }
      if(victim===player){$('reloadText').textContent='KNIFED · LOST ONE LEVEL';playActionSfx('damageHit',{volumeScale:.9});setTimeout(()=>{if($('reloadText').textContent.startsWith('KNIFED'))$('reloadText').textContent='';},1250);}
      updateScoreHUD();return true;
    }
    function awardElimination(attacker,victim,eliminationWeapon=null){
      if(!attacker||attacker===victim)return;
      attacker.kills=(attacker.kills||0)+1;
      if(attacker===player&&activeMode.buyMode==='economy')player.money=Math.min(16000,player.money+300);
      if(!activeMode.freeForAll&&!activeMode.roundBased&&!activeMode.gunGame&&teamKills[attacker.team]!==undefined)teamKills[attacker.team]++;
      if(activeMode.gunGame){
        if(eliminationWeapon?.id==='knife')applyGunGameKnifeSteal(attacker,victim);
        else advanceGunGame(attacker,eliminationWeapon);
      }
      updateScoreHUD();
    }
    function countAlive(team){
      let n=player.team===team&&!player.dead?1:0;
      for(const b of configuredActiveTeamBots[team])if(!b.dead)n++;
      return n;
    }
    function checkRoundEnd(){
      if(!activeMode.roundBased||!roundActive||gameOver)return;
      const cats=countAlive('cats'),enemies=countAlive('meowfia');
      if(isBombMode()){
        if(bombState.planted){if(cats<=0)finishRound('meowfia','elimination');return;}
        if(cats<=0)finishRound('meowfia','elimination');else if(enemies<=0)finishRound('cats','elimination');
        return;
      }
      if(cats<=0)finishRound('meowfia');else if(enemies<=0)finishRound('cats');
    }
    function finishRound(winner,reason='elimination'){
      if(!roundActive)return;playerSurvivedLastRound=!player.dead;roundActive=false;firing=false;setAiming(false);playerDefusing=false;roundScore[winner]++;
      bombVisual.visible=false;$('objectiveInfo').className='';
      if(activeMode.buyMode==='economy')player.money=Math.min(16000,player.money+(winner===player.team?3250:1400));
      playActionSfx(winner===player.team?'roundWin':'roundLoss');
      updateScoreHUD();
      const reasonText=reason==='defused'?'C4 DEFUSED':reason==='exploded'?'C4 EXPLODED':reason==='timeout'?'TIME EXPIRED':'TEAM ELIMINATED';
      $('reloadText').textContent=`${winner==='cats'?'COUNTER-TERRORISTS':'TERRORISTS'} WIN · ${reasonText}`;
      if(roundScore[winner]>=activeMode.roundTarget){matchWinner=winner;setTimeout(()=>{if(!gameOver)endGame();},900);return;}
      roundRestartAt=performance.now()/1000+3;
    }
    function resolveRoundTimeout(){
      if(isBombMode()){
        if(bombState.planted)explodeBomb();else finishRound('cats','timeout');
        return;
      }
      const catValue=countAlive('cats')*100+(player.team==='cats'&&!player.dead?player.health:0)+activeBots().filter(b=>b.team==='cats'&&!b.dead).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.health,0);
      const enemyValue=countAlive('meowfia')*100+(player.team==='meowfia'&&!player.dead?player.health:0)+activeBots().filter(b=>b.team==='meowfia'&&!b.dead).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.health,0);
      finishRound(catValue>enemyValue?'cats':'meowfia','timeout');
    }
    function startRound(initial=false){
      roundActive=true;stopReloadAudio();reloading=false;firing=false;lastCountdownSecond=-1;nextHeartbeatAt=0;if(!initial)roundNumber++;
      playActionSfx('roundStart');
      timeLeft=activeMode.roundTime;lastTimerText='';buyWindowUntil=activeMode.buyMode==='economy'?performance.now()/1000+20:0;lastPurchase=null;
      if(!initial&&!playerSurvivedLastRound&&activeMode.buyMode==='economy'){
        loadoutSlots=[null,defaultSecondaryForTeam(player.team),'knife'];syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);switchOwnedSlot(SLOT_SECONDARY,true);
      }
      player.health=100;player.dead=false;player.velY=0;player.onGround=true;respawnPlayer();
      for(const w of weapons){w.ammo=w.magSize;w.reserve=Infinity;}
      for(const b of bots)respawnBot(b,true);
      if(activeMode.objective==='retake'){
        const retakePool=player.team==='meowfia'?['ak47','galil','sg553','mac10','nova']:['m4a4','famas','aug','ssg08','mp9'];const id=retakePool[(roundNumber-1)%retakePool.length];
        loadoutSlots=[id,defaultSecondaryForTeam(player.team),'knife'];syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);switchOwnedSlot(SLOT_PRIMARY,true);
      }
      beginBombObjective();
      if(activeMode.buyMode==='economy')$('reloadText').textContent='BUY TIME — PWESS B';
      else if(!isBombMode())$('reloadText').textContent='';
      setTimeout(()=>{if(roundActive&&activeMode.buyMode==='economy'&&!bombState.planted)$('reloadText').textContent='';},1800);
      updateScoreHUD();updateHUD();
    }
    function isBuyAllowed(){
      if(!started||gameOver||activeMode.buyMode==='none')return false;
      if(activeMode.buyMode==='free')return true;
      return performance.now()/1000<=buyWindowUntil;
    }
    function createBuyWeaponButton(w,slot,free){
      const selected=loadoutSlots[slot]===w.id;
      const canAfford=selected||free||player.money>=w.price;
      const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';
      btn.className='buyWeapon'+(selected?' slot-equipped':'')+(w===currentWeapon?' current':'')+(canAfford?'':' locked')+(slot===SLOT_KNIFE?' fixedKnife':'');
      const priceText=slot===SLOT_KNIFE?'ALWAYS OWNED':free?'FREE':selected?'EQUIPPED':`$${w.price}`;
      btn.innerHTML=`<b>${w.name}</b><span class="stats">DMG ${w.damage} · ${w.rpm} RPM · ${w.type==='melee'?'MELEE':`${w.magSize} per mag`}</span><span class="price">${priceText}</span>`;
      btn.onclick=()=>buyWeapon(w.id);return btn;
    }
    function renderBuyMenu(){
      const grid=$('buyGrid');grid.replaceChildren();
      const free=activeMode.buyMode==='free';$('buyMoney').textContent=free?'FREE LOADOUT':`$${player.money}`;
      const slotBar=document.createElement('div');slotBar.className='buySlotBar';
      for(let slot=0;slot<3;slot++){
        const id=loadoutSlots[slot],w=id?weaponById.get(id)?.weapon:null;
        const card=document.createElement('button');card.type='button';card.className='buySlotCard'+(slot===activeBuySection?' active':'');
        card.innerHTML=`<span class="slotNumber">${slot+1} · ${SLOT_LABELS[slot]}</span><b>${w?.name||'EMPTY'}</b>`;
        card.disabled=!w;card.onclick=()=>{activeBuySection=slot;switchOwnedSlot(slot,true);renderBuyMenu();};slotBar.appendChild(card);
      }
      grid.appendChild(slotBar);
      const sections=[
        {slot:SLOT_PRIMARY,items:weapons.filter(w=>w.id!=='goldenpaw'&&w.id!=='knife'&&weaponSlotFor(w)===SLOT_PRIMARY)},
        {slot:SLOT_SECONDARY,items:weapons.filter(w=>weaponSlotFor(w)===SLOT_SECONDARY)},
        {slot:SLOT_KNIFE,items:[weaponById.get('knife').weapon]}
      ];
      if(!sections.some(section=>section.slot===activeBuySection))activeBuySection=SLOT_PRIMARY;
      const sectionData=sections.find(section=>section.slot===activeBuySection)||sections[0];
      const section=document.createElement('section');section.className='buySection';
      let visibleItems=sectionData.items;
      if(sectionData.slot===SLOT_PRIMARY){
        const categories=[...new Set(sectionData.items.map(w=>w.category))];
        if(!categories.includes(activeBuyPrimaryCategory))activeBuyPrimaryCategory=categories[0]||'Rifles';
        const subtabs=document.createElement('div');subtabs.className='buySubcategoryTabs';subtabs.setAttribute('aria-label','Primary weapon class');
        for(const category of categories){
          const subtab=document.createElement('button');subtab.type='button';subtab.className='buySubcategoryTab'+(category===activeBuyPrimaryCategory?' active':'');
          subtab.textContent=category.toUpperCase();subtab.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(category===activeBuyPrimaryCategory));
          subtab.onclick=()=>{activeBuyPrimaryCategory=category;renderBuyMenu();};subtabs.appendChild(subtab);
        }
        section.appendChild(subtabs);visibleItems=sectionData.items.filter(w=>w.category===activeBuyPrimaryCategory);
      }
      const weaponGrid=document.createElement('div');weaponGrid.className='buyWeaponGrid';
      for(const w of visibleItems)weaponGrid.appendChild(createBuyWeaponButton(w,sectionData.slot,free));
      section.appendChild(weaponGrid);grid.appendChild(section);
    }
    function buyWeapon(id){
      const item=weaponById.get(id);if(!item)return;const w=item.weapon;const slot=weaponSlotFor(w);const free=activeMode.buyMode==='free';
      if(slot===SLOT_KNIFE){setLoadoutSlot(SLOT_KNIFE,'knife',true);$('buyNotice').textContent='KNIFE EQUIPPED · SLOT 3';renderBuyMenu();return;}
      if(!isBuyAllowed()){$('buyNotice').textContent='BUY TIME IS OVER.';playActionSfx('buyFail');return;}
      const previousId=loadoutSlots[slot];const alreadySelected=previousId===id;const mustPay=!free&&!alreadySelected;
      if(mustPay&&player.money<w.price){$('buyNotice').textContent='NOT ENOUGH MONEY.';playActionSfx('buyFail');return;}
      if(mustPay){player.money-=w.price;lastPurchase={id,price:w.price,round:roundNumber,used:false,slot,previousId};}
      setLoadoutSlot(slot,id,true);w.ammo=w.magSize;w.reserve=Infinity;
      $('buyNotice').textContent=`SLOT ${slot+1} · ${SLOT_LABELS[slot]}: ${w.name}`;playActionSfx('buySuccess');updateHUD();updateScoreHUD();renderBuyMenu();
    }
    function sellLastPurchase(){
      if(activeMode.buyMode!=='economy'||!lastPurchase||lastPurchase.used||lastPurchase.round!==roundNumber||currentWeapon.id!==lastPurchase.id){$('buyNotice').textContent='NOTHING ELIGIBLE TO SELL.';playActionSfx('buyFail');return;}
      const purchase=lastPurchase;player.money=Math.min(16000,player.money+purchase.price);lastPurchase=null;
      loadoutSlots[purchase.slot]=purchase.previousId||null;loadoutSlots[SLOT_KNIFE]='knife';syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);
      const fallback=loadoutSlots[purchase.slot]||loadoutSlots[SLOT_SECONDARY]||loadoutSlots[SLOT_KNIFE];if(fallback)switchWeaponById(fallback,true);
      playActionSfx('buySuccess');updateScoreHUD();updateHUD();renderBuyMenu();$('buyNotice').textContent='LAST BUY REFUNDED.';
    }
    $('buySell').onclick=sellLastPurchase;
    function openBuyMenu(){
      if(!isBuyAllowed()){$('reloadText').textContent=activeMode.gunGame?'NO SHOP IN GUN GAME':activeMode.objective==='retake'?'AUTO LOADOUTS IN RETAKES':'BUY TIME IS OVER';playActionSfx('buyFail');setTimeout(()=>{$('reloadText').textContent='';},1300);return;}
