      buyMenuOpen=true;firing=false;stopReloadAudio();reloading=false;setAiming(false);forceClearScope();$('reloadText').textContent='';clearInputKeys();renderBuyMenu();$('buyNotice').textContent='';
      $('buyMenu').classList.add('open');$('buyMenu').setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
      if(activePointerLockElement()){explicitPointerUnlock=true;(document.exitPointerLock||document.mozExitPointerLock||document.webkitExitPointerLock).call(document);}
    }
    function closeBuyMenu(event=null){
      if(!buyMenuOpen)return;playActionSfx('menuClose');buyMenuOpen=false;$('buyMenu').classList.remove('open');$('buyMenu').setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      lastTime=performance.now();if(!usingMobileControls()&&!gameOver)requestMouseLock(event);
    }
    function toggleBuyMenu(event=null){if(!started||gameOver)return;if(buyMenuOpen)closeBuyMenu(event);else openBuyMenu();}
    $('buyClose').onclick=e=>closeBuyMenu(e);

    function registerTeamKill(team){
      if(teamKills[team]===undefined)return;teamKills[team]++;updateScoreHUD();
    }
    function damageBot(bot,amount,attacker,eliminationWeapon=null){
      if(!bot.active||bot.dead||performance.now()/1000<bot.invulnerableUntil)return;
      bot.health-=amount;meowHit();if(attacker===player)hitMarker();impactBurst(bot.pos.x,1.45,bot.pos.z,bot.fur);
      if(bot.health<=0){
        bot.dead=true;bot.deaths++;bot.target=null;bot.respawn=activeMode.respawn?performance.now()/1000+2.5:Infinity;startBotRagdoll(bot,attacker);
        awardElimination(attacker,bot,eliminationWeapon);
        const killerName=attacker===player?'YOU':(attacker?.name||'An opponent');
        const killerClass=attacker===player||attacker?.team===player.team?'you':'enemy';const victimClass=bot.team===player.team?'you':'enemy';
        addKillFeed(`<span class="${killerClass}">${killerName}</span> launched a cat at <span class="${victimClass}">${bot.name}</span>.`);
        if(bombState.enabled&&!bombState.planted&&bombState.carrier===bot){playActionSfx('bombDrop');bombState.carrier=null;bombState.plantProgress=0;chooseBombCarrier();}
        playActionSfx('bodyDrop',{volumeScale:.72});if(attacker===player)playActionSfx('killConfirm');checkRoundEnd();
      }
    }
    function damagePlayer(amount,attacker,eliminationWeapon=null){
      const now=performance.now()/1000;if(player.dead||gameOver||now<player.invulnerableUntil)return;
      if(respawnProtectionArmed)respawnProtectionArmed=false;
      player.health=Math.max(0,player.health-amount);playActionSfx('playerHurt',{volumeScale:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(amount/35,.35,1)});updateHUD();
      const flash=$('damageFlash');flash.style.background='rgba(255,30,30,.22)';setTimeout(()=>flash.style.background='rgba(255,30,30,0)',70);
      if(player.health<=0){
        player.dead=true;player.deaths++;respawnProtectionArmed=false;player.invulnerableUntil=0;firing=false;stopReloadAudio();reloading=false;setAiming(false);forceClearScope();respawnAt=activeMode.respawn?now+2.5:Infinity;startPlayerRagdoll(attacker);
        awardElimination(attacker,player,eliminationWeapon);
        const attackerSide=attacker?.team==='cats'?'A Counter-Terrorist':attacker?.team==='meowfia'?'A Terrorist':'An opponent';
        addKillFeed(`<span class="enemy">${attacker?.name||attackerSide}</span> bonked <span class="you">YOU</span> with concentwated meowing`);
        if(bombState.enabled&&!bombState.planted&&bombState.carrier===player){playActionSfx('bombDrop');bombState.carrier=null;bombState.plantProgress=0;chooseBombCarrier();}
        $('reloadText').textContent=activeMode.respawn?'RESPAWNING...':'ELIMINATED — WAIT FOR ROUND';playActionSfx('playerDeath');checkRoundEnd();
      }
    }
    function respawnPlayer(){
      const wasDead=player.dead;stopPlayerRagdoll();const p=chooseTeamSpawn(player.team,player,player.spawnSlot);player.pos.set(p.x,1.65,p.z);player.velX=player.velZ=player.moveSpeed=0;player.yaw=p.yaw;player.pitch=0;resetWeaponAccuracy();resetFootsteps();
      player.health=100;player.dead=false;player.velY=0;player.onGround=true;armRespawnProtection();
      if(activeMode.gunGame){
        const expectedId=GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[Math.min(player.gunGameLevel,GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length-1)];
        if(loadoutSlots[SLOT_PRIMARY]!==expectedId||currentWeapon.id!==expectedId)setGunGameLevel(player,player.gunGameLevel,true,false);
      }
      currentWeapon.ammo=currentWeapon.magSize;currentWeapon.reserve=Infinity;
      if(wasDead)playActionSfx('respawn');if(!bombState.enabled)$('reloadText').textContent='';updateHUD();
    }

    const botAimDirection=new THREE.Vector3();
    const botMuzzleOrigin=new THREE.Vector3();
    const botAimRight=new THREE.Vector3();
    const botAimUp=new THREE.Vector3();
    const botPelletDirection=new THREE.Vector3();
    const BOT_WORLD_UP=new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0);
    const botWeaponProfileCache=new Map();
    function botWeaponProfile(w){
      let profile=botWeaponProfileCache.get(w.id);if(profile)return profile;
      if(w.type==='melee')profile={maxRange:2.5,minRange:0,preferred:1.5,cone:.02,moveCone:.01,burstMin:1,burstMax:1,pauseMin:.35,pauseMax:.55,reactionMin:.22,reactionMax:.38};
      else if(w.sniper)profile={maxRange:155,minRange:12,preferred:38,cone:w.id==='awp'?.0038:.0052,moveCone:.008,burstMin:1,burstMax:w.id==='scar20'?2:1,pauseMin:w.id==='scar20'?.22:.7,pauseMax:w.id==='scar20'?.4:1.05,reactionMin:.38,reactionMax:.72};
      else if(w.type==='shotgun')profile={maxRange:15,minRange:0,preferred:8,cone:.025,moveCone:.02,burstMin:1,burstMax:w.id==='xm1014'?2:1,pauseMin:.34,pauseMax:.7,reactionMin:.26,reactionMax:.52};
      else if(w.category==='Pistols')profile={maxRange:w.id==='deagle'?38:31,minRange:0,preferred:17,cone:w.id==='deagle'?.012:.017,moveCone:.018,burstMin:1,burstMax:w.id==='glock'?3:2,pauseMin:.25,pauseMax:.58,reactionMin:.28,reactionMax:.55};
      else if(w.type==='smg')profile={maxRange:29,minRange:0,preferred:14,cone:.019,moveCone:.018,burstMin:3,burstMax:6,pauseMin:.18,pauseMax:.38,reactionMin:.24,reactionMax:.48};
      else if(w.id==='negev')profile={maxRange:43,minRange:0,preferred:24,cone:.023,moveCone:.024,burstMin:5,burstMax:10,pauseMin:.22,pauseMax:.46,reactionMin:.34,reactionMax:.62};
      else if(w.id==='aug'||w.id==='sg553')profile={maxRange:58,minRange:0,preferred:29,cone:.0105,moveCone:.017,burstMin:2,burstMax:5,pauseMin:.22,pauseMax:.48,reactionMin:.3,reactionMax:.58};
      else profile={maxRange:46,minRange:0,preferred:24,cone:.0135,moveCone:.019,burstMin:2,burstMax:5,pauseMin:.23,pauseMax:.5,reactionMin:.3,reactionMax:.6};
      profile=Object.freeze(profile);botWeaponProfileCache.set(w.id,profile);return profile;
    }
    function botWeaponProfileFor(bot,w=bot.weaponDef||getBotWeapon(bot)){
      if(bot._weaponProfileWeapon!==w){bot._weaponProfileWeapon=w;bot._weaponProfile=botWeaponProfile(w);}
      return bot._weaponProfile;
    }
    function randomSignedAverage(){return((Math.random()+Math.random()+Math.random())/3-.5)*2;}
    function botShoot(bot,now,target){
      if(now<bot.nextShot||now<bot.reactionUntil||now<bot.burstPauseUntil||!isAliveEntity(target)||!isHostile(bot,target)||(!roundActive&&activeMode.roundBased))return;
      // Revalidate visibility at the exact firing instant. A 100 ms sensing tick
      // can never authorize a shot after the target has stepped behind cover.
      if(!botCanSeeTarget(bot,target)){bot.los=false;bot.lastSeenAt=now;return;}
      const w=bot.weaponDef||getBotWeapon(bot),profile=botWeaponProfileFor(bot,w);
      const dx=target.pos.x-bot.pos.x,dz=target.pos.z-bot.pos.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
      if(dist>profile.maxRange||dist<profile.minRange)return;

      bot.nextShot=now+60/w.rpm*(.96+Math.random()*.16);
      bot.burstShots++;
      const burstLimit=profile.burstMin+Math.floor(Math.random()*(profile.burstMax-profile.burstMin+1));
      if(bot.burstShots>=burstLimit){
        bot.burstShots=0;
        bot.burstPauseUntil=now+profile.pauseMin+Math.random()*(profile.pauseMax-profile.pauseMin);
      }
      if(now>=bot.aimBiasRefresh){
        bot.aimBiasRefresh=now+.28+Math.random()*.38;
        bot.aimBiasX=randomSignedAverage();bot.aimBiasY=randomSignedAverage();
      }

      botMuzzleOrigin.set(bot.pos.x,1.35,bot.pos.z);
      const targetY=entityVisionHeight(target,false);
      botAimDirection.set(target.pos.x-botMuzzleOrigin.x,targetY-botMuzzleOrigin.y,target.pos.z-botMuzzleOrigin.z).normalize();
      botAimRight.crossVectors(botAimDirection,BOT_WORLD_UP);
      if(botAimRight.lengthSq()<1e-5)botAimRight.set(1,0,0);else botAimRight.normalize();
      botAimUp.crossVectors(botAimRight,botAimDirection).normalize();

      const moving=bot._moveMagnitude>.22;
      const rangeStress=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((dist-profile.preferred)/Math.max(1,profile.maxRange-profile.preferred),0,1);
      const sprayStress=Math.min(1,bot.burstShots/Math.max(2,profile.burstMax));
      let cone=profile.cone+(moving?profile.moveCone:0)+rangeStress*profile.cone*.9+sprayStress*profile.cone*.45;
      // A freshly spotted target is deliberately less precise while the bot settles.
      cone*=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(1.55,1,THREE.MathUtils.clamp((now-bot.visibleSince)/.9,0,1));
      const biasScale=cone*.62;
      botAimDirection.addScaledVector(botAimRight,bot.aimBiasX*biasScale+randomSignedAverage()*cone)
        .addScaledVector(botAimUp,bot.aimBiasY*biasScale+randomSignedAverage()*cone*.78).normalize();

      const pellets=w.type==='shotgun'?Math.min(5,w.pellets):1;
      for(let i=0;i<pellets;i++){
        const shotDir=i===0?botAimDirection:botPelletDirection.copy(botAimDirection)
          .addScaledVector(botAimRight,randomSignedAverage()*w.spread)
          .addScaledVector(botAimUp,randomSignedAverage()*w.spread).normalize();
        spawnProjectile(botMuzzleOrigin,shotDir,bot.team,w.damage,0,bot,botMuzzleOrigin,profile.maxRange);
      }
      {const px=bot.pos.x-player.pos.x,pz=bot.pos.z-player.pos.z;const distance=Math.hypot(px,pz);const proximity=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1-distance/90,.05,.58);const pan=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(px/Math.max(6,distance),-.90,.90);shotSound(w,proximity,pan,true);playPostShotMechanic(w,proximity,pan);playCasingMechanic(w,proximity,pan,bot);}
      bot.weapon.rotation.x=-.7;
    }

    // ---------- Effects ----------
    const MAX_PARTICLES = lowSpec ? 48 : 96;
    const particlePositions = new Float32Array(MAX_PARTICLES*3);
    const particleColors = new Float32Array(MAX_PARTICLES*3);
    const particleGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    particleGeometry.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(particlePositions,3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    particleGeometry.setAttribute('color',new THREE.BufferAttribute(particleColors,3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    particleGeometry.setDrawRange(0,0);
    const particleCloud = new THREE.Points(particleGeometry,new THREE.PointsMaterial({size:.11,vertexColors:true,transparent:true,opacity:.95,sizeAttenuation:true}));
    particleCloud.frustumCulled=false;
    particleCloud.updateMatrix();particleCloud.matrixAutoUpdate=false;particleCloud.matrixWorldAutoUpdate=false;
    scene.add(particleCloud);    const particles=[];
    const particlePool=[];
    const impactColorScratch=new THREE.Color();
    let previousParticleDrawCount = 0, particleColorDirty = false;
    function impactBurst(x,y,z,color) {
      impactColorScratch.setHex(color);
      const count=lowSpec?3:5;
      for(let i=0;i<count&&particles.length<MAX_PARTICLES;i++){
        const particle=particlePool.pop()||{};
        particle.x=x;particle.y=y;particle.z=z;
        particle.vx=(Math.random()-.5)*3;particle.vy=Math.random()*2.8;particle.vz=(Math.random()-.5)*3;
        particle.life=.45+Math.random()*.25;
        particle.r=impactColorScratch.r;particle.g=impactColorScratch.g;particle.b=impactColorScratch.b;
        particles.push(particle);particleColorDirty=true;
      }
    }
    function updateParticles(dt) {
      for(let i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){
        const p=particles[i];p.life-=dt;
        if(p.life<=0){particles[i]=particles[particles.length-1];particles.pop();particlePool.push(p);particleColorDirty=true;continue;}
        p.vy-=7*dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.z+=p.vz*dt;
      }
      // R100: after lifetime compaction is complete, every remaining particle is
      // written exactly once to the identically numbered GPU slot. The former
      // `write` counter was therefore bit-for-bit equal to i throughout this loop
      // and to particleCount afterward. Use those existing values directly to
      // remove a redundant per-particle increment without changing buffer order.
      const particleCount=particles.length;
      // R103: particleColorDirty is global for the entire copy pass and is false on
      // ordinary movement-only frames. Split the rare color rewrite from the normal
      // position-only path so every live particle no longer re-tests the same flag.
      // j advances through the same 0,3,6... GPU slots as i*3, preserving exact
      // particle-to-buffer ordering and Float32 coercion.
      if(particleColorDirty){
        for(let i=0,j=0;i<particleCount;i++,j+=3){
          const p=particles[i];particlePositions[j]=p.x;particlePositions[j+1]=p.y;particlePositions[j+2]=p.z;
          particleColors[j]=p.r;particleColors[j+1]=p.g;particleColors[j+2]=p.b;
        }
      }else{
        for(let i=0,j=0;i<particleCount;i++,j+=3){
          const p=particles[i];particlePositions[j]=p.x;particlePositions[j+1]=p.y;particlePositions[j+2]=p.z;
        }
      }
      if(particleCount!==previousParticleDrawCount)particleGeometry.setDrawRange(0,particleCount);
      if(particleCount>0)particleGeometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
      if(particleCount>0&&particleColorDirty)particleGeometry.attributes.color.needsUpdate=true;
      particleColorDirty=false;
      previousParticleDrawCount=particleCount;
    }
    function hitMarker() {
      crosshairElement.classList.add('hit');setTimeout(()=>crosshairElement.classList.remove('hit'),75);
    }
    const killfeedElement=$('killfeed');
    function addKillFeed(html) {
      const div=document.createElement('div'); div.className='kill'; div.innerHTML=html;
      killfeedElement.prepend(div);
      while(killfeedElement.children.length>5)killfeedElement.lastChild.remove();
      setTimeout(()=>{div.style.opacity='0';setTimeout(()=>div.remove(),250)},4200);
    }

    // Prime browser work that exists only on the player's first fatal frame. This is
    // deliberately state-restoring: it exercises the exact player-ragdoll/camera math
    // plus kill-feed HTML/CSS construction while the match is still covered by UI, but
    // it does not award kills, mutate health/deaths/economy, schedule respawns, or emit
    // any visible feed entry. Subsequent real deaths therefore avoid first-use JIT/style
    // setup without changing gameplay semantics.
    let playerDeathCpuUiPathWarmed=false;
    function warmPlayerDeathCpuUiPath(){
      if(playerDeathCpuUiPathWarmed)return true;
      const savedPlayerDead=player.dead,savedPlayerPos=player.pos.clone();
      const savedState={...playerRagdollState};
      const savedRagdollVisible=playerRagdoll.visible;
      const savedRagdollPosition=playerRagdoll.position.clone();
      const savedRagdollQuaternion=playerRagdoll.quaternion.clone();
      const savedCameraPosition=camera.position.clone(),savedCameraQuaternion=camera.quaternion.clone();
      const savedGunVisible=gunPivot.visible,savedPitch=lastCameraPitch,savedYaw=lastCameraYaw;
      let probe=null;
      try{
        probe=document.createElement('div');probe.className='kill';probe.style.cssText='position:absolute;visibility:hidden;pointer-events:none';
        probe.innerHTML='<span class="enemy">BOT</span> bonked <span class="you">YOU</span> with concentwated meowing';
        killfeedElement.appendChild(probe);void probe.offsetWidth;probe.remove();probe=null;

        player.dead=true;playerRagdollState.active=true;playerRagdollState.age=0;
        playerRagdollState.velX=0;playerRagdollState.velY=0;playerRagdollState.velZ=0;
        playerRagdollState.targetX=Math.PI*.48;playerRagdollState.targetZ=.18;playerRagdollState.deathYaw=player.yaw;
        playerRagdoll.visible=true;playerRagdoll.position.set(player.pos.x,.24,player.pos.z);playerRagdoll.rotation.set(0,player.yaw,0);playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
        gunPivot.visible=false;
        // Several tiny deterministic steps are enough to execute and tier the exact
        // branch while keeping collision displacement at zero.
        for(let i=0;i<8;i++)updateRagdolls(1/120);
        playerDeathCpuUiPathWarmed=true;return true;
      }catch(error){safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] death CPU/UI warmup fallback:',error);return false;}
      finally{
        probe?.remove();player.dead=savedPlayerDead;player.pos.copy(savedPlayerPos);
        Object.assign(playerRagdollState,savedState);
        playerRagdoll.visible=savedRagdollVisible;playerRagdoll.position.copy(savedRagdollPosition);playerRagdoll.quaternion.copy(savedRagdollQuaternion);playerRagdoll.updateMatrix();
        camera.position.copy(savedCameraPosition);camera.quaternion.copy(savedCameraQuaternion);camera.updateMatrixWorld(true);
        gunPivot.visible=savedGunVisible;lastCameraPitch=savedPitch;lastCameraYaw=savedYaw;
      }
    }

    // ---------- UI ----------

    const healthElement=$('health'),healthFillElement=$('healthFill'),ammoElement=$('ammo'),reserveElement=$('reserve'),weaponNameElement=$('weaponName');

    }

    const settingsControls={
      mouse:$('mouseSensitivity'),mouseAds:$('mouseAdsSensitivity'),touch:$('touchSensitivity'),touchAds:$('touchAdsSensitivity'),invertY:$('invertLookY')

    function syncSettingsUI(){
      settingsControls.mouse.value=inputSettings.mouse;settingsControls.mouseAds.value=inputSettings.mouseAds;settingsControls.touch.value=inputSettings.touch;settingsControls.touchAds.value=inputSettings.touchAds;settingsControls.invertY.checked=inputSettings.invertY;
      settingsOutputs.mouse.value=`${inputSettings.mouse.toFixed(2)}×`;settingsOutputs.mouseAds.value=`${inputSettings.mouseAds.toFixed(2)}×`;settingsOutputs.touch.value=`${inputSettings.touch.toFixed(2)}×`;settingsOutputs.touchAds.value=`${inputSettings.touchAds.toFixed(2)}×`;
    }
    function applySettingsControl(key,value){
      if(key==='invertY')inputSettings.invertY=Boolean(value);
      else if(key==='mouse'||key==='touch')inputSettings[key]=clampSetting(value,.2,3,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS[key]);
      else inputSettings[key]=clampSetting(value,.2,1.2,DEFAULT_INPUT_SETTINGS[key]);
      refreshInputSensitivity();persistInputSettings();syncSettingsUI();
    }

    }

    function formatTime(s) {
      s=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(s)); return `${Math.floor(s/60)}:${String(s%60).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    }

    function setPause(value, event=null) {
      if(!started||gameOver)return;
      if(settingsMenu.classList.contains('open'))closeSettings();
      firing=false;setAiming(false);forceClearScope();clearInputKeys();
      if(buyMenuOpen){buyMenuOpen=false;$('buyMenu').classList.remove('open');}
      if(value) {
        if(!paused)playActionSfx('pauseOpen');paused=true;updateAmbientAudio(performance.now()*.001,true);
        document.body.classList.add('game-paused');
        document.body.classList.remove('mobile-controls-active');
        pauseScreen.style.display='grid';staticCanvasDirty=true;wakeAnimationLoop();
        if(activePointerLockElement()) {
          explicitPointerUnlock = true;
          (document.exitPointerLock || document.mozExitPointerLock || document.webkitExitPointerLock).call(document);
        }
      } else if(usingMobileControls()) {
        if(paused)playActionSfx('pauseClose');paused=false;updateAmbientAudio(performance.now()*.001,true);
        document.body.classList.remove('game-paused');
        document.body.classList.add('mobile-controls-active');
        pauseScreen.style.display='none';
        lastTime=performance.now();wakeAnimationLoop();
      } else {
        pauseScreen.style.display='none';
        requestMouseLock(event);
      }
    }
    $('resumeBtn').onclick=(event)=>{

      setPause(false, event);
    };

    function startGame(reset=false,event=null){
      if (!window.CatStrikeReady) { loadingUI?.set(96,'ALMOST READY...','Finishing the last startup checks.'); return; }
      ensureAudio();primeSfxContext();
      warmPlayerDeathCpuUiPath();
      if(audioCtx?.state==='running'){startAudioKeepAlive();warmPlayerDeathAudioPath();}
      else audioCtx?.resume().then(()=>{startAudioKeepAlive();warmImmediateShotPath();warmPlayerDeathAudioPath();}).catch(()=>{});
      matchRecordedSfxBuffers.clear();
      for(const [key,buffer] of recordedSfxBuffers)matchRecordedSfxBuffers.set(key,buffer);
      activeMode=GAME_MODES[selectedModeKey]||GAME_MODES.teamdeathmatch;
      if(activeMode.freeForAll){
        player.team='cats';
        document.body.dataset.playerFaction='ffa';
        document.body.dataset.playerTeam='ffa';
      }else{
        lockPlayerToActiveTeamButton();
      }
      stopReloadAudio();reloading=false;firing=false;setAiming(false);forceClearScope();$('reloadText').textContent='';
      started=true;paused=false;gameOver=false;matchWinner=null;buyMenuOpen=false;$('buyMenu').classList.remove('open');settingsMenu.classList.remove('open');settingsMenu.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');lastCountdownSecond=-1;nextHeartbeatAt=0;startAmbientAudio();
      document.body.classList.add('game-active');
      document.body.classList.remove('game-paused');
      document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-controls-active',usingMobileControls());
      stopPlayerRagdoll();player.kills=0;player.deaths=0;player.money=800;player.gunGameLevel=0;player.gunGameKillsOnLevel=0;player.health=100;player.dead=false;
      teamKills.cats=0;teamKills.meowfia=0;roundScore={cats:0,meowfia:0};roundNumber=1;roundActive=true;roundRestartAt=0;
      playerSurvivedLastRound=false;lastPurchase=null;resetBombState();
      const defaultSidearm=defaultSecondaryForTeam(player.team);
      if(activeMode.gunGame){buildGunGameLadder();loadoutSlots=[GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS[0],defaultSidearm,'knife'];}
      else if(activeMode.buyMode==='economy')loadoutSlots=[null,defaultSidearm,'knife'];
      else loadoutSlots=defaultLoadoutForTeam(player.team);
      syncOwnedWeapons();updateLoadoutPreview(false);for(const w of weapons){w.ammo=w.magSize;w.reserve=Infinity;}
      configureBotsForMode();
      if(activeMode.gunGame)setGunGameLevel(player,0,true);
      else if(activeMode.buyMode==='economy')switchOwnedSlot(SLOT_SECONDARY,true);
      else switchOwnedSlot(SLOT_PRIMARY,true);
      if(activeMode.roundBased)startRound(true);else{
        playActionSfx('matchStart');timeLeft=activeMode.duration;lastTimerText='';buyWindowUntil=activeMode.buyMode==='free'?Infinity:0;respawnPlayer();for(const b of bots)respawnBot(b,true);
        objectiveVisuals.visible=false;$('objectiveInfo').className='';
      }
      while(hitscanTracers.length)hitscanTracerPool.push(hitscanTracers.pop());tracerGeometry.setDrawRange(0,0);previousTracerDrawCount=0;tracerPositionDirtyFrom=Infinity;
      while(particles.length)particlePool.push(particles.pop());particleGeometry.setDrawRange(0,0);previousParticleDrawCount=0;$('killfeed').replaceChildren();
      updateScoreHUD();updateHUD();menu.style.display='none';pauseScreen.style.display='none';resultScreen.style.display='none';lastTime=performance.now();wakeAnimationLoop();
      if(usingMobileControls())paused=false;else requestMouseLock(event);
    }
    $('deployBtn').onclick=event=>startGame(false,event);$('restartBtn').onclick=event=>startGame(true,event);
    $('changeModeBtn').onclick=()=>{resultScreen.style.display='none';menu.style.display='grid';showLauncherPanel('modes');};

    function endGame(){
      if(gameOver)return;gameOver=true;firing=false;stopReloadAudio();stopAmbientAudio();reloading=false;setAiming(false);forceClearScope();buyMenuOpen=false;$('buyMenu').classList.remove('open');objectiveVisuals.visible=false;bombVisual.visible=false;$('objectiveInfo').className='';lockRequestToken++;lockRequestPending=false;
      document.body.classList.remove('game-active','game-paused','mobile-controls-active');
      if(activePointerLockElement()){explicitPointerUnlock=true;(document.exitPointerLock||document.mozExitPointerLock||document.webkitExitPointerLock).call(document);}
      let title='MATCH <span>COMPLETE</span>',text='';
      if(activeMode.roundBased){
        const winner=matchWinner||((roundScore.cats>=roundScore.meowfia)?'cats':'meowfia');const won=winner===player.team;
        title=won?'MATCH <span>VICTORY</span>':'MATCH <span>DEFEAT</span>';
        text=`${activeMode.name}: Counter-Terrorists ${roundScore.cats} — ${roundScore.meowfia} Terrorists. You scored ${player.kills} eliminations and have $${player.money}.`;
      }else if(activeMode.freeForAll||activeMode.gunGame){
        const standings=[player,...activeBots()].sort((a,b)=>((b.gunGameLevel||0)-(a.gunGameLevel||0))||((b.gunGameKillsOnLevel||0)-(a.gunGameKillsOnLevel||0))||((b.kills||0)-(a.kills||0)));const winner=matchWinner||standings[0];const won=winner===player;
        title=won?'YOU <span>WIN</span>':'OPPONENT <span>WON</span>';playActionSfx(won?'roundWin':'roundLoss');
        text=`${activeMode.name}: ${winner===player?'YOU':winner.name} wins. You finished with ${player.kills} eliminations and ${player.deaths} deaths${activeMode.gunGame?` at level ${player.gunGameLevel+1}/${GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.length}`:''}.`;
      }else{
        const ownScore=teamKills[player.team],enemyScore=teamKills[player.team==='cats'?'meowfia':'cats'];const won=ownScore>enemyScore,draw=ownScore===enemyScore;
        title=draw?'MATCH <span>DRAW</span>':won?'MATCH <span>VICTORY</span>':'MATCH <span>DEFEAT</span>';playActionSfx(draw?'roundDraw':won?'roundWin':'roundLoss');
        text=`Counter-Terrorists ${teamKills.cats} — ${teamKills.meowfia} Terrorists. You scored ${player.kills} eliminations with ${player.deaths} deaths.`;
      }
      $('resultTitle').innerHTML=title;$('resultText').textContent=text;resultScreen.style.display='grid';staticCanvasDirty=true;wakeAnimationLoop();
    }

    // ---------- Update ----------
    // R66: movement basis depends only on player yaw. Cache the exact sine/cosine
    // scalars instead of recomputing them twice each and writing two temporary
    // Vector3s every live frame. Object.is preserves signed-zero and NaN identity.
    let playerMoveBasisYaw=NaN,playerMoveSinYaw=NaN,playerMoveCosYaw=NaN;
    const FOOTSTEP_KEYS=['footstep1','footstep2','footstep3','footstep4','footstep5','footstep6'];
    let playerStepDistance=0,playerStepIndex=0,lastPlayerStepAt=-999;
    let botStepWindowStart=0,botStepsThisWindow=0,previousCrouched=false,nextHeartbeatAt=0,lastCountdownSecond=-1;
    function playFootstep(volumeScale=.72,pan=0){
      const key=FOOTSTEP_KEYS[playerStepIndex++%FOOTSTEP_KEYS.length];
      if(playActionSfx(key,{volumeScale,pan}))return;
      // Still use a real recorded organic click as the offline footstep fallback.
      if(playActionSfx('uiClick',{volumeScale:volumeScale*.62,pan,rate:.62+Math.random()*.08,duration:.11}))return;
      noiseBurst(.035,.025,720,'lowpass',.65);
    }
    function resetFootsteps(){playerStepDistance=0;lastPlayerStepAt=-999;}
    function maybePlayBotFootstep(bot,movedDistance,detailD2,now){
      if(movedDistance<.001||detailD2>784)return;
      bot.stepDistance=(bot.stepDistance||0)+movedDistance;
      const stride=1.45;
      if(bot.stepDistance<stride||now-(bot.lastFootstepAt||-999)<.16)return;
      if(now-botStepWindowStart>=1){botStepWindowStart=now;botStepsThisWindow=0;}
      if(botStepsThisWindow>=8)return;
      bot.stepDistance%=stride;bot.lastFootstepAt=now;botStepsThisWindow++;
      const distance=Math.sqrt(detailD2),rightX=Math.cos(player.yaw),rightZ=-Math.sin(player.yaw);
      const side=bot.pos.x-player.pos.x,forward=bot.pos.z-player.pos.z;
      const pan=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((side*rightX+forward*rightZ)/Math.max(1,distance),-.92,.92);
      const volume=.48*THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1-distance/28,.08,1);
      playActionSfx(FOOTSTEP_KEYS[(bot.spawnSlot+(bot.team==='cats'?0:3)+Math.floor(now*3))%FOOTSTEP_KEYS.length],{volumeScale:volume,pan,rate:.94+Math.random()*.12});
    }

    function smoothStep01(t){return t*t*(3-2*t);}

    function updatePlayer(dt,now) {
      applyCursorSteer(dt);
      if(player.dead){
        if(activeMode.respawn&&now>=respawnAt)respawnPlayer();
        return;
      }
      if(buyMenuOpen){camera.position.copy(player.pos);return;}

      let ix=inputMoveX, iz=inputMoveZ;
      ix+=moveStick.x; iz+=moveStick.y;
      let mag;if(ix===0)mag=Math.abs(iz);else if(iz===0)mag=Math.abs(ix);else mag=Math.hypot(ix,iz);if(mag>1){ix/=mag;iz/=mag;}
      if(respawnProtectionArmed){if(now>=player.invulnerableUntil)respawnProtectionArmed=false;else if(mag>.05)cancelRespawnProtection();}
      player.crouched=inputCrouch;
      if(player.crouched!==previousCrouched){previousCrouched=player.crouched;playActionSfx('clothMove',{volumeScale:player.crouched?.80:.64,rate:player.crouched?.92:1.08});}
      const walking=inputWalking;
      const maxSpeed=player.crouched?2.25:walking?3.25:5.6;
      if(!Object.is(playerMoveBasisYaw,player.yaw)){
        playerMoveBasisYaw=player.yaw;playerMoveSinYaw=Math.sin(player.yaw);playerMoveCosYaw=Math.cos(player.yaw);
      }
      const targetVX=(playerMoveCosYaw*ix+(-playerMoveSinYaw)*(-iz))*maxSpeed;
      const targetVZ=((-playerMoveSinYaw)*ix+(-playerMoveCosYaw)*(-iz))*maxSpeed;
      const inputActive=mag>.05;
      const opposing=targetVX*player.velX+targetVZ*player.velZ<0;
      const response=inputActive?(opposing?34:18):12;
      const blend=1-Math.exp(-response*dt);
      player.velX=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(player.velX,targetVX,blend);
      player.velZ=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(player.velZ,targetVZ,blend);
      const oldX=player.pos.x,oldZ=player.pos.z;
      moveWithCollision(player.pos,player.velX*dt,player.velZ*dt,player.radius);
      if(Math.abs(player.pos.x-oldX)<Math.abs(player.velX*dt)*.15)player.velX*=.18;
      if(Math.abs(player.pos.z-oldZ)<Math.abs(player.velZ*dt)*.15)player.velZ*=.18;
      player.moveSpeed=Math.hypot(player.velX,player.velZ);
      const moving=player.moveSpeed>.08;

      const wasGrounded=player.onGround,fallVelocity=player.velY;
      player.velY-=16.5*dt;
      player.pos.y+=player.velY*dt;
      if(player.pos.y<=1.65){
        player.pos.y=1.65;player.velY=0;player.onGround=true;
        if(!wasGrounded&&fallVelocity<-.9)playActionSfx('land',{volumeScale:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(fallVelocity)/8,.35,1)});
      }
      if(moving&&player.onGround){
        const movedDistance=Math.hypot(player.pos.x-oldX,player.pos.z-oldZ);
        if(movedDistance>0){
        playerStepDistance+=movedDistance;
        const stride=player.crouched?1.05:walking?1.28:1.58;
        if(playerStepDistance>=stride&&now-lastPlayerStepAt>.11){
          playerStepDistance%=stride;lastPlayerStepAt=now;
          playFootstep(player.crouched?.32:walking?.50:.76,0);
        }
        }
      }else playerStepDistance=Math.min(playerStepDistance,.18);

      if(player.health<=25&&now>=nextHeartbeatAt){
        const urgency=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((25-player.health)/25,0,1);playActionSfx('lowHealthBeat',{volumeScale:.62+urgency*.34,rate:.94+urgency*.12});nextHeartbeatAt=now+THREE.MathUtils.lerp(1.05,.58,urgency);
      }else if(player.health>25)nextHeartbeatAt=0;
      if(now-lastShot>.095){accuracyPenalty=Math.max(0,accuracyPenalty-currentWeapon.recover*dt);}
      if(now-lastShot>.38)shotsFired=0;
      currentInaccuracy=calculateInaccuracy(currentWeapon);

      player.bob += moving && player.onGround ? dt*(walking||player.crouched?6.5:9.5) : dt*4;
      const bob=moving&&player.onGround?Math.sin(player.bob)*((walking||player.crouched)?.018:.033):0;
      const crouchOffset=player.crouched?-.34:0;
      camera.position.set(player.pos.x,player.pos.y+bob+crouchOffset,player.pos.z);
      const cameraPitchChanged=player.pitch!==lastCameraPitch||(player.pitch===0&&1/player.pitch!==1/lastCameraPitch);
      const cameraYawChanged=player.yaw!==lastCameraYaw||(player.yaw===0&&1/player.yaw!==1/lastCameraYaw);
      if(cameraPitchChanged||cameraYawChanged){
        camera.rotation.set(player.pitch,player.yaw,0,'YXZ');
        lastCameraPitch=player.pitch;lastCameraYaw=player.yaw;
      }

      // Staged scope transition: zoom first, fade the cat away, then reveal the scope.
      const adsTarget=aiming?1:0;adsProgress=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(adsProgress,adsTarget,Math.min(1,dt*(aiming?5.8:8.5)));
      if(Math.abs(adsProgress-adsTarget)<.002)adsProgress=adsTarget;
      let gunFade,scopeFade;
      if(adsProgress===0){gunFade=1;scopeFade=0;}
      else if(adsProgress===1){gunFade=0;scopeFade=1;}
      else{gunFade=1-smoothStep01(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(adsProgress/.58,0,1));scopeFade=smoothStep01(THREE.MathUtils.clamp((adsProgress-.36)/.64,0,1));}
      setCurrentGunOpacity(gunFade);
      const scopeOpacity=adsProgress===0?'0.000':adsProgress===1?'1.000':scopeFade.toFixed(3);
      if(scopeOverlayElement&&scopeOpacity!==lastScopeOpacity){scopeOverlayElement.style.opacity=scopeOpacity;lastScopeOpacity=scopeOpacity;}
      const scopeSniper=!!currentWeapon.sniper;
      if(scopeOverlayElement&&scopeSniper!==lastScopeSniper){scopeOverlayElement.classList.toggle('sniper',scopeSniper);lastScopeSniper=scopeSniper;}
      const crosshairOpacity=adsProgress===0?'1':adsProgress===1?'0.020000000000000018':String(1-scopeFade*.98);
      if(crosshairElement&&crosshairOpacity!==lastCrosshairOpacity){crosshairElement.style.opacity=crosshairOpacity;lastCrosshairOpacity=crosshairOpacity;}
      const crosshairScale=1+Math.min(2.2,currentInaccuracy*38);
      if(crosshairScale!==lastCrosshairScale){
        const crosshairTransform=`translate(-50%,-50%) scale(${crosshairScale.toFixed(3)})`;
        if(crosshairElement&&crosshairTransform!==lastCrosshairTransform){crosshairElement.style.transform=crosshairTransform;lastCrosshairTransform=crosshairTransform;}
        lastCrosshairScale=crosshairScale;
      }
      const targetX=.55+Math.sin(player.bob*.5)*(.012*(1-adsProgress));
      const targetY=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(-.48,-.92,adsProgress)+Math.abs(Math.cos(player.bob))*(moving?.018*(1-adsProgress):0);
      const targetZ=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(-1.25,-1.05,adsProgress);const targetGunScale=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(1,.40,adsProgress);
      gunPivot.position.x=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.position.x,targetX,Math.min(1,dt*12));
      gunPivot.position.y=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.position.y,targetY,Math.min(1,dt*12));
      gunPivot.position.z=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.position.z,targetZ,Math.min(1,dt*14));
      const scale=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.scale.x,targetGunScale,Math.min(1,dt*14));gunPivot.scale.setScalar(scale);
      // R120: setting Euler x/y/z separately invokes Three.js' internal Euler→quaternion
      // synchronization callback after each component even though nothing consumes the
      // two intermediate orientations. Compute the same three lerps from the same old
      // components, then commit the identical final Euler once so each frame performs
      // at most one quaternion synchronization instead of three.
      const nextGunRotX=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.rotation.x,-.04*adsProgress,dt*14);
      const nextGunRotY=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.rotation.y,-.08*adsProgress,dt*14);
      const nextGunRotZ=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(gunPivot.rotation.z,-.025*adsProgress,dt*10);
      if(!Object.is(nextGunRotX,gunPivot.rotation.x)||!Object.is(nextGunRotY,gunPivot.rotation.y)||!Object.is(nextGunRotZ,gunPivot.rotation.z))gunPivot.rotation.set(nextGunRotX,nextGunRotY,nextGunRotZ);
      if(muzzleFlash.material.opacity>0)muzzleFlash.material.opacity=Math.max(0,muzzleFlash.material.opacity-dt*12);
      if(muzzleLight.intensity>0)muzzleLight.intensity=Math.max(0,muzzleLight.intensity-dt*38);
      const baseFov=72;const targetFov=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(baseFov,currentWeapon.zoomFov||52,smoothStep01(adsProgress));
      const nextFov=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(camera.fov,targetFov,Math.min(1,dt*9));if(Math.abs(nextFov-camera.fov)>.015){camera.fov=nextFov;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();}

      if(firing)fireWeapon(now);
      if(reloading&&now>=reloadEnd)finishReload();
    }

    function insertOpponentCandidate(entities,distances,count,candidate,distance){
      const limit=4;
      if(count===limit&&distance>=distances[limit-1])return count;
      let insert=count<limit?count:limit-1;
      while(insert>0&&distances[insert-1]>distance){
        if(insert<limit){entities[insert]=entities[insert-1];distances[insert]=distances[insert-1];}
        insert--;
      }
      entities[insert]=candidate;distances[insert]=distance;
      return count<limit?count+1:count;
    }
    function collectOpponentCandidates(bot){
      // findVisibleOpponent() consumes at most the four nearest candidates. Keep only
      // that stable sorted prefix while scanning instead of fully sorting every nearby
      // opponent. Equal-distance candidates retain encounter order exactly as before.
      const entities=bot._candidateEntities||(bot._candidateEntities=new Array(4));
      const distances=bot._candidateDistances||(bot._candidateDistances=new Float64Array(4));
      let count=0,entity,dx,dz,d2;

      entity=player;
      // R97: player is a permanent concrete entity. isAliveEntity(player) is exactly
      // !player.dead, so avoid a helper call on every sensing scan while retaining
      // the live death/hostility/self predicates and their original evaluation order.
      if(!entity.dead&&entity!==bot&&isHostile(bot,entity)){
        dx=entity.pos.x-bot.pos.x;dz=entity.pos.z-bot.pos.z;d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
        if(d2<=10000)count=insertOpponentCandidate(entities,distances,count,entity,d2);
      }
      // Team modes only need the opposite team's bots. teamRingBots preserves the
      // original bots[] encounter order, so equal-distance candidate ordering and
      // every downstream visibility/target-selection result stay unchanged.
      // R87: configuredActiveBots preserves bots[] encounter order exactly and
      // inactive reserve bots are forced dead/hidden by configureBotsForMode() and
      // respawnBot(). FFA sensing can therefore scan the canonical active subset
      // instead of revisiting reserve slots that the original loop immediately
      // rejected via isAliveEntity(). Team-mode candidate selection is unchanged.
      if(activeMode.freeForAll){
        // R88: configuredActiveBots preserves bots[] order and contains the sensing
        // bot itself, so FFA still performs the original self/dead rejection. Every
        // other configured living bot is hostile by definition in FFA; avoid the
        // redundant isHostile() call after the self check.
        const botCandidates=configuredActiveBots;
        for(let i=0;i<botCandidates.length;i++){
          entity=botCandidates[i];
          // configuredActiveBots contains concrete bot objects only. Keep the live
          // dead/self checks but read dead directly instead of routing through the
          // generic nullable-entity helper once per candidate.
          if(entity.dead||entity===bot)continue;
          dx=entity.pos.x-bot.pos.x;dz=entity.pos.z-bot.pos.z;d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
          if(d2<=10000)count=insertOpponentCandidate(entities,distances,count,entity,d2);
        }
      }else{
        // Team caches contain only configured bots on the immutable opposite team.
        // The sensing bot cannot occur in this array and every living member is
        // hostile, so preserve encounter order while removing two impossible tests.
        const botCandidates=configuredActiveTeamBots[bot.team==='cats'?'meowfia':'cats'];
        for(let i=0;i<botCandidates.length;i++){
          entity=botCandidates[i];
          // Opposite-team cache entries are likewise guaranteed concrete objects.
          if(entity.dead)continue;
          dx=entity.pos.x-bot.pos.x;dz=entity.pos.z-bot.pos.z;d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
          if(d2<=10000)count=insertOpponentCandidate(entities,distances,count,entity,d2);
        }
      }
      return count;
    }
    function findVisibleOpponent(bot,currentTargetKnownAliveHostile=false){
      // Keep a current visible target if possible to avoid robotic target flicker.
      // R105: updateBots() has already evaluated the exact current-target life/
      // hostility predicate before entering a sensing pass. Reuse that result on
      // scheduled senses instead of immediately repeating two pure helper checks.
      // Other callers retain the original full validation by using the default.
      const currentTarget=bot.target;
      if(currentTarget&&(currentTargetKnownAliveHostile||(isAliveEntity(currentTarget)&&isHostile(bot,currentTarget)))&&botCanSeeTarget(bot,currentTarget,true))return currentTarget;
      const candidateCount=collectOpponentCandidates(bot),entities=bot._candidateEntities;
      // At most four expensive 3D visibility rays per sense tick. This remains
      // stable in 10v10 while still finding exposed nearby opponents.
      // R113: if the valid current target reached this point, its synchronous
      // visibility test immediately above already returned false. Candidate
      // collection can include that same entity again among the nearest four, but
      // no bot/camera/collider state changes between these two loops. Skip only that
      // proven-false duplicate when the caller already validated it; default/helper
      // semantics still retest a same-identity candidate if no validation flag exists.
      for(let i=0,limit=Math.min(4,candidateCount);i<limit;i++){
        const candidate=entities[i];
        if((candidate!==currentTarget||!currentTargetKnownAliveHostile)&&botCanSeeTarget(bot,candidate,true))return candidate;
      }
      return null;
    }

    let nextBotSteerUpdate=0;
    function updateBots(dt,now) {
      if(activeMode.roundBased&&!roundActive)return;
      const recomputeSteering=now>=nextBotSteerUpdate;
      if(recomputeSteering){
        // R77: CatStrike has exactly ten fixed bot slots (five per team), so the
        // previous activeCount > 12 branch was unreachable. Avoid rescanning all
        // configured bots every steering tick for a condition that can never change
        // the result; the exact existing cadence is always 30 Hz.
        nextBotSteerUpdate=now+1/30;
      }
      // R98: use the stable configured-active index for the outer loop so the
      // crowd-separation pass can structurally skip the current bot without
      // re-testing other===bot for every pair. configuredActiveBots preserves bots[]
      // order, and the two inner ranges below visit all non-self entries in the
      // exact same encounter order as the former full loop.
      // R104: activeMode cannot change during this synchronous bot pass. Cache the
      // FFA flag once, then inline the exact target-life/hostility predicate used by
      // sensing. This removes two helper calls and their repeated null/self/mode
      // checks from the 30 Hz per-bot steering path without changing eligibility.
      const botFrameFreeForAll=activeMode.freeForAll;
      for(let botIndex=0,activeBotCount=configuredActiveBots.length;botIndex<activeBotCount;botIndex++){
        const bot=configuredActiveBots[botIndex];
        if(bot.dead){if(activeMode.respawn&&now>=bot.respawn)respawnBot(bot);continue;}
        if(recomputeSteering){
          const sensedTarget=bot.target;
          // R105: compute current-target eligibility once and carry it into
          // findVisibleOpponent(). The former condition and helper both evaluated
          // the same pure life/hostility facts on ordinary scheduled sense ticks.
          const sensedTargetValid=Boolean(sensedTarget&&!sensedTarget.dead&&sensedTarget!==bot&&(botFrameFreeForAll||bot.team!==sensedTarget.team));
          if(now>=bot.nextSense||!sensedTargetValid){
            const previousTarget=bot.target,wasVisible=bot.los;
            const visibleTarget=findVisibleOpponent(bot,sensedTargetValid);
            bot.nextSense=now+.09+Math.random()*.055;
            if(visibleTarget){
              bot.target=visibleTarget;bot.los=true;
              bot.lastSeenAt=now;bot.lastSeenX=visibleTarget.pos.x;bot.lastSeenZ=visibleTarget.pos.z;
              if(visibleTarget!==previousTarget||!wasVisible){
                const w=bot.weaponDef||getBotWeapon(bot),profile=botWeaponProfileFor(bot,w);
                const ddx=visibleTarget.pos.x-bot.pos.x,ddz=visibleTarget.pos.z-bot.pos.z;
                const distanceDelay=ddx===ddx&&ddz===ddz&&(Math.abs(ddx)>=28||Math.abs(ddz)>=28)?.28:THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.hypot(ddx,ddz)/100,0,.28);
                bot.reactionUntil=now+profile.reactionMin+Math.random()*(profile.reactionMax-profile.reactionMin)+distanceDelay;
                bot.visibleSince=now;bot.burstShots=0;bot.burstPauseUntil=bot.reactionUntil;
                bot.aimBiasRefresh=0;
              }
            }else{
              if(wasVisible)bot.lastSeenAt=now;
              bot.los=false;
              if(!bot.target||bot.target.dead||now-bot.lastSeenAt>1.35)bot.target=null;
            }
          }
          const target=bot.target,objectivePoint=getBotObjectivePoint(bot);let mx=0,mz=0;
          if(target){
            // Once visibility is lost, navigate only to the remembered last-seen
            // point. Never rotate toward or chase the hidden target's live position.
            const tx=bot.los?target.pos.x:bot.lastSeenX,tz=bot.los?target.pos.z:bot.lastSeenZ;
            const dx=tx-bot.pos.x,dz=tz-bot.pos.z,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||.0001;
            bot.dist=dist;bot.dirX=dx/dist;bot.dirZ=dz/dist;
            const profile=botWeaponProfileFor(bot);
            const preferred=profile.preferred;
            if(!bot.los||dist>preferred+3){mx+=bot.dirX;mz+=bot.dirZ;}
            if(bot.los&&dist<Math.max(2,preferred-4)){mx-=bot.dirX*.65;mz-=bot.dirZ*.65;}
            if(bot.los){mx+=-bot.dirZ*bot.strafe*.58;mz+=bot.dirX*bot.strafe*.58;botShoot(bot,now,target);}
            else if(dist<1.25&&now-bot.lastSeenAt>1.1)bot.target=null;
          }else{bot.dirX=bot.team==='cats'?1:-1;bot.dirZ=0;mx=bot.dirX;}
          if(objectivePoint){
            const odx=objectivePoint.x-bot.pos.x,odz=objectivePoint.z-bot.pos.z,od=Math.hypot(odx,odz)||.0001;
            if(od>2.05){const weight=(!target||!bot.los||bot.team==='cats'&&bombState.planted)?1.65:.72;mx+=odx/od*weight;mz+=odz/od*weight;if(!target||!bot.los){bot.dirX=odx/od;bot.dirZ=odz/od;}}
          }
          if(now>bot.nextThink){bot.nextThink=now+1.1+Math.random()*1.4;if(Math.random()<.35)bot.strafe*=-1;}
          // The self entry formerly continued without touching mx/mz. Split at
          // botIndex to remove that always-known equality branch while retaining
          // every preceding and following bot in identical order.
          for(let otherIndex=0;otherIndex<botIndex;otherIndex++){const other=configuredActiveBots[otherIndex];if(other.dead)continue;const sx=bot.pos.x-other.pos.x,sz=bot.pos.z-other.pos.z,d2=sx*sx+sz*sz;if(d2>.0001&&d2<2.25){const inv=1/Math.sqrt(d2);mx+=sx*inv*.85;mz+=sz*inv*.85;}}
          for(let otherIndex=botIndex+1;otherIndex<activeBotCount;otherIndex++){const other=configuredActiveBots[otherIndex];if(other.dead)continue;const sx=bot.pos.x-other.pos.x,sz=bot.pos.z-other.pos.z,d2=sx*sx+sz*sz;if(d2>.0001&&d2<2.25){const inv=1/Math.sqrt(d2);mx+=sx*inv*.85;mz+=sz*inv*.85;}}
          if(!player.dead){const sx=bot.pos.x-player.pos.x,sz=bot.pos.z-player.pos.z,d2=sx*sx+sz*sz;if(d2>.0001&&d2<2.25){const inv=1/Math.sqrt(d2);mx+=sx*inv*.9;mz+=sz*inv*.9;}}
          let ml;if(mx===0)ml=Math.abs(mz);else if(mz===0)ml=Math.abs(mx);else ml=Math.hypot(mx,mz);if(ml>.01){mx/=ml;mz/=ml;}
          if(collideCircleXZ(bot.pos.x+mx*.8,bot.pos.z+mz*.8,bot.radius)){const oldX=mx;mx=-mz*bot.strafe;mz=oldX*bot.strafe;}
          bot.moveX=mx;bot.moveZ=mz;bot._moveMagnitude=ml>.01?1:ml;
        }
        const botStepOldX=bot.pos.x,botStepOldZ=bot.pos.z;
        moveWithCollision(bot.pos,bot.moveX*bot.speed*dt,bot.moveZ*bot.speed*dt,bot.radius);
        bot.root.position.set(bot.pos.x,0,bot.pos.z);
        // Bot facing only changes on steering ticks. Re-applying the same Euler Y every
        // render frame needlessly recomputes Three.js' quaternion; respawns mark the
        // facing dirty so the original next-live-frame overwrite timing is preserved.
        if(recomputeSteering||bot._facingDirty){bot.root.rotation.y=Math.atan2(bot.dirX,bot.dirZ);bot._facingDirty=false;}
        const detailDx=bot.pos.x-player.pos.x,detailDz=bot.pos.z-player.pos.z,detailD2=detailDx*detailDx+detailDz*detailDz;
        // Bot movement distance is consumed only by nearby footstep audio. The
        // footstep path rejects detailD2 > 784 before reading movedDistance, so
        // avoid an otherwise-dead sqrt/hypot for distant bots while preserving
        // the exact nearby value and every audio eligibility/timing decision.
        const botMovedDistance=detailD2<=784?Math.hypot(bot.pos.x-botStepOldX,bot.pos.z-botStepOldZ):0;
        maybePlayBotFootstep(bot,botMovedDistance,detailD2,now);
        const bounce=Math.abs(Math.sin(now*6+bot.pos.x))*.018;
        bot.cat.position.y=bounce;
        // Setting an unchanged Euler component still runs Three.js' Euler→quaternion
        // synchronization callback. Most bot weapons sit exactly at the settled
        // recoil angle, so avoid that callback only when the original lerp result
        // is bit-identical to the current X rotation. Recoil trajectories and every
        // changed value remain exactly the same.
        const currentWeaponRecoilX=bot.weapon.rotation.x;
        const nextWeaponRecoilX=THREE.MathUtils.lerp(currentWeaponRecoilX,-.4,dt*8);
        if(!Object.is(nextWeaponRecoilX,currentWeaponRecoilX))bot.weapon.rotation.x=nextWeaponRecoilX;

        // R117: the removed rig/LOD pipeline can no longer supply a precomputed
        // frustum boolean. Omit the obsolete third argument entirely; JavaScript
        // supplies the same undefined value, so the cached fallback path is identical.
        {
          const teammate = !activeMode.freeForAll && bot.team === player.team;
          // Teammate tags are intentionally always visible, so never run the
          // expensive enemy occlusion/frustum query when its result cannot affect
          // the tag. Enemy/FFA visibility remains byte-for-byte the same path.
          const enemyTagVisible = !teammate && detailD2 < 900 && playerCanSeeBotForOutline(bot, now);
          bot._nameTagVisible = teammate || enemyTagVisible;
          bot._nameTagTeammate = teammate;
          // Keep distant teammate labels legible without letting them become huge.
          // Preserve the exact original clamp curve while avoiding a square root
          // whenever the result is already pinned to either clamp endpoint.
          // The transition band still uses the original sqrt expression unchanged.
          bot._nameTagScale = teammate
            ? detailD2 <= 28*28 ? 1
              : detailD2 >= (28*2.15)**2 ? 2.15
              : Math.sqrt(detailD2) / 28
            : 1;
        }
        if(bot.signature)bot.signature.visible=detailD2<625;
        if(bot.teamRing)bot.teamRing.visible=detailD2<1600;
      }
      updateBatchedBotWeapons();
      updateBatchedTeamRings();
      updateBatchedNameTags(now);
    }

    // Hitscan tracers are updated by updateProjectiles() above.

    // ---------- Minimap ----------
    const mapCanvas=$('minimap'), ctx=mapCanvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});
    const mapStatic=document.createElement('canvas');mapStatic.width=mapCanvas.width;mapStatic.height=mapCanvas.height;
    const staticCtx=mapStatic.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});
    const minimapWidth=mapCanvas.width,minimapHeight=mapCanvas.height,minimapScale=minimapWidth/MAP_RENDER_SIZE,minimapHalfWidth=minimapWidth*.5,minimapHalfHeight=minimapHeight*.5;
    function buildStaticMinimap(){
      const w=minimapWidth,h=minimapHeight,s=minimapScale;
      staticCtx.fillStyle='rgb(10,12,18)';staticCtx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      staticCtx.save();staticCtx.translate(w/2,h/2);staticCtx.fillStyle='rgb(49,51,57)';
      for(const c of colliders)staticCtx.fillRect(c.minX*s,c.minZ*s,(c.maxX-c.minX)*s,(c.maxZ-c.minZ)*s);
      staticCtx.restore();
    }
    buildStaticMinimap();
    let nextMinimapDraw=0;
    function drawMinimap(now){
      if(now<nextMinimapDraw)return;nextMinimapDraw=now+.1;
      const s=minimapScale;
      ctx.drawImage(mapStatic,0,0);
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(minimapHalfWidth,minimapHalfHeight);
      ctx.fillStyle='#76bdff';ctx.beginPath();let allyCount=0;
      if(!activeMode.freeForAll){
        const allies=configuredActiveTeamBots[player.team];
        for(let i=0;i<allies.length;i++){const bot=allies[i];if(!bot.dead){ctx.moveTo(bot.pos.x*s+4,bot.pos.z*s);ctx.arc(bot.pos.x*s,bot.pos.z*s,4,0,Math.PI*2);allyCount++;}}
      }
      if(allyCount)ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff7272';ctx.beginPath();let enemyCount=0;
      if(activeMode.freeForAll){
        // R82: configuredActiveBots is the same bots[] order with only inactive
        // reserve slots removed. FFA minimap rendering already rejects every inactive
        // bot, so traversing the canonical active list preserves every drawn marker
        // while avoiding dead work on reserve slots at the 10 Hz minimap cadence.
        for(let i=0;i<configuredActiveBots.length;i++){const bot=configuredActiveBots[i];if(!bot.dead){ctx.moveTo(bot.pos.x*s+4,bot.pos.z*s);ctx.arc(bot.pos.x*s,bot.pos.z*s,4,0,Math.PI*2);enemyCount++;}}
      }else{
        const enemies=configuredActiveTeamBots[player.team==='cats'?'meowfia':'cats'];
        for(let i=0;i<enemies.length;i++){const bot=enemies[i];if(!bot.dead){ctx.moveTo(bot.pos.x*s+4,bot.pos.z*s);ctx.arc(bot.pos.x*s,bot.pos.z*s,4,0,Math.PI*2);enemyCount++;}}
      }
      if(enemyCount)ctx.fill();
      if(bombState.enabled){
        for(const label of activeMode.bombSites||[]){const site=BOMB_SITES[label];ctx.strokeStyle=label==='A'?'#ffb44c':'#58bfff';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(site.x*s,site.z*s,site.radius*s,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();}
        if(bombVisual.visible){ctx.fillStyle=bombState.planted?'#ff4242':'#ffd45e';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bombVisual.position.x*s,bombVisual.position.z*s,5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
      }
      ctx.translate(player.pos.x*s,player.pos.z*s);ctx.rotate(-player.yaw);
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffd45e';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-8);ctx.lineTo(6,6);ctx.lineTo(-6,6);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
    }


    // Legacy projected-capsule outline removed. Exact skinned silhouette pass is defined above.

    let perfFrames=0,perfWindowStart=performance.now(),lastTimerText='',lastTimerSecond=NaN;
    let performancePressure=lowSpec?1:0,performanceRecoveryWindows=0;
    function applyPerformancePressure(level){
      level=Math.max(0,Math.min(3,level|0));
      performancePressure=level;
      const renderTargets=lowSpec?[.88,.80,.70,.60]:[1,.92,.82,.70];
      const outlineScales=lowSpec?[.48,.43,.37,.32]:[.66,.59,.51,.43];
      const outlineRates=lowSpec?[24,20,16,12]:[40,34,28,22];
      const nextRender=Math.max(MIN_RENDER_SCALE,renderTargets[level]);
      const nextOutline=outlineScales[level];
      const outlineChanged=Math.abs(modelOutlineScale-nextOutline)>.001;
      renderScale=nextRender;modelOutlineScale=nextOutline;modelOutlineHz=outlineRates[level];
      applyRenderScale();
      // Outlines are disabled in the current CatStrike visual profile. Do not
      // allocate/resize their offscreen target while adapting main render scale.
      if(ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES&&outlineChanged)resizeExactModelOutline();
    }
    applyPerformancePressure(performancePressure);
    function monitorPerformance(t){
      if(!started||paused||gameOver)return;
      // The third-person death camera intentionally sees more of the map than the
      // normal first-person view. Do not let that temporary 2.5 s view downgrade
      // renderScale: changing pixel ratio reallocates the drawing buffer and can
      // turn a single death-view spike into a much larger one-time hitch. Start a
      // fresh sample after respawn instead, so quality decisions describe gameplay.
      if(player.dead){perfFrames=0;perfWindowStart=t;return;}
      perfFrames++;
      const elapsed=t-perfWindowStart;
      if(elapsed<1800)return;
      const fps=perfFrames*1000/elapsed;
      const requested=fps<28?3:fps<40?2:fps<50?1:0;
      if(requested>performancePressure){
        performanceRecoveryWindows=0;applyPerformancePressure(requested);
      }else if(requested<performancePressure){
        // Require several consistently smooth windows before restoring cost.
        performanceRecoveryWindows++;
        if(performanceRecoveryWindows>=3){performanceRecoveryWindows=0;applyPerformancePressure(performancePressure-1);}
      }else performanceRecoveryWindows=0;
      perfFrames=0;perfWindowStart=t;
    }

    let staticCanvasDirty = true;
    function animate(t) {
      // Final safety net: a few browsers/embed shells can lock the cursor but
      // delay or suppress pointerlockchange. Never allow that state to freeze play.
      // R84: paused is the gate for this pointer-lock recovery path. Check it before
      // the mobile-capability and document pointer-lock reads so ordinary live play
      // does not perform those otherwise-dead checks every animation frame. All
      // predicates are side-effect free, so the recovery condition/result is identical.
      if(started&&!gameOver&&paused&&!usingMobileControls()&&!explicitPointerUnlock&&activePointerLockElement()) enterGameplay();
      const rawDt=Math.min(.05,(t-lastTime)/1000);lastTime=t;
      const liveGameplay = started&&!paused&&!gameOver;
      if(liveGameplay){
        const now=t/1000;
        if(activeMode.roundBased){
          if(roundActive){updateBombObjective(rawDt,now);timeLeft-=rawDt;if(timeLeft<=0)resolveRoundTimeout();}
          else if(roundRestartAt&&now>=roundRestartAt){roundRestartAt=0;startRound(false);}
        }else{timeLeft-=rawDt;if(timeLeft<=0)endGame();}
        const countdownSecond=Math.ceil(timeLeft);
        if(!bombState.planted&&countdownSecond>0&&countdownSecond<=10&&countdownSecond!==lastCountdownSecond){lastCountdownSecond=countdownSecond;playActionSfx('countdownTick',{volumeScale:countdownSecond<=3?.92:.58,rate:countdownSecond<=3?1.18:1});}
        else if(countdownSecond>10)lastCountdownSecond=-1;
        if(countdownSecond!==lastTimerSecond||lastTimerText===''){const timerText=formatTime(timeLeft);if(timerText!==lastTimerText){$('timer').textContent=timerText;lastTimerText=timerText;}lastTimerSecond=countdownSecond;}
        updateRagdolls(rawDt);updatePlayer(rawDt,now);updateBots(rawDt,now);
        // R107: tracer membership can change only when a shot appends a tracer,
        // updateProjectiles() compacts/expires live tracers, or match reset clears
        // the draw range and bookkeeping directly. Once the array is already empty,
        // the prior live update has already set drawRange=0, previousTracerDrawCount=0
        // and tracerPositionDirtyFrom=Infinity, so another empty update is a pure no-op.
        if(hitscanTracers.length)updateProjectiles(rawDt);
        // R106: particle membership can change only in impactBurst(), updateParticles(),
        // or the match reset path. updateParticles() itself performs the final draw-range
        // clear on the frame the last live particle expires, and reset clears it directly.
        // Therefore an already-empty array has no simulation, buffer, or cleanup work left;
        // skip the entire function call on those common between-impact frames.
        if(particles.length)updateParticles(rawDt);
        updateEnvironment(rawDt,now);updateAmbientAudio(now);drawMinimap(now);monitorPerformance(t);
      }
      // Menu, pause, and result screens cover a static world. Draw once after a
      // state/size change instead of keeping the GPU awake at four idle FPS.
      if(liveGameplay || staticCanvasDirty){
        renderer.render(scene,camera);
        if(liveGameplay&&ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES)renderExactModelOutlines(t / 1000);
        staticCanvasDirty=false;
      }
    }
    loadingUI?.set(86, 'PWEPARING INSTANT AUDIO...', 'Decoding embedded gun and reload buffers before play.');
    try { await preloadEmbeddedSfx(); } catch (error) { safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Embedded audio preload fallback:',error); }
    loadingUI?.set(90, 'LOADING SFX...', 'Caching actual firearm shots, magazines, shells, pumps, and footsteps.');
    try {
      const recordedWarmup=preloadRecordedSfx();
      const actionWarmup=preloadActionSfx();
      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled([recordedWarmup,actionWarmup]),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,2200))]);
    } catch (error) { safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Recorded SFX background fallback:',error); }
    installInterfaceSfx();
    loadingUI?.set(94, 'WAWMING THE GPU...', 'Compiling the shaders used by the map and animated cats.');
    try {
      if (renderer.compileAsync) {
        await Promise.race([renderer.compileAsync(scene,camera),new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,4500))]);
      } else renderer.compile(scene,camera);
    } catch (error) { safeConsoleWarn('[CatStrike] Shader warmup fallback:',error); }
    loadingUI?.set(96, 'PREWARMING DEATH VIEW...', 'Uploading map batches before the first third-person death camera.');
    warmDeathRenderPath();
    await new Promise(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(resolve)));
    window.CatStrikeAudioDebug={
      shotProfiles:WEAPON_SHOT_PROFILES,
      getMapRenderStats:()=>window.CatStrikeMapRenderStats||null,
      getState:()=>({recorded:[...recordedSfxBuffers.keys()],actions:[...actionSfxBuffers.keys()],local:[...localSfxBuffers.keys()],embedded:[...embeddedSfxBuffers.keys()],reloadSources:activeReloadAudioSources.size,activeGameplaySources:activeGameplaySfxSources.size,activeShotSources:activeShotSources.size,weaponSourceStarts:debugWeaponSourceStarts,shotStartsByWeapon:Object.fromEntries(debugShotStartsByWeapon),lastShotDurations:Object.fromEntries(debugLastShotDurations),eventCounts:Object.fromEntries(soundEventCounts),eventFailures:Object.fromEntries(soundEventFailures),selectedFaction,selectedTeam,playerTeam:player.team,playerFaction:TEAM_TO_FACTION[player.team]||'ffa',ctButtonActive:$('ctTeamBtn').classList.contains('active'),tButtonActive:$('tTeamBtn').classList.contains('active'),starterWeapon:currentWeapon.id,loadout:[...loadoutSlots],gunGameLevel:player.gunGameLevel,gunGameProgress:player.gunGameKillsOnLevel,gunGameLadder:[...GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS],gunGameCategories:GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.map(gunGameCategoryForId),awpGuaranteed:GUN_GAME_LADDER_IDS.includes('awp'),performancePressure,renderScale,modelOutlineScale,modelOutlineHz,mode:activeMode.key,activeCounts:{cats:activeBots().filter(b=>b.team==='cats').length,meowfia:activeBots().filter(b=>b.team==='meowfia').length},playerPos:{x:player.pos.x,y:player.pos.y,z:player.pos.z},playerDead:player.dead,respawnProtection:{active:respawnProtectionArmed&&performance.now()/1000<player.invulnerableUntil,remaining:Math.max(0,player.invulnerableUntil-performance.now()/1000)},playerRagdollVisible:playerRagdoll.visible,ragdollBots:configuredActiveBots.filter(b=>b.dead&&b.ragdollActive&&b.root.visible).length,playerRole:player.team==='cats'?'defuse':'plant',bombCarrier:bombState.carrier===player?'player':bombState.carrier?.name||null,bombPlanted:bombState.planted}),
      testAction:key=>playActionSfx(key,{volumeScale:.01}),
      localActionKeys:()=>[...localSfxBuffers.keys()],
      resetEventAudit:()=>{soundEventCounts.clear();soundEventFailures.clear();},
      testAllLocalActions:()=>[...localSfxBuffers.keys()].map(key=>[key,Boolean(playActionSfx(key,{volumeScale:.001,duration:.025}))]),
      selectTeam:factionOrTeam=>{updateTeamSelection(factionOrTeam);return {selectedFaction,selectedTeam};},
      selectMode:key=>{const button=document.querySelector(`.modeChoice[data-mode="${key}"]`);if(!button)return false;updateModeSelection(button);return true;},
      startSelected:()=>{startGame(true,null);return true;},
      resetShotCounter:()=>{debugWeaponSourceStarts=0;debugShotStartsByWeapon.clear();debugLastShotDurations.clear();},
      testShot:id=>{const entry=weaponById.get(id);return !!entry&&shotSound(entry.weapon,id==='awp'?.16:.01,0,false);},
      testAwpBoom:()=>playAwpBoomSound(.18,0,false),
      testPostShot:id=>{const entry=weaponById.get(id);if(!entry)return false;playPostShotMechanic(entry.weapon,.01,0);playCasingMechanic(entry.weapon,.01,0,null);return true;},
      testReload:id=>{const entry=weaponById.get(id);if(!entry)return false;beginReloadAudio(entry.weapon,.65);setTimeout(stopReloadAudio,120);return true;},
      testReloadAtAmmo:(id,ammo,duration=.8)=>{const entry=weaponById.get(id);if(!entry)return false;beginReloadAudio({...entry.weapon,ammo:Math.max(0,Math.min(entry.weapon.magSize,Number(ammo)||0))},duration);return true;},
      stopReloadAudio
    };
    window.CatStrikeReady = true;
    const playButton = $('playBtn');
    const deployButtonReady = $('deployBtn');
    playButton.disabled = false;
    const playLabel = playButton.querySelector('span');
    if (playLabel) playLabel.textContent = 'PWAY'; else playButton.textContent = 'PWAY';
    deployButtonReady.disabled = false;
    updateTeamSelection(selectedFaction);
    loadingUI?.ready();

    // R173: Keep full requestAnimationFrame cadence only while gameplay is live.
    // Menus, pause, and result screens are DOM over a static WebGL frame; waking at
    // 60-144 Hz there burned CPU/battery without changing a pixel. A 200 ms idle
    // heartbeat preserves pointer-lock recovery, while state changes wake instantly.
    let animationFrameHandle=0,idleAnimationTimer=0;
    function scheduleAnimationFrame(){
      if(animationFrameHandle||idleAnimationTimer)return;
      if(started&&!paused&&!gameOver||staticCanvasDirty){
        animationFrameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(animationTick);
      }else{
        idleAnimationTimer=setTimeout(()=>{idleAnimationTimer=0;animationFrameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(animationTick);},200);
      }
    }
    function animationTick(t){
      animationFrameHandle=0;animate(t);scheduleAnimationFrame();
    }
    function wakeAnimationLoop(){
      if(idleAnimationTimer){clearTimeout(idleAnimationTimer);idleAnimationTimer=0;}
      if(!animationFrameHandle)animationFrameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(animationTick);
    }
    wakeAnimationLoop();

    // Resize, orientationchange and VisualViewport resize commonly fire as a burst
    // for one physical change. Coalesce them into one framebuffer reallocation and
    // skip exact duplicates. Disabled outline targets are never resized.
    let resizeFramePending=false,lastRendererWidth=innerWidth,lastRendererHeight=innerHeight,lastRendererDpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
    function performRendererResize(){
      resizeFramePending=false;
      const width=innerWidth,height=innerHeight,dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
      if(width===lastRendererWidth&&height===lastRendererHeight&&dpr===lastRendererDpr)return;
      lastRendererWidth=width;lastRendererHeight=height;lastRendererDpr=dpr;
      pointerCoordScaleX=2/Math.max(1,width);pointerCoordScaleY=2/Math.max(1,height);if(stickPointer!==null)refreshStickGeometry();
      camera.aspect=width/height;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      renderer.setSize(width,height);applyRenderScale();
      if(ENABLE_MODEL_OUTLINES)resizeExactModelOutline();
      staticCanvasDirty=true;wakeAnimationLoop();
    }
    function resizeRenderer(){
      if(resizeFramePending)return;resizeFramePending=true;requestAnimationFrame(performRendererResize);
    }
    addEventListener('resize',resizeRenderer,{passive:true});
    addEventListener('orientationchange',()=>setTimeout(resizeRenderer,120),{passive:true});
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize',resizeRenderer,{passive:true});

    // Mobile gameplay controls suppress gestures in their own pointer handlers.
    // Do not cancel touchstart on menu/shop buttons: doing that suppresses click
    // on iOS and was the reason touch users could fail to select modes or deploy.
    document.querySelectorAll('#mobileUI button').forEach(button=>button.style.touchAction='none');
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
      if(document.hidden&&usingMobileControls()&&started&&!gameOver&&!paused)setPause(true);
    });
  </script>
